### [1] [Active Learning for Communication Structure Optimization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05703)
*Huchen Yang,Xinghao Dong,Dan Negrut,Jin-Long Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型多智能体系统的通信结构优化，现有方法在有限训练预算下因随机采样训练任务导致不稳定问题。本文提出基于集成信息论的任务选择框架，通过集成卡尔曼反演高效估计候选任务对图参数分布的影响，并引入嵌入代表选择和批量汤普森采样提升可扩展性，在良性设置和攻击设置下均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-MAS通信结构优化方法依赖随机采样训练任务，但任务在难度和领域上差异显著，信息量不均等，导致有限预算下优化不稳定且对训练集高度敏感。需要主动识别最有价值的任务来提升优化效率和稳定性。

Method: 提出基于集成信息论的任务选择框架：(1) 用集成卡尔曼反演作为无导数的贝叶斯更新近似，高效估计候选任务对图参数分布的影响；(2) 通过嵌入代表选择构建紧凑候选池；(3) 结合替代建模和批量汤普森采样进行信息性任务选择。

Result: 在良性设置和存在智能体攻击的设置下均验证了有效性，表明该方法在有限计算预算下能有效优化通信结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动任务选择框架能显著提升LLM-MAS通信结构优化的稳定性和效率，尤其适合黑盒、带噪声的多智能体系统场景。

Abstract: Optimizing the communication structure of large language model based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) has been shown to improve downstream performance and reduce token usage. Existing methods typically rely on randomly sampled training tasks. However, tasks may differ substantially in difficulty and domain, and thus they are not equally informative for updating communication structure, making optimization under limited training budgets often unstable and highly sensitive to the particular training set. To actively identify the most valuable tasks for communication-structure optimization, we propose an ensemble-based information-theoretic task selection framework. The proposed method estimates task informativeness by how much a candidate task changes the distribution over graph parameters, using ensemble Kalman inversion as an efficient and derivative-free approximation of the corresponding Bayesian update. The resulting estimator is especially suitable for black-box and noisy multi-agent systems. To enhance scalability, we construct a compact candidate pool through embedding-based representative selection and combine the informative selection with surrogate modeling and batch Thompson sampling. We validate our method in both benign settings and settings with agent attacks, demonstrating its effectiveness for communication-structure optimization under constrained computational budgets.

</details>


### [2] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于外部测量的封闭经验循环的自动化研究系统。该系统通过智能体提交包含假设、代码编辑、评估结果和反馈的试验，生成可审计的轨迹而非单一论文或模型。在1197次试验中无需人工干预，结果在三个基准任务上分别实现了0.81%、38.7%和4.59%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种完全自动化的研究循环，使智能体能够通过外部测量反馈自主提出假设、编辑代码、运行实验并迭代改进，无需人工选择提案、编辑代码或修复失败的试验。

Method: 构建封闭经验循环：每个试验包含假设、可执行代码编辑、评估者持有的结果和反馈；使用专业智能体划分配方面并共享谱系信息；通过谱系反馈将评估结果（包括崩溃、超预算等）转化为后续的程序级编辑。

Result: 在1197次试验中无需人工干预。三个主要运行结果：Parameter Golf验证bpb降低0.81%，NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%，CIFAR-10 Airbench96墙钟时间减少4.59%。系统还包含严格架构域审计和程序重写。

Conclusion: 该自动化循环能够自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、应用和组合已知技术，并在各自环境内改进公开的起始配方，生成可审计的完整研究轨迹。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [3] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于凸优化的卫星集群孔径评估框架，利用磁相互作用维持编队。通过解决非凸功耗约束问题，研究发现增加卫星数量可提高编队保持的功率效率，表明磁驱动集群架构是构建大规模空间系统的节能方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星集群构成的分布式太空孔径具有高增益、窄波束、大孔径等优势，但长期编队维护面临不稳定轨道动力学和功耗约束非线性的挑战，亟需有效的系统级配置分析方法。

Method: 将电磁力和力矩模型的非线性功耗约束转化为凸优化问题，构建基于凸优化的评估框架，分析大规模磁驱动卫星集群的功耗特性。

Result: 增加卫星数量能够提高编队保持的功率效率；磁驱动集群架构比传统的少数卫星电磁编队飞行方案更具功率优势。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星集群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种功率有效的替代方案，克服了传统电磁编队飞行在规模扩展上的局限性。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [4] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LATTE提出一种受分布式系统启发的LLM团队协调框架，通过共享演化协调图动态分配任务、减少通信开销和冲突，在多个任务上匹配或超越现有方法的同时显著降低token消耗、时间和协调失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM团队协调方法处于两个极端：高度结构化方法（如固定角色、流水线、先验任务分解）缺乏灵活性；完全非结构化团队虽具备适应性和探索能力，但存在错误传播、智能体间冲突和资源浪费（时间、token、文件操作）等低效问题。亟需一种兼顾结构化与灵活性的协调方案。

Method: LATTE框架受分布式系统思想启发，让LLM团队中的多个智能体协作构建并维护一个共享的、动态演化的协调图（coordination graph），该图编码了子任务依赖关系、个体智能体分配以及子任务当前进度。该协议在维护一致性的同时，赋予智能体动态分配工作、调整协调策略和发现新任务的能力。

Result: 在多种协作任务和基础模型上，LATTE在匹配或超越MetaGPT、去中心化团队、自上而下Leader-Worker层级和静态分解等标准设计的准确率的同时，显著降低了token使用量、墙钟时间、通信量和协调失败（如文件冲突和冗余输出）。

Conclusion: LATTE通过动态协调图在结构化和非结构化方法之间取得了有效平衡，为LLM团队协作提供了一种高效、可扩展且健壮的协调机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [5] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协调感知（coordination-aware）的评估视角，用于补充合作式多智能体强化学习（MARL）中仅依赖回报（return）的基准测试方法。通过构建一个名为STAT的受控约束空间任务分配测试平台，作者评估了六种代表性基于价值的MARL方法，发现相似的回报趋势可能掩盖截然不同的协调机制（如冗余分配、分配多样性、任务完成效率），从而论证了过程级诊断指标对于理解多智能体协作行为的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL基准测试过度聚焦于回报、成功率等聚合指标，无法揭示智能体间协调的具体机制。尤其在组合规模随智能体、任务和联合分配选择爆炸性增长的任务中，仅凭回报指标无法区分不同的协调行为模式，因此需要引入协调感知的过程级诊断方法来补充评估体系。

Method: 作者首先设计了一个受约束的承诺约束空间任务分配测试平台STAT，在固定观测访问和任务规则的前提下，系统性地变化智能体数量、任务数量和环境规模。然后选取六种代表性的基于价值的MARL方法（涵盖不同集中化程度），在STAT上进行评估，并引入冗余分配（redundant assignment）、分配多样性（assignment diversity）和任务完成效率（task-completion efficiency）等过程级诊断指标来补充回报指标。

Result: 实验结果显示，不同方法在回报趋势上可能相似，但其协调机制存在显著差异，包括冗余分配模式、分配多样性程度和任务完成效率的不同。此外，在承诺约束的任务分配中，性能受多种因素影响：名义动作空间大小、分配压力（assignment pressure）、稀疏决策机会（sparse decision opportunities）以及相互依赖智能体之间的冗余选择（redundant choices）。

Conclusion: 本文结论指出，仅依赖回报指标的基准测试无法充分揭示多智能体协调的本质，协调感知的评估（包括过程级诊断指标）应作为合作式MARL评估的必要补充，以更全面地理解智能体在不同场景下的协作行为。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>

### [6] [A diagrammatic proof-theoretic semantics for the Greimas semiotic square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05273)
*Michael Fowler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文基于蜘蛛图形式化方法，为格雷马斯的符号学方阵建立了一种图解证明系统。核心结果是四项元术语可通过固定推导模式从基本配置的合取对统一导出，并论证了图解否定是一种区域决定的语义对立而非布尔补余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的符号学方阵多为静态结构描述，缺乏形式化的推演机制。作者希望为格雷马斯的结构语义学提供一个基于图解推理的证明论框架，使元术语的生成具有构造性和规则性。

Method: 以蜘蛛图为底层形式化工具，构建基本术语的图解配置；定义矛盾关系与蕴含关系为受推理规则支配的变换；引入轮廓引入和生境变换规则，设计统一的推导模式来生成四项元术语。

Result: 四项元术语均可通过固定的推导图式从基本配置的合取对中导出；图解否定被刻画为区域决定的语义对立，而非布尔补余；为符号学中的"+"运算提供了推导构造式的语义解释。

Conclusion: 该框架扩展了蜘蛛图演算的表达范围，展示了图解推理可用于在统一推演框架内形式化非经典语义操作，为结构语义学提供了组合性的、基于规则的语义机制。

Abstract: We develop a diagrammatic proof system for a fragment of structural semantics inspired by the Greimas semiotic square, using spider diagrams as the underlying formalism. The basic terms are represented as diagrammatic configurations, and the relations of contradiction and implication are interpreted as transformations governed by a set of inference rules. These transformations are realised as derivations, with proof trees serving as witnesses. Our main result shows that the construction of the four meta-terms can be captured uniformly: each is derivable from a conjunctive pair of basic configurations via a fixed derivation schema composed of contour introduction and habitat transformation rules. This yields a proof-theoretic account of the combinatorial operation underlying meta-term formation, and provides a semantic interpretation of the Greimasian operation `+' as a derivational construction rather than a logical combination. We further show that diagrammatic negation in this setting is not a Boolean complement, but a restricted, zone-determined semantic counter-position, reflecting the relational character of opposition in structural semiotics. The resulting framework provides a compositional, rule-based semantics in which complex configurations are generated constructively from simpler ones. In addition to extending the expressive scope of spider diagram calculi, the system illustrates how diagrammatic reasoning can be used to formalise non-classical semantic operations within a unified inferential setting.

</details>


### [7] [Paraconsistent Semantics for Extended Fuzzy Logic Programs via Approximation Fixpoint Theory [Extended Version]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05286)
*Pascal Kettmann,Hannes Strass,Jesse Heyninck,Jeroen Spaans*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文基于近似不动点理论，为同时包含失败否定和强否定的模糊逻辑程序定义了良好行为的语义，该框架泛化了多种现有语义并衍生出新语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为同时包含失败否定和强否定的模糊逻辑程序定义语义是一项挑战，现有方法难以很好地结合这两种否定与模糊扩展，需要统一的理论框架。

Method: 利用近似不动点理论（approximating fixpoint theory）构建框架，形式化地定义同时包含"by-failure"否定和强否定的模糊逻辑程序的语义。

Result: 该框架能够泛化多种现有语义，并衍生出一系列新的语义。

Conclusion: 近似不动点理论为处理含两种否定的模糊逻辑程序提供了统一的语义基础，具有广泛的适用性和扩展能力。

Abstract: In logic programming, negation can be interpreted in various ways. Probably best known is the concept of "negation as failure", where "$\mathit{not}\, p$" is true if we have no evidence for $p$. On the other hand, strong negation requires that we have evidence for $p$ being false. Defining semantics for logic programs containing both kinds of negation is a challenging task, and this becomes even more challenging when combining this with other extensions of logic programming, e.g. fuzziness. In this work, we use the framework of approximating fixpoint theory to formulate well-behaved semantics for fuzzy logic programs containing both "by-failure" and strong negation. We show that this framework generalizes several existing semantics as well as giving rise to a host of new semantics.

</details>


### [8] [A Practical Specification Language for Automatic Quantum Program Verification (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05786)
*Wei-Lun Tsai,Yu-Fang Chen,Ondřej Lengál*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了一种扩展的基于集合的量子程序规约语言及其到自动机的线性翻译算法，实现了固定量子比特量子程序的完全自动Hoare风格验证，显著提升了可扩展性和表达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hoare风格的量子程序验证方法无法实现全自动验证；基于自动机的验证虽然扩展性好，但将高级的集合式断言翻译为自动机时存在指数爆炸问题，严重限制了实用性。

Method: 提出了一种扩展的基于集合的规约语言，并设计了一种规约到自动机的翻译算法，通过受控自动机构造和量子比特重排序，使得翻译复杂度与量子比特数呈线性关系。

Result: 生成的紧凑自动机使得固定量子比特量子程序的完全自动Hoare风格验证达到了此前不可行的规模，同时显著提升了表达力且没有牺牲效率。

Conclusion: 通过线性复杂度的规约-自动机翻译算法，解决了自动机验证框架中的指数爆炸瓶颈，实现了高效且表达力更强的全自动量子程序验证。

Abstract: Hoare-style verification provides a principled foundation for reasoning about the correctness of quantum programs, but existing approaches do not allow fully automatic verification. While automata-based verification scales well when specifications are given directly as automata, prior frameworks incur exponential blow-up when translating high-level set-based assertions into automata, which severely limits practicality. We introduce an extended set-based specification language and a specification-to-automata translation algorithm whose complexity is linear in the number of qubits, enabled by controlled automaton construction and qubit reordering. The resulting compact automata enable fully automatic Hoare-style verification of fixed-qubit quantum programs at previously infeasible scales, while substantially improving expressiveness without compromising efficiency.

</details>


### [9] [Self-Correcting Gossip Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05801)
*Giorgio Cignarale,Hans van Ditmarsch,Stephan Felber,Malvin Gattinger,Hugo Rincon Galeana,Vaishnavi Sundararajan*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出采用动态认知逻辑来研究存在错误的自纠正八卦协议，展示了如何在无中心协调的情况下纠正传输错误，并分析了其对最优性的影响以及与有界记忆和全信息协议的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多使用时间认知逻辑来处理分布式计算中的错误协议，作者希望引入动态认知逻辑这一新的形式化工具来更好地建模和分析自纠正八卦协议中的错误处理机制。

Method: 采用动态认知逻辑（Dynamic Epistemic Logic）作为形式化框架，研究存在传输错误的自纠正八卦协议，分析在无中心协调者情况下纠正错误消息的机制，并与有界记忆协议和全信息协议进行对比。

Result: 展示了如何在无中央权威协调的情况下纠正因错误消息导致的传输错误，分析了自纠正机制对协议最优性的影响，并与有界记忆和全信息协议进行了比较。

Conclusion: 动态认知逻辑为研究存在错误的自纠正八卦协议提供了有效的形式化工具，在无中心协调的情况下能够实现错误纠正，但会对协议的最优性产生影响，与有界记忆和全信息协议相比各有优劣。

Abstract: We investigate self-correcting gossip protocols with errors. In distributed computing, protocols with errors have been widely investigated in temporal epistemic logics. Instead, we propose a dynamic epistemic logic. We show how to correct transmission errors due to faulty messages without a central authority coordinating protocol execution, how this affects optimality, and how this compares to bounded memory and full information protocols.

</details>


### [10] [Decidability Results for Fragments of First-Order Logic via a Symbolic Model Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05840)
*Neta Elad,Sharon Shoham*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出将符号结构推广到任意具有标准模型的基础理论，并利用符号模型性质证明多个扩展分层公式片段的可判定性，通过通用构造方法从任意模型构建符号模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号结构最初使用线性整数算术项和公式来有限表示潜在的无限一阶结构，作者希望将其推广到任意具有标准模型的基础理论，并利用符号模型性质证明更多逻辑片段的可判定性。

Method: (1) 将符号结构从线性整数算术推广到任意具有标准模型的基础理论；(2) 利用符号模型性质（可满足公式存在符号模型）推导可判定性；(3) 针对扩展分层公式的多个片段（允许自循环函数但受特定限制），从任意模型出发构造符号模型，并以通用方式证明构造的正确性。

Result: 证明了多个扩展分层公式片段（在量化交替约束上放宽限制，允许某种具有自循环函数）的可判定性，并提供了一个可实例化到其他类似受限片段的通用构造框架。

Conclusion: 符号结构可推广至任意具有标准模型的基础理论，符号模型性质为证明逻辑片段可判定性提供了通用工具，且构造方法具有泛化性，可应用于其他受限片段。

Abstract: Recently, symbolic structures were proposed as finite representations of potentially infinite first-order structures, where Linear Integer Arithmetic terms and formulas define the domain and interpretations of a structure. We generalize symbolic structures to use any base theory that admits a standard model. Symbolic structures induce a symbolic model property, which holds for a fragment of first-order logic if every satisfiable formula in the fragment has a symbolic model. The symbolic model property implies decidability, since the model-checking problem for symbolic structures is decidable. We use the symbolic model property to prove decidability for several fragments that extend the fragment of stratified formulas, relaxing the quantifier-alternation constraints by allowing one sort to have self-looping functions, under certain restrictions. To establish the symbolic model property for these fragments we construct a symbolic model for a formula from an arbitrary model. The construction and its correctness are proved in a generic fashion, which may be instantiated to other similarly restricted fragments.

</details>


### [11] [Graded Monad Coalgebras for Continuous-Time Transition Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06268)
*Elena Di Lavore,Jonas Forster,Mario Román*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出用分级余代数（graded coalgebras of graded monads）来建模连续时间迁移系统，发展了分级余代数的理论（包括分级分配律和终端余代数存在条件），定义了分支时间语义和迹语义，并与Feller-Dynkin过程建立联系，最后发展了对应的余代数模态逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统函子余代数只能描述离散步骤演化的迁移系统，而现实中的许多系统（如连续时间系统）需要连续时间建模。本文旨在将余代数理论扩展到连续时间领域。

Method: 引入分级余代数（graded coalgebras of graded monads）作为连续时间迁移系统的模型；发展分级余代数的理论框架，包括分级分配律（graded distributive laws）；给出终端余代数存在的条件；定义分支时间语义和迹语义；建立与Feller-Dynkin过程的联系；开发对应的余代数模态逻辑并建立不变性和表达性判据。

Result: 成功构建了分级余代数理论，给出了终端余代数存在条件，定义了两种语义（分支时间和迹语义），建立了与Feller-Dynkin过程的联系，并开发了对应的模态逻辑系统。

Conclusion: 分级余代数为连续时间迁移系统提供了统一的理论框架，拓展了经典余代数理论的应用范围，并为连续时间系统的语义和逻辑推理奠定了基础。

Abstract: Functor coalgebras capture a wide range of transition systems that must however evolve in discrete steps. We introduce graded coalgebras of graded monads and propose them to model continuous-time transition systems. We develop the theory of graded coalgebras-including graded distributive laws between graded monads-and we give conditions for the existence of terminal coalgebras. We define both branching-time and trace semantics, linking them to recent work on Feller-Dynkin processes. Finally, we develop coalgebraic modal logics for both process semantics and state criteria for invariance and expressivity.

</details>


### [12] [Relational Dualities and Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06533)
*Piotr Kozicki,Alex Kavvos*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过构建Tarski对偶和Thomason对偶的关系扩展，将关系框架（如计算系统间的双模拟）与谓词间的关系对应起来，并由此衍生出跨系统的推理证明系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范畴对偶性将关系框架（计算系统状态）与逻辑谓词代数对应，但现有工作仅处理系统与谓词之间的对应，尚未将系统间的关系（如双模拟）与谓词间的关系建立对应。

Method: 构造Tarski对偶（用于无穷经典命题逻辑）和Thomason对偶（用于无穷经典模态逻辑）的关系扩展版本，将系统间的良基关系提升至谓词代数之间的对应关系。

Result: 成功建立了系统间关系（如双模拟）与谓词间关系的形式化对应，并由此衍生出一个跨系统的推理证明系统。

Conclusion: 关系化对偶性为连接计算系统的行为等价关系与逻辑推理提供了统一的范畴框架，可支撑不同系统间的形式化推理。

Abstract: The Kripke semantics of various logics arises via categorical dualities between a category of relational frames and their maps, and a category of algebras and logical homomorphisms. When the relational frames are considered as computational systems (e.g. the states of a machine), the corresponding algebra is one of logical predicates on these systems (e.g. predicates on these states, i.e. program logics). Our aim is to extend this phenomenon to relations, putting well-behaved relations between systems (e.g. bisimulations) in correspondence with relations between predicates. This is achieved by constructing particular relational extensions of Tarski duality (for infinitary classical propositional logic) and Thomason duality (for infinitary classical modal logic). We sketch how these dualities give rise to a proof system that relates formulae between different systems.

</details>

### [13] [Anatomy of a Query: W5H Dimensions and FAR Patterns for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05525)
*Vicki Stover Hertzberg,Eduardo Valverde,Joyce C. Ho*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出QUEST框架，包含FAR结构不变性（所有查询归结为过滤、聚合、返回）和W5H语义维度（六维过滤）。在12万+文本转SQL数据上验证，FAR普适成立，W5H分布差异显著：医疗查询高度集中于时间（80.4%）和人物（73.0%），因果和机制推理近乎缺失，揭示了机器推理需要跨越的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据库接口日益流行，但评估与设计这些系统的理论基础尚不完善，缺乏系统性的分析框架。

Method: 提出QUEST框架，包含两个独立组件：FAR结构不变性（任何良构查询均可归约为Filter、Aggregate、Return操作）和W5H语义维度框架（所有过滤条件映射至Who/What/Where/When/Why/How六个语义维度）。在五个文本转SQL数据集（n=120,464）上验证。

Result: FAR一致性在所有领域和模式类型中普遍成立；W5H维度分布差异显著：医疗查询集中于WHEN（80.4%）和WHO（73.0%），远高于通用领域基准；因果（WHY）和机制（HOW）推理接近为零，表面上的HOW例外实际反映的是量化聚合而非真正的程序性推理。

Conclusion: 这些结果识别出机器在结构化数据上进行真正推理必须跨越的前沿边界。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces to databases have gained popularity, yet the theoretical foundations for evaluating and designing these systems remain underdeveloped. We present QUEST (Query Understanding Evaluation through Semantic Translation), a framework resting on two independently motivated components: the FAR structural invariant, which holds that every well-formed query reduces to Filter, Aggregate, and Return operations; and the W5H dimensional framework, which holds that all filtering criteria map to six semantic dimensions (Who, What, Where, When, Why, and How). Validated across five text-to-SQL datasets (n = 120,464), FAR conformance is universal across all domains and schema types, while W5H dimensional profiles vary substantially. Healthcare queries are strongly concentrated in temporal (WHEN: 80.4%) and person-centric (WHO: 73.0%) dimensions far exceeding general-domain benchmarks, and causal (WHY) and mechanistic (HOW) reasoning are near-zero everywhere, with apparent HOW exceptions reflecting quantitative aggregation rather than genuine procedural reasoning. These results identify a frontier that must be crossed for genuine machine reasoning over structured data.

</details>


### [14] [An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)
*Sicheng Pan,Shuxian Wang,Wesley Zheng,Zirong Zeng,Vijay Sharma,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Rulescript是一种引擎无关的DSL，用于开发查询重写规则，支持“一次编写，到处部署”范式，通过在CockroachDB和Apache Data Fusion上部署33条来自Apache Calcite的重写规则验证了其可移植性和低集成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询重写规则通常手动实现、紧耦合于特定执行引擎，且缺乏形式化正确性保证，导致开发新引擎时需要重新实现大量规则，工程成本高、可移植性差且易引入新bug。

Method: 设计Rulescript DSL，基于关系代数核心语言将规则定义为匹配和变换两个阶段，支持自定义算子扩展引擎特定语义，通过轻量适配器将核心/自定义算子映射到目标引擎实现，且所有重写自动进行形式化验证。

Result: 重新实现了33条Apache Calcite重写规则，扩展了若干自定义算子，并自动将这些规则部署到CockroachDB和Apache Data Fusion两个后端差异显著的引擎上。

Conclusion: Rulescript实现了查询计划重写的“一次编写，到处部署”，以极低的工作量将已有规则部署到新数据引擎上。

Abstract: Logical query plan rewriting transforms a relational database query into an equivalent but more efficient form and is crucial to the performance of database-backed applications. In existing systems, rewrite rules are typically implemented manually, tightly coupled to specific execution engines, and often lack formal correctness guarantees. Consequently, developing a new engine requires reimplementing both legacy and new rules, incurring significant engineering cost, limiting portability, and every new implementation is an opportunity for introducing new bugs.
  We introduce Rulescript, an engine-agnostic domain-specific language (DSL) for developing query rewrite rules. Rulescript separates rule definition from execution infrastructure via a relational algebra-inspired core language and an explicit decomposition of rules into matching and transformation phases. Developers express rewrites by pattern-matching query plans using Rulescript's core operators and constructing semantically equivalent transformed plans, with all rewrites automatically verified formally to ensure correctness. Rulescript is extensible: users can define custom operators in terms of the core language to capture engine-specific semantics. To integrate with an existing system, developers need only implement a lightweight adapter that maps Rulescript's core and custom operators to the operators implemented in the target engine.
  We evaluate Rulescript by reimplementing 33 rewrite rules from Apache Calcite and extending the language with several custom operators. To demonstrate portability, we automatically deploy these rules to CockroachDB and Apache Data Fusion, two engines with substantially different backends. Our results show that Rulescript enables "write once, deploy everywhere" paradigm for query plan rewriting, with minimal effort required to deploy previously written rules on a new data engine.

</details>


### [15] [Low-Latency Out-of-Core ANN Search in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05787)
*Ziwen Song,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang,Junhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SkipDisk是一种磁盘-内存混合的近似最近邻搜索方法，通过紧下界过滤、三级数据剪枝和异步I/O策略，以约10%的内存占用达到HNSW 85%的延迟性能，或以约20%的内存占用达到HNSW 63%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内存图式ANN搜索性能优秀但内存占用大，磁盘方法虽减少内存但磁盘访问延迟高。现有基于下界的过滤方法下界过于松散，无法有效减少磁盘访问。目标是实现显著降低内存占用的同时，达到与内存方法相当甚至更低的搜索延迟。

Method: 1. 为每个点设计专用枢轴（pivot），提升三角不等式下界紧致性，实现高效过滤；基于该下界设计估计方法。2. 采用三级数据剪枝策略，将信息量大的数据保留在内存中。3. 通过在内存中存储邻居节点，解耦内存搜索与磁盘访问，设计异步I/O策略。

Result: 以约10%的内存占用达到HNSW 85%的延迟性能；以约20%的内存占用达到HNSW 63%的延迟性能。

Conclusion: SkipDisk通过紧下界过滤、数据剪枝和异步I/O设计，成功在内存占用与搜索延迟之间取得了优异的平衡，显著优于现有磁盘方法。

Abstract: In-memory graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search has superior search performance but incurs significant memory footprint. Disk-based methods reduce memory usage but suffer from high disk access latency. A common challenge is how to achieve low-latency search while significantly reducing memory footprint. In this paper, we propose SkipDisk, a disk-memory hybrid ANN search that significantly reduces memory footprint while achieving search latency comparable to or lower than in-memory method HNSW. By analyzing existing disk-based methods, we observed that disk access remains the primary bottleneck, and existing lower bound based filtering methods are two loose to effectively reduce disk access. Therefore, we design SkipDisk to achieve tight lower bound with low memory footprint to reduce the search latency. First, we design a dedicated pivot for each point to improve the lower bound of the triangle inequality for effective filtering. We further design an estimation-based approach based on this lower bound. Second, to reduce the memory footprint, we employ a three-level data pruning strategy to preserve informative data in memory. Third, to further reduce search latency, we design an asynchronous I/O strategy based on the decoupling of in-memory search and disk access by storing neighbor nodes in memory. Experiments show that our method achieves a latency of 85 of HNSW's latency with approximately 10 memory footprint, and a latency to 63 of HNSW's with a slightly higher memory footprint of around 20.

</details>

### [16] [Monitoring autonomous persistent surveillance missions using invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06062)
*Vladislav Nenchev,Prodromos Sotiriadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对黑箱自主导航机器人持续监视任务，提出一种基于离线计算不变量的运行时监控方法。面对大型环境难以计算全局不变量的问题，通过去中心化计算各区域的低维不变量，并在在线阶段检查其合取条件，从而构建组合式监控器。在共同独立假设下，该组合式监控器相对于全系统不变量是完备且可靠的。通过真实机器人在迷宫中的持续监视实验验证了方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在黑箱自主栈（即底层控制算法不可知）条件下执行持续监视任务时，需要一个运行时监控机制来确保系统行为符合预期。由于被监视空间大时，全局不变量的离线计算难以实现，因此需要分解式、可扩展的监控方法。

Method: 将环境划分为有限区域，每个区域关联一个不确定性状态（观察到时降低，否则增加）；将闭环系统建模为具有线性参数变化动力学（LPV）的状态相关混合系统；离线计算不变量；针对大型环境，提出组合式监控方法——对各区域分散计算低维不变量，在线检查所有区域的合取条件。在共同独立假设下证明了组合方法的可靠性与完备性。

Result: 在真实机器人持续监视迷宫的案例研究中，验证了所提组合式监控方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种面向黑箱自主导航的持续监视运行时监控框架，通过组合式不变量方法解决了大规模空间的监控可扩展性问题，并在实物实验中证明了其有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies runtime monitoring for persistent surveillance by autonomous robots when the autonomy stack is a black box. The environment is partitioned into finitely many parts, each carrying an uncertainty state that decreases when observed and increases otherwise. We model the closed loop as a state-dependent hybrid system with linear parameter varying dynamics and design a monitor based on an invariant computed offline. As this invariant is typically hard to obtain for large to-be-surveyed spaces, we propose a compositional monitor obtained by decentralized computation of low-dimensional invariant sets for each uncertainty region, and checking their conjunction online. Under common independence assumptions, the compositional monitor is sound and complete with respect to the full-system invariant. The approach is applied in a case study with a real robot persistently monitoring a labyrinth, emphasizing its applicability in practice.

</details>


### [17] [A GPU-Accelerated Hybrid Method for a Class of Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05208)
*Zhenyu Lei,Jin-Kao Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合算法，通过集成学习驱动的多样性控制交叉算子、多惩罚评估函数引导的可行-不可行搜索框架、专用局部搜索算子以及张量GPU加速策略，高效求解多车场车辆路径问题（MDVRP）及其变种，在大规模实例上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDVRP在多个实际场景中广泛存在，但由于其固有复杂性，计算上求解极为困难，现有方法在大规模问题上表现不足。

Method: 提出混合算法，核心组件包括：(1) 学习驱动的多样性控制路径交换交叉算子；(2) 多惩罚评估函数引导的多车场可行-不可行搜索框架；(3) 两个专门的车场相关局部搜索算子；(4) 基于张量的GPU加速与新颖的多移动更新策略以提升计算效率和可扩展性。

Result: 在三个MDVRP变种的基准实例上的大量计算实验表明，所提算法与最先进方法相比具有高度竞争力，尤其在大规模实例上表现突出。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合算法有效解决了MDVRP的计算挑战，在多种变种和大规模场景下均取得优异性能，验证了学习驱动搜索与GPU加速相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-depot vehicle routing problems (MDVRPs) are prevalent in a variety of practical applications. However, they are computationally challenging to solve due to their inherent complexity. This paper proposes an effective hybrid algorithm for a class of MDVRPs. The algorithm integrates a learning-driven, diversity-controlled route-exchange crossover and a multi-depot-supported feasible-and-infeasible search framework guided by a multi-penalty evaluation function. Two dedicated depot-related local search operators are incorporated to further strengthen the search capability in multi-depot settings. To improve computational efficiency and scalability, an enhanced version of the algorithm is developed that uses a tensor-based GPU acceleration combined with a novel multi-move update strategy. Extensive computational experiments on benchmark instances of three MDVRP variants show that the proposed algorithms are highly competitive with state-of-the-art methods, especially for large-scale instances.

</details>


### [18] [Lie Group Formulation of Recursive Dynamics Algorithms of Higher Order for Floating-Base Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06498)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了浮基树形机器人（基座位姿在SE(3)上演化，机构构型在T^{n1}×R^{n2}上）的李群牛顿-欧拉、聚合体惯量及混合动力学算法的高阶时间导数计算方法，推导了满足无源性条件的闭式运动方程，证明了聚合体惯量张量在任意阶导数下保持不变，并在12自由度空中机械臂上验证了高达五阶的动力学，展示了计算成本随导数阶数二次增长（而自动微分基线呈指数增长）的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮基机器人动力学算法缺乏对高阶时间导数的系统化计算方法，而高阶导数对于高精度控制、轨迹优化及灵敏度分析等应用至关重要。

Method: 采用空间旋量表示法，在SE(3)和T^{n1}×R^{n2}流形上推导了牛顿-欧拉、聚合体惯量及混合动力学的递归算法，并计算其各阶时间导数；从递归中提取闭式运动方程，识别满足无源性的科里奥利矩阵，证明聚合体惯量张量的导数不变性。

Result: 在12自由度空中机械臂上成功解析推导了几何正向与逆向动力学及其一阶导数，数值仿真验证了高达五阶的动力学的正确性；基准测试表明，所提方法的计算复杂度随导数阶数二次增长，而自动微分基线呈指数增长。

Conclusion: 所提算法为浮基树形机器人高阶动力学的解析计算提供了高效且可扩展的框架，在计算效率上显著优于自动微分方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we describe procedures for computing higher-order time derivatives of the Lie-group Newton-Euler, Articulated-Body Inertia, and hybrid dynamics algorithms for floating-base trees, where the base configuration evolves on SE(3) and the attached mechanism is an open kinematic tree with configuration on the (n1+n2)-dimensional manifold T^{n1} \times R^{n2}, using spatial representation of twists. After presenting the algorithms, we collect the resulting recursions into closed-form equations of motion, identifying an admissible Coriolis matrix satisfying the passivity property, and showing that the articulated inertia tensor remains unchanged across all time derivatives. We then apply the developed methods to a 12-DoF aerial manipulator to derive analytical expressions for its geometric forward and inverse dynamics along with their first time derivatives whereas the numerical simulations successfully evaluate these dynamics up to fifth order. Finally, to demonstrate their practical utility, we benchmark the proposed extensions and show that, in the considered tests, their computational cost scales quadratically with the derivative order, whereas the automatic-differentiation baseline exhibits exponential scaling.

</details>


### [19] [DexSim2Real: Foundation Model-Guided Sim-to-Real Transfer for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05241)
*Zijian Zeng,Fei Ding,Huiming Yang,Xianwei Li,Yuhao Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSim2Real是一个利用视觉-语言基础模型弥合灵巧操作模拟到现实迁移鸿沟的集成框架。它通过FM-DR（视觉真实性批评+CMA-ES优化）、TVCAP（跨注意力触觉-视觉融合策略）和PSC（基于LLM的渐进技能课程）三个组件，在六项灵巧操作任务上实现了78.2%的平均真实世界成功率，将模拟到现实的性能差距缩小至仅8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟到现实的迁移是灵巧操作策略从仿真部署到真实机器人的关键瓶颈。现有方法依赖手工设计的域随机化或特定任务适配，限制了它们在多样操作场景中的泛化能力。本文旨在利用视觉-语言基础模型来自动化地弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出DexSim2Real框架，包含三个组件：(1) FM-DR：使用视觉语言模型作为视觉真实性批评器，通过闭环CMA-ES优化仿真参数；(2) TVCAP：将跨注意力机制的视觉-触觉融合适配到零样本模拟到现实强化学习；(3) PSC：基于LLM的任务分解与难度调度器，为接触密集的灵巧任务设计渐进式技能课程。

Result: 在六个具有挑战性的灵巧操作任务上，DexSim2Real实现了78.2%的平均真实世界成功率，优于DrEureka和DeXtreme，并将模拟到现实的性能差距缩小至仅8.3%。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言基础模型能够有效驱动域随机化自动化与策略泛化，显著提升灵巧操作在模拟到现实迁移中的实用性。DexSim2Real为高维、接触密集的灵巧操作任务提供了一种可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer remains a critical bottleneck for deploying dexterous manipulation policies learned in simulation to real-world robots. Existing approaches rely on manually designed domain randomization or task-specific adaptation, limiting their generalizability across diverse manipulation scenarios. We present DexSim2Real, an integrated framework that leverages vision-language foundation models to bridge the sim-to-real gap for dexterous manipulation. Our system combines three components: (1) Foundation Model-Guided Domain Randomization (FM-DR), which uses a vision-language model as a visual realism critic to optimize simulation parameters via closed-loop CMA-ES, complementing text-based approaches like DrEureka with direct visual feedback; (2) a Tactile-Visual Cross-Attention Policy (TVCAP) that adapts cross-attention visuo-tactile fusion to zero-shot sim-to-real RL; and (3) a Progressive Skill Curriculum (PSC) that builds on LLM-based task decomposition with a difficulty scheduler tailored to contact-rich dexterous tasks. Extensive experiments on six challenging manipulation tasks with blinded evaluation demonstrate that DexSim2Real achieves a 78.2% average real-world success rate, outperforming DrEureka and DeXtreme while reducing the sim-to-real performance gap to only 8.3%.

</details>


### [20] [Track A*: Fast Visibility-Aware Trajectory Planning for Active Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05338)
*Hanxuan Chen,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TA*，一种基于离散四维时空网格(x,y,z,t)的离线搜索轨迹规划器，用于主动目标跟踪中的可见性感知参考轨迹生成。通过分层DAG搜索结合三种工程优化（BVH跨时间障碍距离缓存、每层波束剪枝、可配置多射线可见性评估器），在保持近基线可见性性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线参考轨迹对于构建多模态跟踪数据集和在可重复条件下基准测试在线跟踪规划器至关重要，但现有方法在计算效率和可扩展性方面存在不足。

Method: 将问题建模为四维时空网格上的离散搜索，采用分层有向无环图(DAG)搜索，结合三种优化：基于BVH的跨时间障碍距离缓存、每层波束剪枝、可配置多射线可见性评估器。使用波束剪枝启发式搜索在离散图中高效寻找高质量跟踪轨迹。

Result: 在1000场景压力测试中100%收敛，45秒完成（32 worker）；与BinaryHeap A*基线相比，平均规划时间降低23.0倍，最坏情况降低11.8倍，收敛率从56.9%提升至100%；在基线收敛子集上平均可见性仅下降0.15个百分点。

Conclusion: TA*可作为特定条件下的实用离线参考规划器，但在密集植被等环境中存在局限性和失败案例，需进一步讨论。

Abstract: Offline reference trajectories for active target tracking are needed both for building multi-modal tracking datasets and for benchmarking online tracking planners under repeatable conditions. We present Track A star (TA star), an offline search-based trajectory planner that targets the visibility-aware target tracking objective on a discretized four-dimensional spatio-temporal grid (x, y, z, t). TA star combines a layered Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) search with three engineering optimizations: cross-time obstacle distance caching against a Bounding Volume Hierarchy (BVH), per-layer beam pruning, and a configurable multi-ray visibility evaluator. TA star employs a beam-pruned heuristic search on this discrete graph to efficiently find high-quality tracking trajectories. While it trades strict theoretical optimality for practical scalability, our empirical results demonstrate robust, near-baseline visibility performance at a fraction of the computational cost. On a 1000-scenario stress test across eight CARLA Optimized maps, TA star converges on all scenarios and completes in 45 s using 32 workers; on a 248-scenario controlled comparison against an unoptimized priority-queue A star baseline (BinaryHeap implementation) under identical scenario inputs and a 5 x 10^6 expansion cap, TA star reduces mean planning time by 23.0x and worst-case planning time by 11.8x, while raising convergence from 56.9% to 100%. On the n=141 baseline-converged subset, TA star changes average visibility by only -0.15 percentage points (pp), with no scenario exceeding a 5 pp drop. We position TA star as a practical offline reference planner under these specific conditions, with limitations and failure cases discussed for environments such as Town07 dense vegetation.

</details>


### [21] [Passive Fault Tolerance through Tension-to-Thrust Feed-Forward: Hybrid Input-to-State Stability for Decentralized Multi-UAV Slung-Load Transport under Abrupt Cable Severance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05339)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Paul Schmitt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多无人机吊挂运输中的缆绳突然断裂问题，提出了一种被动式容错控制架构，通过将每架无人机测得的缆绳张力直接馈入高度推力指令，并结合级联控制器实现分散式恢复。理论贡献是条件混合实用输入-状态稳定性证书，在仿真中实现了0.312-0.328 m RMSE和快速恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机吊挂运输中缆绳突然断裂会重新分配负载并改变主动约束集，现有控制器依赖协调力分配、对等状态交换或固定缆绳拓扑，缺乏针对突发性断裂的认证分散式恢复机制。

Method: 提出被动式架构：将各无人机实测缆绳张力直接引入高度推力指令$T_i^{\mathrm{ff}}=T_i$，外围采用比例-微分、防摆和投影级联控制器保持局部跟踪可行性。主要贡献是条件混合实用输入-状态稳定性证书，组合了松弛偏移有界紧缆约简、有界断裂后Lyapunov跳跃、故障间衰减和每故障周期收缩$\rho\in (0,1)$，形成显式恢复包络。

Result: 在Drake多体仿真中，5架无人机、10 kg负载、Kelvin-Voigt缆绳、Dryden风况下单/双断裂场景下，闭环RMSE为0.312-0.328 m，峰值下垂76.1-95.2 mm，在一个摆锤周期内恢复。禁用张力前馈后巡航误差增加34-39%，峰值下垂增大3.6-4.0倍。

Conclusion: 局部张力前馈是所测试分散式级联控制中占主导地位的被动恢复机制。

Abstract: Abrupt cable severance in multi-UAV slung-load transport redistributes load and changes the active constraint set, leaving limited time for fault diagnosis and reconfiguration. Existing controllers rely on coordinated force allocation, peer-state exchange, or fixed cable topology, and therefore lack a certified decentralized recovery mechanism for unannounced severance. We present a passive architecture that routes each vehicle's measured cable tension directly into its altitude thrust command, $T_i^{\mathrm{ff}}=T_i$, while a surrounding proportional-derivative, anti-swing, and projection cascade preserves local tracking feasibility. The main contribution is a conditional hybrid practical input-to-state-stability certificate that composes a slack-excursion-bounded taut-cable reduction, bounded post-severance Lyapunov jumps, inter-fault decay, and per-fault-cycle contraction $ρ\in (0,1)$ into an explicit recovery envelope under stated actuator, slack, and dwell assumptions. We validate the controller in Drake multibody simulation with five vehicles, a 10 kg payload, Kelvin-Voigt cables, Dryden wind, and single- and dual-severance schedules: the closed loop attains 0.312-0.328 m RMSE, 76.1-95.2 mm peak sag, and recovery within one payload-pendulum period. Disabling the identity inflates cruise error by 34-39% and peak sag by 3.6x-4.0x, identifying local tension feed-forward as the dominant passive recovery mechanism in the tested decentralized cascade.

</details>


### [22] [Creative Robot Tool Use by Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05411)
*M. Tuluhan Akbulut,Varun Satheesh,Ahmed Jaafar,Alper Ahmetoglu,Shane Parr,Aditya Ganeshan,Shivam Vats,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种机器人创造性工具使用的因果推理框架，通过动力学模型中的模拟实验发现工具与任务间的因果关系，结合VLM特征建议和反事实工具生成，实现基于因果特征的工具选择与技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人工具使用缺乏对因果关系的形式化推理，难以实现超越工具主要用途的创造性工具使用，需要一种能够理解工具物理特性与任务需求间因果关系的框架。

Method: 将因果发现问题解耦为两个互补组件：VLM-based特征建议和通过几何与物理特征扰动进行反事实工具生成；基于识别的因果特征对新颖物体分类；通过关键点匹配实现技能迁移。

Result: 在三种任务场景（用不同棍子够远物、用多样物品舀糖果、用不同箱子/板条箱作为垫脚台取高处物）中，基线比较表明识别因果特征并将其锚定在物理工具属性中可带来更可靠的工具选择和更强的技能关键点迁移。

Conclusion: 通过在动力学模型中重建任务，该方法将工具使用锚定在问题的物理学基础上，因果特征的识别与物理工具属性的结合有效提升了创造性工具使用的性能。

Abstract: We propose a causal reasoning framework for creative robot tool use where a suitable tool for a task is correctly identified for use beyond its primary objectives. The proposed framework first discovers the causal relationships between the tool and the task by conducting simulated experiments in a dynamics model. We decouple the causal discovery problem into two complementary components: VLM-based feature suggestion and counterfactual tool generation via targeted geometric and physical feature perturbations. Then, novel objects are classified based on identified causal features, and the tool use skill is transferred via keypoint matching conditioned on the identified causal features. By reconstructing the task in a dynamics model, our approach grounds tool use in the physics of the problem. We illustrate our approach in reaching a distant object with different sticks, scooping candies from a bowl using diverse items, and using different boxes or crates as stepping platforms to retrieve an object from a high shelf. Our baseline comparisons show that identifying causal features and grounding them in physical tool properties leads to more reliable tool selection and stronger skill keypoint transfer.

</details>


### [23] [Contact-Free Grasp Stability Prediction with In-Hand Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05461)
*Kyle DuFrene,Cindy Grimm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多区域飞行时间传感器的无接触抓取稳定性预测方法，在不接触物体的情况下以15Hz频率预测抓取稳定性，在真实世界实验中达到85.5%-86.0%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于触觉的抓取稳定性分类器需要接触并抓取物体才能检测失败，导致效率低下。本文旨在开发一种无需接触即可预测抓取稳定性的方法，以加快稳定性分类过程。

Method: 在夹爪远端连杆安装多区域飞行时间传感器，收集2500+次真实世界抓取数据训练分类器，在15个物体上训练，并在6个未见物体（3个验证、3个测试）上进行评估。

Result: 验证集准确率85.5%，测试集准确率86.0%，分类频率达15Hz。

Conclusion: 该方法无需接触物体即可有效预测抓取稳定性，显著加快了稳定性分类流程，在真实场景中展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Current approaches to grasp planning for robotics demonstrate high success rates, but degrade with noisy sensors and other factors. Previous works have proposed tactile-based grasp stability classifiers to detect failures, but these approaches rely on making contact and grasping the object to do so. We propose a contact-free grasp stability predictor using multi-zone time-of-flight sensors mounted in the distal links of a gripper. Our method, as it does not require grasping the object to make a prediction, significantly speeds up the stability classification process, cycling at 15 Hz. We collected over 2,500 real-world grasps across 15 objects to train a classifier. Additionally, we conducted grasp attempts over six additional unseen objects, three for validation and model selection, and three for model testing. Our approach demonstrated strong classification performance, with an accuracy of 85.5% on validation and 86.0% on test objects.

</details>


### [24] [Cross-Modal Navigation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06595)
*Shuo Liu,Xinzichen Li,Christopher Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CRONA框架，通过多智能体强化学习实现跨模态导航协作，利用控制相关辅助信念和集中式多模态评论家，在视觉-声学导航任务中显著优于单智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量对齐的多模态数据难以获取，单模态模型训练面临策略空间膨胀问题，需要可扩展的跨模态协作范式。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架CRONA，包含控制相关的辅助信念和具有全局状态的集中式多模态评论家。

Result: 视觉-声学导航实验表明多智能体方法在性能和效率上显著优于单智能体基线；同质协作适合短程显著线索导航；异质协作通用高效；复杂大环境需要更丰富的多模态感知和更大模型容量。

Conclusion: CRONA框架有效实现了跨模态协作导航，不同协作模式适用于不同复杂度的导航场景。

Abstract: Robust embodied navigation relies on complementary sensory cues. However, high-quality and well-aligned multi-modal data is often difficult to obtain in practice. Training a monolithic model is also challenging as rich multi-modal inputs induce complex representations and substantially enlarge the policy space. Cross-modal collaboration among lightweight modality-specialized agents offers a scalable paradigm. It enables flexible deployment and parallel execution, while preserving the strength of each modality. In this paper, we propose \textbf{CRONA}, a Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for \textbf{Cro}ss-Modal \textbf{Na}vigation. CRONA improves collaboration by leveraging control-relevant auxiliary beliefs and a centralized multi-modal critic with global state. Experiments on visual-acoustic navigation tasks show that multi-agent methods significantly improve performance and efficiency over single-agent baselines. We find that homogeneous collaboration with limited modalities is sufficient for short-range navigation under salient cues; heterogeneous collaboration among agents with complementary modalities is generally efficient and effective; and navigation in large, complex environments requires both richer multi-modal perception and increased model capacity.

</details>


### [25] [Robust $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ Controller Design For INDI-Controlled Quadrotor Using Online Parameter Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05483)
*Tom Aantjes,Till M. Blaha,Spilios Theodoulis,Ewoud J. J. Smeur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于在线参数辨识的增量非线性动态逆（INDI）控制器外环增益鲁棒调度方法，通过信号基$\mathcal{H}_\infty$闭环整形设计了增益调度级联姿态控制器，在仿真和飞行实验中验证了其在参数不确定性下的稳定性和跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INDI控制器的物理参数可在半秒内在线估计，但目前缺乏根据模型参数鲁棒调节外环增益的方法。

Method: 采用基于INDI的内环结合在线系统参数辨识，利用信号基$\mathcal{H}_\infty$闭环整形为对称四旋翼设计带有前馈滤波器的增益调度级联姿态控制器。

Result: 控制器具有良好的稳定裕度，非线性仿真验证了不确定性下的有效跟踪性能。飞行实验中，即使辨识参数远超出定义的不确定范围，当执行器时间常数低于40 ms时仍能保持可接受的飞行性能。

Conclusion: 所提增益调度方法能够在线参数辨识下实现鲁棒控制，对参数偏差具有一定容忍度，执行器响应时间对性能影响关键。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that all physical parameters of an Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) controller can be estimated onboard a multirotor within half a second, which is fast enough to do the full identification during a throw in the air. However, a robust method to tune outer loop gains for this feedback-linearizing INDI controller depending on the model parameters is still missing. This work presents the design of a robust gain-scheduled controller for attitude control of quadrotor, using an INDI-based inner loop with online identification of its system parameters. A gain-scheduled cascaded attitude controller with a feedforward filter is synthesized for a symmetric quadrotor using signal-based $\mathcal{H}_\infty$ closed-loop shaping. The resulting controller exhibits good stability margins, with nonlinear simulations confirming effective tracking performance under uncertainty. Experimental evaluation is also conducted through flight tests with full online parameter identification. Even though the identified parameters during these tests are far outside the defined uncertainty range, acceptable flight performance comparable to simulation results is maintained for actuator time constants below 40 ms.

</details>


### [26] [Real-world Latency Analysis of Vehicular Visible Light Communication with Multiple LED Transmitters and an Event-Based Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05541)
*Ryota Soga,Tsukasa Shimizu,Shintaro Shiba,Quan Kong,Shan Lu,Takaya Yamazato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件相机的可见光通信系统，通过正事件模式、协议设计、多发射器识别等方法，解决了带宽饱和、多路接收和延迟问题，在真实车辆场景中验证了其满足协同感知需求，表明事件相机VLC可作为现有V2X技术的可行补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率、低延迟和宽动态范围等优势，有望成为V2X应用中VLC的理想接收器，但现有系统面临带宽饱和、多发射器同时接收以及延迟特性不明确等关键挑战。

Method: 采用正事件模式并设计协议抑制事件生成，同时保持通信距离和宽视野；提出多发射器识别方法，实现最多三个LED的同时接收；在真实车辆场景中评估端到端延迟。

Result: 成功实现了多发射器同时接收（最多三个LED），系统端到端延迟满足协同感知需求。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的VLC系统是现有V2X技术（如射频）的可行补充方案。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution, low latency, and wide dynamic range, making them promising receivers for visible light communication (VLC) in vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. This work presents an event-camera-based VLC system addressing three key challenges: bandwidth saturation, multi-transmitter reception, and latency characterization.
  We adopt a positive-event-only mode and design a protocol that suppresses event generation while maintaining communication distance and a wide field of view. We also propose a method to identify multiple transmitters and demonstrate simultaneous reception from up to three LEDs. Finally, we evaluate end-to-end latency in real vehicular scenarios and show that the system meets cooperative perception requirements. These results demonstrate that event-camera-based VLC is a feasible complement to existing V2X technologies (e.g., RF).

</details>


### [27] [On the Emergence of Pendular Structure in Multi-Contact Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05707)
*Lingxue Lyu,Zihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了线性倒立摆模型（LIPM）在腿足运动控制中并非作为控制器成本函数的偏好，而是作为建模选择的现象。通过一个惩罚角动量变化率的小规模质心最优控制问题，分析了最优解的特征：全秩支撑姿态下最优力模式趋向钟摆模式；双足支撑（如小跑）时摩擦锥引入角动量变化率的下界；添加非零角动量变化率的任务项会可预测地偏移最优解。实验使用点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1在MuJoCo中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIPM在腿足运动控制中广泛使用，但通常是作为建模选择而非控制器成本函数真正偏好的行为。本文试图建立两者之间的明确联系，揭示LIPM与最优控制中角动量惩罚之间的内在关系。

Method: 从一个惩罚角动量变化率的小规模质心最优控制问题出发，分析了最优解的结构特征。使用SVD分析力矩雅可比矩阵，推导了全秩支撑和双足支撑（N=2）两种工况下的理论结果。分别在点质量四足机器人和Unitree Go1上通过开环QP和闭环转矩级控制器进行实验验证。

Result: 全秩支撑姿态下最优力模式趋向钟摆模式，其速率由力矩雅可比矩阵的SVD决定，常数由足部跨度几何决定且与实验误差在16%以内。双足支撑（如小跑）时摩擦锥引入角动量变化率的下界，该下界无法通过权重调节消除；存在一个非光滑可行性拐点，其临界水平加速度可闭式求解。添加非零角动量变化率的任务项会可预测地将最优解偏移出钟摆集合。

Conclusion: 本文结果与经典ZMP/DCM框架高度一致，但提供了更明确的优化视角解释。实验验证了理论预测的有效性，同时指出了闭环控制下渐近分析失效的边界条件。

Abstract: LIPM is everywhere in legged-locomotion control, but almost always as a modeling choice rather than as something the
  controller's cost actually prefers. This note tries to make that link more explicit. Working from a small centroidal
  OCP that penalizes the rate of angular momentum, we look at what its optimum tends to look like. Three things come
  out. With full-rank stance, the optimum drifts toward a pendular force pattern at a rate determined by the SVD of the
  moment Jacobian; the constant is set by foot-span geometry and matches the experiments to within 16%. With N=2 stance,
  as in trot, the friction cone introduces a lower bound on $\|\dot{H}_G\|$ that no amount of weight tuning fixes; we
  also see a non-smooth feasibility kink at a critical horizontal acceleration that we can write in closed form. Adding
  a task term that asks for a nonzero $\dot{H}_G$ moves the optimum off the pendular set in a predictable way. None of
  this is far from the classical ZMP/DCM picture. We test these claims on a point-mass quadruped and on the Unitree Go1
  in MuJoCo (open-loop QP and a torque-level closed-loop controller), and we note where the asymptotic story stops being
  a good description of what the closed loop actually does.

</details>


### [28] [MaMi-HOI: Harmonizing Global Kinematics and Local Geometry for Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05756)
*Hao Wang,Shiqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MaMi-HOI框架，通过几何感知邻近适配器(GAPA)和运动学和谐适配器(KHA)解决扩散模型在3D人-物交互中因语义特征掩盖几何特征导致的"几何遗忘"问题，实现自然运动与精确接触的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人-物交互生成方法在语义对齐上表现优异，但难以维持精确的物体接触。作者发现一个关键现象——"几何遗忘"：随着扩散模型深度增加，语义特征会掩盖物体几何特征，导致模型失去对物体几何的感知能力。

Method: 提出MaMi-HOI层次化框架：1) 几何感知邻近适配器(GAPA)显式重新注入密集物体细节，进行残差快照校正以实现精确接触；2) 运动学和谐适配器(KHA)主动对齐全身姿态与空间目标，确保骨架主动适应约束而不失自然性。

Result: 实验验证MaMi-HOI能同时实现自然运动与精确接触，并扩展至具有复杂轨迹的长期任务，弥合全局导航与高保真操作之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过显式对抗"几何遗忘"并平衡宏观运动流畅性与微观空间精度，MaMi-HOI有效提升了3D人-物交互生成的质量和泛化能力。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is a fundamental task for applications ranging from embodied AI to virtual content creation, which requires harmonizing high-level semantic intent with strict low-level physical constraints. Existing methods excel at semantic alignment, however, they struggle to maintain precise object contact. We reveal a key finding termed \textit{Geometric Forgetting}: as diffusion model depth increases, semantic feature tend to overshadow object geometry feature, causing the model to lose its perception to object geometry. To address this, we propose MaMi-HOI, a hierarchical framework reconciling \textbf{Ma}cro-level kinematic fluidity with \textbf{Mi}cro-level spatial precision. First, to counteract geometric forgetting, we introduce the Geometry-Aware Proximity Adapter (GAPA), which explicitly re-injects dense object details to perform residual snapping corrections for precise contact. Nevertheless, such aggressive local enforcement can disrupt global dynamics, leading to robotic stiffness. In response, we introduce the Kinematic Harmony Adapter (KHA), which proactively aligns whole-body posture with spatial objectives, ensuring the skeleton actively accommodates constraints without compromising naturalness. Extensive experiments validate that MaMi-HOI simultaneously achieves natural motion and precise contact. Crucially, it extends generation capabilities to long-term tasks with complex trajectories, effectively bridging the gap between global navigation and high-fidelity manipulation in 3D scenes. Code is available at https://github.com/DON738110198/MaMi-HOI.git

</details>
