*Benedetta Rota,Mirko Mizzoni,Amr Afifi,Pieter van Goor,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过仿真比较了增量非线性动态逆（INDI）与带非线性干扰观测器的非线性动态逆（NDI+NDO）在全驱动飞行机器人上的鲁棒性，结果表明INDI在多种模型失配和组合应力场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性，而NDI+NDO主要在标称工况下表现相当但在非理想条件下更为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对全驱动飞行机器人，系统比较两种主流非线性控制策略（INDI与NDI+NDO）在参数变化、外部干扰和测量噪声下的鲁棒性差异，为实际应用提供选型指导。

Method: 采用仿真方法，在代表性运行场景下系统化开展控制实验，对比INDI和NDI+NDO在参数摄动、外部扰动和测量噪声下的跟踪性能、鲁棒性和控制能耗。

Result: INDI在多种模型失配和组合应力情况下展现出更强的鲁棒性；NDI+NDO在标称工况下与INDI性能相当，但在多种非理想条件下表现出更高的敏感性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为飞行机器人控制中增量式与基于观测器的反演策略提供了各自的优势与局限的实践性指导，INDI在鲁棒性方面更具优势。

Abstract: This work presents a simulation-based comparative robustness analysis of Incremental Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion (INDI) and Nonlinear Dynamic Inversion augmented with a nonlinear disturbance observer (NDI+NDO) for fully actuated aerial robots. A systematic simulation campaign across representative operating scenarios is conducted, where we compare tracking performance, robustness, control effort, under parametric variations, external disturbances, and measurement noise. Results show that INDI demonstrates stronger robustness in several model-mismatch and combined-stress cases, while NDI+NDO primarily matches nominal performance but exhibits greater sensitivity under several non-ideal conditions. These findings provide practical guidance on the relative strengths and limitations of incremental and observer-based inversion strategies for aerial robotic applications.

</details>


### [30] [Cycle-resolved Cephalopod-Inspired Pulsed-Jet Robot With High-Volume Expulsion and Drag-Reduced Gliding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05875)
*Yiyuan Zhang,Anye Zhong,Junkai Chen,Wenci Xin,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受头足类生物启发的脉冲喷射机器人，采用刚柔混合折纸结构的外套膜，实现75%的有效腔体体积收缩和约75.7%的横截面阻力面积减少。机器人峰值速度达0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），平均速度超0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s），并建立了喷射-滑翔-充填循环解析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头足类生物的脉冲喷射运动并非单一喷射事件，而是包含喷射、被动滑翔和外套膜充填的协调循环。现有机器人研究多关注单次喷射，缺乏对这一完整周期运动机制的研究与复现。

Method: 设计了刚柔混合折纸外套膜结构，刚性折叠板与柔性硅胶框架结合，实现大形变几何引导驱动；搭建循环解析框架，分别研究喷射体积、滑翔时长和充填路径对运动性能的影响。

Result: 机器人峰值速度约0.5 m/s（3.8 BL/s），首个喷射周期内平均速度超0.2 m/s（1.5 BL/s）；验证了高体积收缩比在速度生成中的作用、低阻力滑翔在不同时长的表现，以及被动进气阀辅助充填的效果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了主动变形头足类喷射推进的机器人实现方案，以及用于研究脉冲喷射运动中喷射-滑翔-充填动力学的统一实验平台。

Abstract: Cephalopod pulsed-jet locomotion is not a single isolated expulsion event, but a coordinated cycle involving jet expulsion, passive gliding, and mantle refilling. Inspired by this cycle-resolved biological strategy, this paper presents a cephalopod-inspired pulsed-jet robot with a rigid-soft hybrid origami mantle that enables large, actively driven, and geometry-guided body deformation. The proposed mantle integrates rigid folding panels with a compliant silicone framework, allowing a 75% effective cavity-volume reduction during expulsion and reducing the projected cross-sectional drag area by approximately 75.7% in the contracted gliding configuration. Using this platform, we formulate a cycle-resolved framework to separately investigate how expelled volume, glide duration, and refill pathway influence whole-cycle locomotion performance. Experiments show that the robot reaches a peak speed of approximately 0.5 m/s (3.8 BL/s) and an average speed exceeding 0.2 m/s (1.5 BL/s) within the first jetting cycle. The results further demonstrate the roles of high expelled-volume-ratio contraction in speed generation, reduced-drag-area gliding under different glide durations, and mantle-aperture-inspired passive inlet valves in assisting refill. This work provides both a robotic implementation of actively deformable cephalopod-like jet propulsion and a unified experimental platform for studying expulsion-gliding-refilling dynamics in pulsed-jet locomotion.

</details>


### [31] [Generating Roadside LiDAR Datasets from Vehicle-Side Datasets via Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05897)
*Yuhan Xia,Runxin Zhao,Hanyang Zhuang,Chunxiang Wang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VRS框架，通过车辆点云补全和基于占位的可见性约束，将车载LiDAR数据集转换为路侧LiDAR数据集，缓解路侧感知数据稀缺问题，提升3D目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模标注的路侧LiDAR数据集稀缺，难以训练高性能路侧感知模型。

Method: 提出VRS框架：1）车辆点云补全补偿缺失几何；2）基于占位的可见性约束处理大视角变化下的跨视角渲染；3）实现灵活多视角渲染以生成可扩展的路侧数据。

Result: 合成的路侧数据有效补充真实路侧数据，缓解了真实数据有限的局限性，并提升了在未见过的路侧视角上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VRS通过数据合成方式缓解路侧LiDAR标注数据稀缺问题，在3D目标检测任务上验证了合成数据的有效性。

Abstract: Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) require reliable environmental perception to support safe and efficient transportation. With the rapid development of Vehicle-to-everything (V2X), roadside perception has become an effective means to extend sensing coverage and improve traffic safety. However, the scarcity of large-scale annotated roadside LiDAR datasets poses a major challenge for training high-performance roadside perception models. In this paper, we introduce Vehicle-to-Roadside LiDAR Synthesis (VRS), a data synthesis framework that generates labeled roadside LiDAR datasets from vehicle-side datasets via LiDAR novel view synthesis. To mitigate the vehicle-to-roadside domain gap, VRS employs vehicle point cloud completion to compensate for missing geometry in vehicle-side observations, and introduces an occupancy-based visibility constraint to handle large viewpoint changes during cross-view rendering. The proposed framework enables flexible multi-view rendering for scalable roadside data generation. Extensive experiments on roadside 3D object detection demonstrate that the synthesized data effectively complements real roadside data, mitigates the limitations of limited real-world roadside data, and improves generalization to unseen roadside viewpoints.

</details>


### [32] [DexSynRefine: Synthesizing and Refining Human-Object Interaction Motion for Physically Feasible Dexterous Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05925)
*Hyesung Lee,Hyunwoo Jung,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSynRefine是一个三组件框架，从稀疏的人-物交互演示中合成灵巧操作轨迹，通过任务空间残差强化学习进行物理落地，并利用接触动力学自适应模块实现sim-to-real迁移，在五个灵巧操作任务上优于基线方法50-70个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人-物交互数据学习灵巧操作是一种可扩展的替代遥操作的方法，但HOI演示数据稀疏且仅提供运动学信息，在实体不匹配和接触丰富动力学条件下不可直接执行。

Method: 提出DexSynRefine框架，包含三个耦合组件：1) HOI-MMFP，一种任务和物体初始状态条件化的运动流形基元扩展，从稀疏HOI演示合成协调的手-物体轨迹；2) 任务空间残差RL策略，在继承运动学结构的同时物理落地合成参考轨迹；3) 接触和动力学自适应模块，利用本体感受历史实现sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在五个灵巧操作任务（抓取放置、工具使用、物体重定向）上，任务空间残差策略在仿真中优于先前动作表征基线，并在真实机器人上全部五个任务迁移成功，相比运动学重定向提升50-70个百分点。

Conclusion: DexSynRefine有效解决了从稀疏HOI数据学习灵巧操作中的实体不匹配和接触动力学挑战，证明了任务空间残差策略结合运动流形基元在灵巧操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from human-object interaction (HOI) data is a scalable alternative to teleoperation, but HOI demonstrations are sparse and provide only kinematic motion that is not directly executable under embodiment mismatch and contact-rich dynamics. We present DexSynRefine, a framework with three coupled components: HOI-MMFP, a task- and object-initial-state-conditioned extension of motion manifold primitives that synthesizes coordinated hand-object trajectories from sparse HOI demonstrations; a task-space residual RL policy that physically grounds the synthesized reference while inheriting its kinematic structure; and a contact-and-dynamics adaptation module that enables sim-to-real transfer from proprioceptive history. Across five dexterous manipulation tasks spanning pick-and-place, tool use, and object reorientation, our task-space residual policy outperforms prior action-representation baselines in simulations and transfers to a real robot on all five tasks, improving over kinematic retargeting by 50-70 percentage points.

</details>


### [33] [Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion for Universal Goal-Oriented Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05960)
*Zhixuan Shen,Yijie Zeng,Shengxiang Luo,Tianrui Li,Haonan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PLMD，一种基于扩散模型的地图补全方法，通过为未观测区域生成障碍物和语义标签，解决目标导向导航中部分观测环境下的目标定位问题，在三个GON任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于地图的GON方法通常使用自中心语义地图，面临依赖完整地图或语义关联不一致的挑战，亟需一种能在部分观测环境下完成地图补全并实现准确目标定位的方法。

Method: 提出Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion (PLMD)，基于DDPM定义新颖的地图补全扩散模型，通过扩散过程生成未观测区域的障碍物和语义标签；利用已知与未知障碍物布局的结构一致性，将障碍物先验融入语义去噪过程，缓解语义关联不一致问题。

Result: PLMD有效扩展未知地图区域，可无缝集成到现有依赖语义地图的导航策略中，在三个GON任务上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: PLMD通过扩散模型实现部分观测环境下的地图补全与语义生成，为GON提供了一种即插即用且高效的解决方案，显著提升了导航能力。

Abstract: In embodied vision, Goal-Oriented Navigation (GON) requires robots to locate a specific goal within an unexplored environment. The primary challenge of GON arises from the need to construct a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) map to understand the environment while simultaneously localizing an unobserved goal. Existing map-based methods typically employ self-centered semantic maps, often facing challenges such as reliance on complete maps or inconsistent semantic association. To this end, we propose Plug-and-Play Label Map Diffusion (PLMD), which defines a novel map completion diffusion model based on Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM). PLMD generates obstacle and semantic labels for unobserved regions through a diffusion-based completion process, thereby enabling goal localization even in partially observed environments. Moreover, it mitigates inconsistent semantic association by leveraging structural consistency between known and unknown obstacle layouts and integrating obstacle priors into the semantic denoising process. By substituting predicted labels for unobserved regions, robots can accurately localize the specified objects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PLMD \textbf{(I)} effectively expands the region of unknown maps, \textbf{(II)} integrates seamlessly into existing navigation strategies that rely on semantic maps, \textbf{(III)} achieves state-of-the-art performance on three GON tasks.

</details>


### [34] [Accurate Trajectory Tracking with MPCC for Flapping-Wing MAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06042)
*Charbel Toumieh,Jack Zeng,Niel Mistry,Dario Floreano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC）的扑翼飞行器控制方法，通过弧长参数化轨迹跟踪和在线优化进度，实现了对鸟尺度扑翼飞行器耦合动力学的精确自主控制，在XFly平台上以高达3 m/s的速度实现了6.5-9 cm的平均轨迹偏差，较之前方法提升了近10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器比旋翼无人机更安静、更安全，但其升力、空速和转向能力高度耦合且仅由少数控制输入决定，而传统级联控制器将高度、速度和航向独立处理，导致复杂机动中持续存在跟踪误差；时间参数化轨迹跟踪需要预定义速度剖面，现有方法无法鲁棒地处理这些耦合动力学。

Method: 提出模型预测轮廓控制（MPCC）方法，跟踪弧长参数化轨迹的同时在线优化进度，消除对预定义时序的需求；提出紧凑、连续可微的动力学模型，捕获鸟尺度扑翼飞行器的主要耦合效应，满足实时非线性优化的计算预算。

Result: 在XFly扑翼飞行器上沿圆形和三维竞速轨迹验证，在高达3 m/s的速度下实现平均轨迹偏差6.5-9 cm，较之前扑翼控制方法提升近10倍。

Conclusion: 所提MPCC方法结合紧凑耦合动力学模型，能够在实时约束下显著提升扑翼飞行器的轨迹跟踪精度，有效解决传统方法在处理复杂耦合动力学时的局限性。

Abstract: Flapping-wing micro aerial vehicles offer quieter and safer operation than rotary-wing drones, yet achieving precise autonomous control of bird-scale ornithopters remains challenging: lift, airspeed, and turning authority are tightly coupled and governed by only a few control inputs. Conventional cascaded controllers treat altitude, speed, and heading independently, producing persistent tracking errors during complex maneuvers, while time-parameterized trajectory tracking requires predefined speed profiles that existing methods cannot robustly produce for these coupled dynamics. We address both limitations simultaneously with a Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) approach that tracks arc-length-parameterized trajectories while optimizing progress online, eliminating the need for predefined timing. However, MPCC requires a dynamical model that captures the coupled aerodynamics without exceeding the computational budget of real-time nonlinear optimization. Here, we propose a compact, continuously differentiable model that captures the dominant couplings of bird-scale ornithopters, enabling real-time predictive control. We validated the method with the XFly ornithopter flying along circular and three-dimensional racing trajectories and achieved a mean deviation from the reference trajectory between 6.5 and 9 cm at speeds up to 3 m/s, which represents an almost 10-fold improvement over prior ornithopter control methods.

</details>


### [35] [When to Trust Imagination: Adaptive Action Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06222)
*Rui Wang,Yue Zhang,Jiehong Lin,Kuncheng Luo,Jianan Wang,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FFDC，一种轻量级验证器，通过联合推理预测动作、预测视觉动态、真实观测和语言指令，实现世界动作模型的自适应执行。该方法能在预测与现实一致时延长执行步数，不一致时提前重新规划，在RoboTwin基准上减少69.10%前向传播和34.02%执行时间，同时提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在执行固定数量的预测动作后才会重新推理，导致机器人无法感知预测的未来是否与实际物理 rollout 一致，在复杂或接触密集的任务中缺乏响应能力。

Method: 提出Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention (FFDC) 轻量级验证器，联合推理预测动作、预测视觉动态、真实观测和语言指令，判断剩余动作序列是否可信；并引入Mixture-of-Horizon Training提升长程轨迹覆盖。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准上减少69.10%前向传播和34.02%执行时间，提升2.54%成功率；真实世界实验提升35%成功率。

Conclusion: FFDC实现了动作块大小的自适应调整，在保持长程执行效率的同时，在接触密集或困难阶段恢复响应能力，实现了鲁棒性与效率的强平衡。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual observations and future actions. However, current WAMs typically execute a fixed number of predicted actions after each model inference, leaving the robot blind to whether the imagined future remains consistent with the actual physical rollout. In this work, we formulate adaptive WAM execution as a future-reality verification problem: the robot should execute longer when the WAM-predicted future remains reliable, and replan earlier when reality deviates from imagination. To this end, we propose Future Forward Dynamics Causal Attention (FFDC), a lightweight verifier that jointly reasons over predicted future actions, predicted visual dynamics, real observations, and language instructions to estimate whether the remaining action rollout can still be trusted. FFDC enables adaptive action chunk sizes as an emergent consequence of prediction-observation consistency, preserving the efficiency of long-horizon execution while restoring responsiveness in contact-rich or difficult phases. We further introduce Mixture-of-Horizon Training to improve long-horizon trajectory coverage for adaptive execution. Experiments on the RoboTwin benchmark and in the real world demonstrate that our method achieves a strong robustness-efficiency trade-off: on RoboTwin, it reduces WAM forward passes by 69.10% and execution time by 34.02%, while improving success rate by 2.54% over the short-chunk baseline; in real-world experiments, it improves success rate by 35%.

</details>


### [36] [RobotEQ: Transitioning from Passive Intelligence to Active Intelligence in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06234)
*Kuofei Fang,Xinyi Che,Haomin Ouyang,Shufan Zhang,Xuehao Wang,Qi Liu,Liyi Liu,Chenqi Zhang,Wenxi Cai,Wenyu Dai,Jinyang Wu,Fan Zhang,Haoyu Chen,Bin He,Zheng Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RobotEQ，首个面向主动智能的基准测试，旨在评估现有模型在具身场景中理解和遵守社会规范的能力。该基准包含1900张第一人称图像、5353个动作判断问题和1286个空间定位问题，实验表明当前模型在主动智能上表现不佳，尤其是空间定位方面，而引入RAG技术可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI研究聚焦于根据用户明确指令完成任务，但机器人融入人类社会需要理解哪些行为允许或禁止，即使没有明确指令。本文将用户引导的AI称为被动智能，无引导的AI称为主动智能，但目前缺乏评估主动智能的基准。

Method: 构建RobotEQ-Data数据集，包含1900张第一人称图像，涵盖10个具身类别和56个子类别，通过人工标注提供5353个动作判断问题和1286个空间定位问题。建立RobotEQ-Bench评估框架，对现有最先进模型进行评测。

Result: 当前模型在主动智能任务上表现不足，尤其在空间定位方面表现较差。利用RAG技术引入外部社会规范知识库可普遍提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了机器人从用户引导的被动操控向主动遵守社会规范的转变，揭示了当前模型在社会规范理解方面的局限性，并指出了RAG技术作为提升路径的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied AI is a prominent research topic in both academia and industry. Current research centers on completing tasks based on explicit user instructions. However, for robots to integrate into human society, they must understand which actions are permissible and which are prohibited, even without explicit commands. We refer to the user-guided AI as passive intelligence and the unguided AI as active intelligence. This paper introduces RobotEQ, the first benchmark for active intelligence, aiming to assess whether existing models can comprehend and adhere to social norms in embodied scenarios. First, we construct RobotEQ-Data, a dataset consisting of 1,900 egocentric images, spanning 10 representative embodied categories and 56 subcategories. Through extensive manual annotation, we provide 5,353 action judgment questions and 1,286 spatial grounding questions, specifying appropriate robot actions across diverse scenarios. Furthermore, we establish RobotEQ-Bench to evaluate the performance of state-of-the-art models on this task. Experimental results show that current models still fall short in achieving reliable active intelligence, particularly in spatial grounding. Meanwhile, we observe that leveraging RAG techniques to incorporate external social norm knowledge bases can generally enhance performance. This work can facilitate the transition of robotics from user-guided passive manipulation to active social compliance.

</details>


### [37] [CKT-WAM: Parameter-Efficient Context Knowledge Transfer Between World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06247)
*Yuhua Jiang,Yijun Guo,Hongbing Yang,Guojun Lei,Nuo Chen,Yinuo Zhang,Shaoqiang Yan,Bo Lin,Feifei Gao,Biqing Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CKT-WAM，一种通过文本嵌入空间中的紧凑上下文进行知识迁移的参数高效框架，在LIBERO-Plus上仅用1.17%可训练参数达到86.1%总成功率，并在真实世界长时程操作任务中取得83.3%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）在具身控制中表现出色，但异构WAM间的知识迁移面临潜在接口不匹配、高适应成本和传统蒸馏目标僵化等挑战。

Method: CKT-WAM通过压缩器的可学习查询交叉注意力（LQCA）提取并压缩教师中间隐状态，利用始终在线的广义适配器、轻量路由器和稀疏激活的专用适配器进行变换，将生成的上下文附加到学生的条件文本嵌入中，实现知识注入。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上达到86.1%总成功率（仅1.17%可训练参数），接近全微调性能；在真实世界四类多步长时程任务中达到83.3%平均成功率。

Conclusion: CKT-WAM通过紧凑上下文而非输出模仿或密集隐状态匹配，实现了高效异构WAM知识迁移，在仿真和真实场景中均展现优异性能。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) provide a powerful generative framework for embodied control, yet transferring knowledge across heterogeneous WAMs remains challenging due to mismatched latent interfaces, high adaptation cost, and the rigidity of conventional distillation objectives. We propose \textbf{CKT-WAM}, a parameter-efficient \textbf{C}ontext \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{T}ransfer framework that transfers teacher WAM's knowledge into a student WAM through a compact context in the text embedding space, rather than output imitation or dense hidden-state matching. Specifically, CKT-WAM extracts intermediate teacher hidden states, reduces the number of tokens via compressors' learnable-query cross attention (LQCA), and transforms them through an always-on generalized adapter, a lightweight router, and sparsely activated specialized adapters. The resulting context is then appended to the student's conditioning textual embeddings, thereby injecting the transferred knowledge into the student with minimal architectural modification. Experiments show that CKT-WAM consistently improves zero-shot generalization and achieves the best overall performance on LIBERO-Plus, reaching 86.1\% total success rate with only 1.17\% trainable parameters, while approaching full fine-tuning performance. Beyond simulation, CKT-WAM also demonstrates strong real-world long-horizon manipulation ability, achieving the best average success rate of 83.3\% across four multi-step and long-horizon tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YuhuaJiang2002/CKT-WAM.

</details>


### [38] [Toward Visually Realistic Simulation: A Benchmark for Evaluating Robot Manipulation in Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06311)
*Yixin Zhu,Zixiong Wang,Jian Yang,Jin Xie,Jingyi Yu,Jiayuan Gu,Beibei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VISER，一个基于物理渲染的高保真机器人操控仿真基准，通过精细化光照和材质建模缩小sim-to-real视觉差距，实现了仿真与真实环境之间平均0.92的皮尔逊相关系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真基准虽然覆盖多种任务类别，但缺乏视觉真实感，导致仿真与真实环境之间存在巨大的领域差距，削弱了仿真评估预测真实世界性能的可靠性。光照和材质因素对几何推理和空间定位至关重要，但在现有基准中被严重忽视。

Method: 1) 构建包含1000+3D资产的高保真数据集，采用物理渲染(PBR)材质；2) 提出基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的自动化管线，实现材质感知的部件分割和材质检索，支持可扩展的物理逼真资产生成；3) 基于高保真资产构建多样化的评估任务（抓取、放置、长时序任务），用于评估视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型。

Result: VISER在仿真与真实世界性能之间实现了强相关性，不同策略的平均皮尔逊相关系数达到0.92。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析光照和材质的影响并构建高视觉真实感的仿真基准，可以显著提升仿真评估对真实世界性能的预测可靠性，为VLA模型的可扩展、可复现评估提供了有效平台。

Abstract: Reliable simulation evaluation of robot manipulation policies serves as a high-fidelity proxy for real-world performance. Although existing benchmarks cover a wide range of task categories, they lack visual realism, creating a large domain gap between simulation and reality. This undermines the reliability of simulation-based evaluation in predicting real-world performance. To mitigate the sim-to-real visual gap, we conduct a systematic analysis to isolate the effects of lighting and material. Our results show that these factors play a critical role in geometric reasoning and spatial grounding, yet are largely overlooked in existing benchmarks. Motivated by the analysis, we propose VISER, a visually realistic benchmark for evaluating robot manipulation in simulation. VISER features a high-fidelity dataset of over 1,000 3D assets with physically-based rendering (PBR) materials, along with 3D scenes created from these assets through curated layouts or generation. To this end, we propose an automated pipeline leveraging Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for material-aware part segmentation and material retrieval, enabling scalable generation of physically plausible assets. Building on the high-fidelity 3D asset dataset, we construct diverse evaluation tasks, such as grasping, placing, and long-horizon tasks, enabling scalable and reproducible assessment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. Our benchmark shows a strong correlation between simulation and real-world performance, achieving an average Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.92 across different policies.

</details>


### [39] [AssistDLO: Assistive Teleoperation for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06323)
*Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Kay Pompetzki,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AssistDLO框架，结合多视角状态估计、视觉辅助和基于控制障碍函数的几何感知共享自主控制器（SA-CBF），用于机器人操作可变形线性物体（DLO）的辅助遥操作。22人双手解绳结用户研究表明，SA-CBF对新手用户提升最显著（成功率从71%提升至88%），而专家用户偏好视觉辅助，且不同绳索特性需要不同辅助策略，揭示了DLO遥操作需要自适应、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO操作因其无限维构型空间和复杂非线性动力学极具挑战性，遥操作中深度不确定性阻碍状态感知与反应，而传统共享自主方法常依赖简单几何吸引子，无法保持DLO几何形态。

Method: 提出AssistDLO框架，包含实时多视角状态估计、视觉辅助（VA）和基于控制障碍函数的几何感知共享自主控制器（SA-CBF），SA-CBF作为几何感知漏斗实现精确抓取同时保留操作者高级别控制权。

Result: 22人双手解绳结实验表明：SA-CBF对新手用户效果最显著（任务成功率从71%提升至88%），对刚性绳索更有效；专家用户偏好视觉辅助；高柔性长绳索更受益于视觉支持而非局部动作辅助。

Conclusion: 有效DLO遥操作不能依赖固定策略，亟需自适应、用户感知和材料感知的共享自主方法。

Abstract: Manipulating Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) is challenging in robotics due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and complex nonlinear dynamics. In teleoperation, depth uncertainty hinders state perception and reaction. AssistDLO addresses this challenge as an assistive teleoperation framework for DLO manipulation that combines real-time multi-view state estimation, visual assistance (VA), and a geometry-aware shared-autonomy controller based on Control Barrier Functions (SA-CBF). While traditional shared autonomy methods often rely on simple geometric attractors and may fail to preserve DLO geometry, SA-CBF acts as a geometry-aware funnel, facilitating precise grasping while preserving the operator's high-level authority. The framework is evaluated in a bimanual knot-untangling user study (N = 22) using ropes with varying length and rigidity. Results show that the effectiveness of the assistance depends strongly on operator expertise and DLO properties. SA-CBF provides the strongest gains for naive users, acting as a skill equalizer that increases task success from 71% to 88%, and is effective for stiffer ropes. Conversely, expert users prefer VA, and highly compliant, long ropes benefit more from visual support than localized action assistance. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that effective DLO teleoperation cannot rely on a fixed strategy, highlighting the critical need for adaptive, user-aware, and material-aware shared autonomy.

</details>


### [40] [TouchDrive: Electronics-Free Tactile Sensing Interface for Assistive Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06432)
*Jing Xu,Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gurdur Broo,Klas Hjort*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchDrive是一种低成本、无电子元件的触觉传感接口，通过气动阀门将接触力直接转换为触觉反馈，帮助用户在辅助抓取中调节抓取力，实现对易碎和柔性物体的精确操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助抓取系统依赖电子传感和多阶段处理流程，系统复杂且可及性低，亟需一种简化、低成本的替代方案。

Method: 采用气动常闭阀、压缩空气罐、传感元件和触觉反馈执行器构建纯机械闭环回路，通过阀门切换将接触力直接转化为气动触觉反馈。

Result: 在多种机器人平台上验证，成功实现对水果和日常物品（最多20种）的可靠抓取，展现出稳定的性能和实际应用价值。

Conclusion: TouchDrive通过无电子的触觉反馈机制，有效降低了辅助抓取系统的复杂度和成本，提升了可及性与操作精细度。

Abstract: Assistive robotic grasping plays an important role in enabling safe and adaptive manipulation of diverse objects. However, existing systems often rely on electronic sensing and multi-stage processing pipelines, increasing system complexity and reducing accessibility. To address these limitations, we present TouchDrive, a cost-effective, electronics-free tactile sensing interface for assistive grasping. TouchDrive directly converts contact forces into pneumatic feedback through valve-mediated switching, integrating sensing, signal generation, and feedback within a single passive mechanical loop. The system can be employed using a pneumatic normally closed valve, a compressed air tank, sensing element, and haptic feedback actuator without electronics. By delivering tactile cues, TouchDrive empowers users to modulate grasp forces, enabling precise and robust delicate manipulation of compliant and fragile objects. The interface has been validated across diverse robotic platforms, consistently demonstrating reliable performance and practical applicability in assistive grasping tasks, such as handling fruits and everyday items (up to 20 objects).

</details>


### [41] [GA3T: A Ground-Aerial Terrain Traversability Dataset for Heterogeneous Robot Teams in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06478)
*Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Quan Tran,Christian Ricks,Dhanush Parthasarathy,Amir Kaidarov,Neil Deshpande,Sukaina Najm,David Han,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GA3T是一个真实世界的空地机器人协同感知数据集，包含地面无人车（Clearpath Husky）和空中无人机（Autel EVO II）在多种非结构化地形（森林小径、岩石路、泥地、雪地、草地）中的多模态同步数据，总计超过13,000帧、约29分钟，包含SAM 3零样本分割和8,000多张人工标注图像，旨在支持跨视角感知、空地视角融合、可通行性估计和协作场景理解研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同感知在复杂户外环境中潜力巨大，但缺乏真实世界中包含重叠多模态观测的公开数据集，现有数据集多聚焦于SLAM或模拟协同驾驶，难以支撑跨视角感知、空地融合等研究。

Method: 使用Clearpath Husky UGV（搭载3D LiDAR、立体相机、IMU、GPS）和Autel EVO II UAV（搭载RGB相机、热红外传感器、GPS）在4种非结构化环境（森林、岩石、泥地、雪地、草地）中同步采集数据，提供SAM 3零样本分割和8,000+人工标注图像。

Result: 数据集包含4个独特环境、超过13,000帧同步数据（约29分钟），提供跨模态（LiDAR、RGB、热红外、IMU、GPS）和跨视角（地面+空中）观测，早春采集条件下稀疏树冠允许空中机器人部分观测地面机器人和地形，支持遮挡感知协同感知。

Conclusion: GA3T是首个专门针对真实越野环境中空地机器人协同感知设计的数据集，填补了跨视角感知、视角融合、可通行性估计和协作场景理解研究的数据空白。

Abstract: Heterogeneous air-ground robot teams combine complementary sensing modalities, mobility characteristics, and spatial viewpoints that can significantly enhance perception in complex outdoor environments. However, progress in multi-robot collaborative perception has been constrained by the lack of real-world datasets featuring overlapping multi-modal observations from platforms operating in unstructured terrain. We present GA3T (Ground-Aerial Team for Terrain Traversal), a real-world multi-robot collaborative perception dataset collected using a Clearpath Husky UGV and an Autel EVO~II UAV across diverse unstructured environments, including forest trails, rocky paths, muddy terrain, snow piles, and grass-covered fields. The ground platform provides 3D LiDAR, stereo camera, IMU, and GPS data, while the aerial platform contributes RGB imagery, thermal/infrared observations, and GPS from a complementary overhead viewpoint, allowing for rich cross-modal and cross-view perception. The dataset is collected in 4 unique environments, with over 13,000 synchronized frames across approximately 29 minutes of operation, and includes both SAM~3-based zero-shot segmentation and over 8,000 manually labeled images. A unique aspect of the dataset is its early-spring collection period, during which sparse tree canopies allow the aerial robot to partially observe the ground robot and terrain through the trees, allowing for occlusion-aware collaborative perception. Unlike prior multi-robot datasets that focus on SLAM or simulated cooperative driving, GA3T is specifically designed to support research on cross-view perception, air-ground viewpoint fusion, traversability estimation, and collaborative scene understanding in real off-road environments.

</details>


### [42] [OA-WAM: Object-Addressable World Action Model for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06481)
*Yushan Liu,Peibo Sun,Shoujie Li,Yifan Xie,Lingfeng Zhang,Xintao Chao,Shiyuan Dong,Fang Chen,Xiao-Ping Zhang,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OA-WAM模型，通过将场景分解为可寻址的物体槽位（N个物体槽+1个机器人槽），每个槽位包含固定地址向量和时变内容向量，在注意力机制中通过仅使用地址键路由跨槽注意力，并在每层重置地址切片，实现物体身份与当前状态的解耦，从而在场景变化下实现鲁棒的机器人操作。在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上达到SOTA，并在因果槽干预测试中展现出极强的物体绑定能力（0.87 vs 0.09）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）将预测的世界表示为整体图像、视频token或全局隐变量，这些表示在指令涉及特定物体时难以被动作解码器有效利用，尤其在场景偏移（scene shifts）下物体身份与上下文纠缠在一起，导致操作鲁棒性下降。

Method: 1）将每帧分解为N+1个槽位（1个机器人槽+N个物体槽），每个槽包含固定的地址向量（address vector）和时变的内容向量（content vector）；2）槽位与文本、图像、本体感知、历史动作token以块因果序列方式融合；3）世界头（world head）预测下一帧槽状态，流匹配动作头（flow-matching action head）在同一前向传播中解码16步连续动作块；4）通过路由跨槽注意力仅使用地址键（address-only keys），并在每个Transformer层重置地址切片，实现可寻址性（addressability）。

Result: LIBERO上97.8%，SimplerEnv上79.3%；在LIBERO-Plus几何轴上达到SOTA，七轴聚合上保持竞争力；因果槽干预测试中交换绑定余弦为0.87，而整体基线最高仅0.09。

Conclusion: 可寻址的物体状态为场景扰动下的鲁棒世界动作建模提供了有效接口。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enhance Vision-Language-Action policies by jointly predicting scene evolution and robot actions, but existing methods usually represent the predicted world as holistic images, video tokens, or global latents. These representations are difficult for an action decoder to address when an instruction refers to a particular object, especially under scene shifts where object identity is entangled with context. We propose OA-WAM, an Object-Addressable World Action Model for robust robot manipulation. OA-WAM decomposes each frame into N+1 slot states, with one robot slot and N object slots. Each slot contains a persistent address vector and a time-varying content vector, and is fused with text, image, proprioception, and past-action tokens in a block-causal sequence. A world head predicts next-frame slot states, while a flow-matching action head decodes a 16-step continuous action chunk in the same forward pass. Addressability is enforced by routing cross-slot attention through address-only keys and resetting the address slice at every transformer layer, separating which object to act on from what that object currently is without adding extra tokens. OA-WAM matches strong VLA and WAM baselines on LIBERO (97.8%) and SimplerEnv (79.3%), reaches state-of-the-art performance on the most relevant LIBERO-Plus geometric axes, and remains competitive on the seven-axis aggregate. A causal slot-intervention test yields a swap-binding cosine of 0.87, versus at most 0.09 for holistic baselines. These results suggest that addressable object states provide an effective interface for robust world-action modeling under scene perturbations.

</details>


### [43] [ReActor: Reinforcement Learning for Physics-Aware Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06593)
*David Müller,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双层优化框架，将人类运动重定向到机器人形态的同时训练跟踪策略，无需手动调参，能生成物理合理的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动重定向方法常产生脚滑、自碰撞或动力学不可行的运动，阻碍下游模仿学习。

Method: 双层优化框架：上层优化适应运动到机器人形态，下层用强化学习训练跟踪策略；通过推导上层损失近似梯度使优化可处理；仅需稀疏语义刚体对应关系。

Result: 在仿真和硬件上验证，成功将人类运动重定向到与人类差异显著的形态（包括四足机器人），生成物理合理的运动。

Conclusion: 该方法无需手动调参，能保留特征运动，且通过集成物理仿真产生物理合理的运动，促进鲁棒的模仿学习。

Abstract: Retargeting human kinematic reference motion onto a robot's morphology remains a formidable challenge. Existing methods often produce physical inconsistencies, such as foot sliding, self-collisions, or dynamically infeasible motions, which hinder downstream imitation learning. We propose a bilevel optimization framework that jointly adapts reference motions to a robot's morphology while training a tracking policy using reinforcement learning. To make the optimization tractable, we derive an approximate gradient for the upper-level loss. Our framework requires only a sparse set of semantic rigid-body correspondences and eliminates the need for manual tuning by identifying optimal values for a parameterization expressive enough to preserve characteristic motion across different embodiments. Moreover, by integrating retargeting directly with physics simulation, we produce physically plausible motions that facilitate robust imitation learning. We validate our method in simulation and on hardware, demonstrating challenging motions for morphologies that differ significantly from a human, including retargeting onto a quadruped.

</details>


### [44] [Multi-Robot Coordination in V2X Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06662)
*John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向社交机器人的V2X通信框架，包括机器人感知服务（RAS）和机器人机动协调服务（RMCS），通过专用消息实现非集中式的多机器人协作，并在真实场景和仿真中验证了其在道路行人辅助场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X标准主要面向车辆，缺乏对社交机器人（如人形机器人、四足机器人）在复杂城市交通环境中进行角色感知、任务驱动和低延迟协调的通信支持，亟需一种可与ETSI标准对齐的机器人中心化协作框架。

Method: 提出两个设施层服务：RAS（基于Robot Awareness Message实现角色感知、任务导向的机器人感知，并集成外部检测到的弱势道路使用者）和RMCS（基于Robot Maneuver Coordination Message实现事件驱动的低延迟机动协调），采用形式化有限状态机建模协调过程。

Result: 真实场景验证了人形机器人与四足机器人在行人过街场景中的确定性多机器人协调；仿真表明RAS聚类在混合V2X环境中有效整合非V2X弱势道路使用者，并减少V2X使能设备冗余传输，降低信道负载。

Conclusion: 该框架为将协作机器人无缝集成到未来互联、协作和自动化出行生态系统提供了可扩展且符合标准的基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication framework that enables decentralized cooperation among social robots operating in complex urban traffic environments. Building on ETSI Cooperative Awareness and Maneuver Coordination services, the framework introduces two robot-centric facility-layer services: the Robot Awareness Service (RAS) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Service (RMCS), realized through the Robot Awareness Message (RAM) and the Robot Maneuver Coordination Message (RMCM), respectively. RAS enables role-aware, task-oriented robot awareness while integrating externally detected Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs), including non-V2X pedestrians, into cooperative awareness. RMCS supports event-driven, low-latency coordination of robot maneuvers under explicitly established roles, without centralized infrastructure or prior pairing. A real-world proof of concept demonstrates deterministic multi-robot coordination between a humanoid robot and a quadrupedal robot assisting a pedestrian during a road-crossing scenario, governed by a formally specified finite-state coordination model. Complementary simulations evaluate robot-mediated VRU clustering in mixed V2X environments, showing that RAS-based clustering integrates non-V2X VRUs in safety-critical areas while reducing redundant transmissions from V2X-enabled VRUs, thereby lowering channel load. Together, the proposed services provide a scalable and standards-aligned foundation for integrating cooperative robots into future Connected, Cooperative, and Automated Mobility ecosystems.

</details>

### [45] [Performance Characterization of dApps in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05426)
*Conrado Boeira,Eduardo Baena,Andrea Lacava,Tommaso Melodia,Dimitrios Koutsonikolas,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在裸机和容器化环境中部署并评估了代表性O-RAN实时应用（dApps），分析了延迟、可扩展性和资源利用的权衡，并展示了将dApps卸载到智能网卡等硬件加速器上可缓解性能瓶颈、提升实时响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有O-RAN dApps多部署在裸机服务器上，且缺乏对智能与非智能dApp资源使用模式的可见性，阻碍了合理的部署决策。

Method: 在裸机和容器两种部署场景下实现并评估代表性dApps，对比延迟、可扩展性和资源利用的权衡；识别性能瓶颈并通过卸载到智能网卡等硬件加速器进行优化。

Result: 定量表征了不同部署场景下的性能权衡，明确了关键性能瓶颈，并验证了硬件加速器卸载可有效提升实时响应能力。

Conclusion: 容器化部署与硬件加速器卸载相结合是提升O-RAN系统实时性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Despite recommendations to deploy real-time Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) applications (dApps) in containerized environments, existing approaches predominantly rely on bare-metal servers. Moreover, current dApp deployments offer limited visibility into the resource usage patterns of both intelligent and non-intelligent dApps, hindering informed deployment decisions. This work addresses these gaps by implementing and evaluating representative dApps across multiple deployment scenarios (bare-metal and containers) to characterize the trade-offs in latency, scalability, and resource utilization. Additionally, we identify key performance bottlenecks and demonstrate how offloading dApps to emerging hardware accelerators, such as smart Network Interface Cards (NICs), can alleviate these limitations and improve real-time responsiveness in O-RAN systems.

</details>

### [46] [Governed Metaprogramming for Intelligent Systems: Reclassifying Eval as a Governed Effec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05248)
*Alan L. McCann*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种"受控元编程"语言设计，将代码到执行的转换（eval）从语言原语重新分类为受控效应，要求对程序进行结构性检查（能力需求、策略合规、资源估算）后才允许执行，并在MashinTalk DSL中实现，集成了454个Rocq定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI系统（LLM生成代码、智能体构建工作流、自改进系统）中，代码表示到执行的转换是权限放大的关键点，但传统同像语言和分阶段语言中eval是未受管控的原语。现有系统缺乏对此转换的结构化治理机制。

Method: 提出受控元编程设计：将程序表示（机器形式）作为一等值，形式操作是纯计算，而具体化（形式到可执行机器的转换）是受控效应。设计了两个判断规则——纯形式求值（不产生指令）和受控具体化（产生一条受控指令），并实现了形式操作纯性、无旁路定理和边界保持三个性质的形式化证明。

Result: 在MashinTalk DSL中实现该设计（编译为BEAM字节码），集成了454个现有机器检查的Rocq定理，证明了形式操作的纯性、禁止绕过治理机制的能力，以及系统边界的一致性保持。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将eval从语言原语重新分类为受控效应，为AI系统中动态代码执行提供了形式化治理框架，确保代码生成与执行之间的转换受结构性检查和策略约束。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly synthesize executable structure at runtime: LLMs generate programs, agents construct workflows,self-improving systems modify their own behavior. In classical homoiconic and staged languages, the transition from coderepresentation to execution is unrestricted. eval is a language primitive, not a governed operation. We argue that ingovernedintelligent systems, this transition is an authority amplification: it converts symbolic structure into executableauthority andmust be mediated like any other effect. We present governed metaprogramming, a language design where programrepresentations(machine forms) are first-class values, form manipulation is pure computation, and materialization (the transition fromform toexecutable machine) is a governed effect subject to structural inspection. The governance system analyzes the proposedprogram'scapability requirements, policy compliance, and resource estimates before permitting execution. We formalize twojudgments: pureform evaluation (which emits no directives) and governed materialization (which emits exactly one governed directive). Weprovethree properties: purity of form manipulation, the no-bypass theorem, and boundary preservation. We implement the designinMashinTalk, a DSL for AI workflows compiling to BEAM bytecode, and report on integration with 454 existingmachine-checked Rocqtheorems. The central contribution is reclassifying eval from a language primitive into a governed effect.

</details>


### [47] [Beyond BLEU: A Semantic Evaluation Method for Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05282)
*Julius Näumann,Sven Keidel,Amir Molzam Sharifloo,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义等价性的代码翻译评估方法，并证明BLEU等语法指标与语义正确性相关性极低，LLM方法在反编译任务中显著优于启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码翻译评估指标（如BLEU）仅衡量句法相似性，忽略了程序语义，无法正确评估翻译结果的正确性。

Method: 将编译器测试方法论应用于新领域，提出语义正确性得分（定义为产生正确执行结果的翻译比例），并通过评估基于LLM和启发式反编译器进行应用验证。

Result: LLM方法显著优于启发式方法；BLEU得分与语义正确性之间相关性极弱（r = -0.127 ~ 0.354）。

Conclusion: 语法指标无法预测代码翻译的功能正确性，语义等价性评估才是更可靠的评价方式。

Abstract: Code translation is one of the core capabilities of LLMs. However, evaluating the correctness of translations remains difficult, as commonly used metrics such as BLEU measure only syntactic similarity, disregarding program semantics. We propose a novel evaluation methodology for code translation tasks, emphasizing semantic equivalence over surface-level string similarity. Our approach applies established compiler testing methodology to a new domain, allowing the assessment of an LLM fine-tuned for binary lifting tasks (i.e. decompiling binaries to higher-level representations). We introduce a semantic correctness score, defined as the proportion of translations that produce correct execution outcomes, and demonstrate its application by evaluating LLM-based and heuristic decompilers. Our findings show that LLM-based approaches significantly outperform heuristic ones, while BLEU scores show negligible correlation with semantic correctness (r = -0.127 to 0.354), demonstrating that syntactic metrics fail to predict functional accuracy.

</details>

### [48] [Automated Population-Level Audit Assurance via AI-Based Document Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05252)
*Santosh Vasudevan,Velu Natarajan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI文档智能的自动化审计交易测试框架，利用Snowflake Document AI从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据，并与权威数据源进行大规模对账，实现从抽样测试到全量测试的转变，提升审计覆盖率和持续审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工抽样审计方式处理非结构化PDF对账单工作量大、效率低，无法扩展到百万级交易规模，亟需一种自动化、可扩展的大规模审计交易测试方案。

Method: 采用Snowflake Document AI技术，仅需约20份文档的小规模标注语料即可训练模型，从非结构化PDF对账单中提取结构化数据，随后与权威数据源进行自动化对账以识别差异，并通过交互式仪表盘和自动化报告呈现结果。

Result: 实现了从抽样测试到总体层面（population-level）的全量测试，显著提高了审计覆盖率；支持近实时的风险识别和持续审计目标。

Conclusion: 结合文档智能与数据分析驱动的审计框架，能够实现可扩展、近实时的风险识别和持续保证，为大规模审计交易测试提供了行之有效的自动化方案。

Abstract: Audit transaction testing validates accuracy and completeness of customer-facing statements against internal systems of record. Traditional manual, sample-based review of unstructured PDF statements is labor-intensive and does not scale to millions of transactions. This paper presents an automated framework for large-scale audit transaction testing using AI-based document intelligence. The solution leverages Snowflake Document AI to extract structured data from unstructured PDF statements using a small labeled corpus (approximately 20 documents). Extracted data are reconciled against authoritative source-of-truth datasets to identify discrepancies at scale. Results are surfaced through interactive dashboards and automated reports. The framework enables population-level testing rather than sampling-based approaches, improving audit coverage and supporting continuous assurance objectives. Recent advances in document intelligence and analytics-driven audit frameworks enable scalable, near real-time risk identification and continuous assurance.

</details>


### [49] [Teaching LLMs Program Semantics via Symbolic Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06184)
*Jonas Bayer,Stefan Zetzsche,Olivier Bouissou,Remi Delmas,Michael Tautschnig,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SV-COMP 2025的500个C语言验证任务评估框架，发现现有模型在违规检测上存在严重缺陷。通过在通用开源C代码上运行Soteria符号执行引擎生成约3,000条bug轨迹，对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练，结合思维链推理，将违规检测准确率提升17个百分点以上，使8B模型在违规检测上超越4倍大的Qwen3-32B（无思维链）。轨迹训练与思维链的协同效应呈超加性，且改进跨所有五种属性类型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在代码验证任务中高总体准确率掩盖了违规检测能力严重不足的问题，且检测效果随程序长度急剧下降。亟需弥合这一能力差距。

Method: 构建500个C语言验证任务的评估框架（涵盖内存安全、溢出、终止、可达性、数据竞争五类属性）；在通用开源C代码上运行Soteria符号执行引擎生成形式化验证轨迹；利用轨迹对Qwen3-8B进行持续预训练；在推理阶段结合思维链推理。

Result: 仅约3,000条bug轨迹结合思维链推理使违规检测提升17个百分点以上；8B模型在违规检测上超越无思维链的Qwen3-32B，总体准确率接近后者；轨迹训练与思维链呈超加性协同；改进跨全部五种属性类型泛化；效果源于轨迹语义而非代码量，且轨迹格式与筛选至关重要。

Conclusion: 形式化验证轨迹的持续预训练与思维链推理的结合能有效提升代码验证模型的违规检测能力，实现更平衡的准确率分布，且具备跨属性类型的泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce an evaluation framework of 500 C verification tasks across five property types (memory safety, overflow, termination, reachability, data races) built on SV-COMP 2025, and evaluate 14 models across six families. We find that high overall accuracy masks a critical weakness: while most models reliably confirm properties hold, violation detection varies widely and degrades sharply with program length. To close this gap, we train on formal verification artifacts: running the Soteria symbolic execution engine on generic open-source C code and using the resulting traces for continued pretraining of Qwen3-8B. Just ${\sim}$3,000 bug traces combined with chain-of-thought reasoning at inference time improve violation detection by over 17 percentage points, producing one of the most balanced accuracy profiles among evaluated models. On violation detection, the trained 8B model outperforms the 4$\times$ larger Qwen3-32B without thinking and approaches it in overall accuracy. The interaction between trace training and chain-of-thought is superadditive: neither alone provides meaningful gains, but their combination does. Improvements transfer across all five property types, including ones the training traces do not target. Our 28 configurations confirm the gains stem from trace semantics, not code volume, and that trace curation and format matter.

</details>


### [50] [PARNESS: A Paper Harness for End-to-End Automated Scientific Research with Dynamic Workflows, Full-Text Indexing, and Cross-Run Knowledge Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05258)
*Yuchen Wang,Zhongzhi Luan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PARNESS 是一个开源框架，通过四层设计——DAG调度内核、全文PDF解析、知识图谱索引和智能体扩展接口——解决了现有自主科研系统工作流僵化的问题，支持跨学科、跨轮次的动态科研过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主科研系统（如AI-Scientist、PaperOrchestra等）在框架层面固定了控制流形状（线性流水线、状态机、单智能体循环或固定配方技能包），无法适应科研工作流的动态性和学科特异性。论文归纳了五个根源：工作流动态且学科特异、跨领域创意受限于LLM上下文、摘要视角遗漏论文正文、论文与代码库链接被忽视、缺乏跨轮次持久化知识检索工具。

Method: PARNESS 包含四个设计：(i) 基于DAG的轻量内核，通过四字段Agent契约将调度与领域语义解耦，支持用户编辑YAML表达任意学科工作流；(ii) 全文PDF解析与文献库子系统，将正文、图表解析为类型化对象，支持摘要回退；(iii) 知识图谱索引覆盖论文、想法、实验和代码仓库，支持场景类型检索（相似/矛盾/跨领域/反直觉）；(iv) 轻量扩展接口，允许Claude Code、Cursor等编码智能体添加或替换模块。

Result: PARNESS 是首个结合声明式流水线、全文PDF与代码仓库索引、跨轮次知识检索的开源系统。代码已开源在 GitHub。

Conclusion: 通过解耦调度与语义、深度索引文献与代码、以及跨轮次知识图谱检索，PARNESS 为自主科研系统提供了更灵活、可扩展的框架基础，有望推动多学科自动化科研的发展。

Abstract: Recent autonomous research systems -- AI-Scientist, PaperOrchestra, AutoSOTA, DeepResearch, InternAgent, ResearchAgent and others -- show LLM agents can ideate, run experiments and write papers, but each fixes a particular control-flow shape (linear pipeline, state machine, single-agent loop, or fixed-recipe skill pack) at the framework level. We argue this rigidity has five roots: (1) workflows are dynamic and discipline-specific (lab work, surveys, simulations, theory all loop differently); (2) ideation is bounded by LLM context and cross-domain ideation needs knowledge a single context cannot hold; (3) summary-only views miss the paper body, yet full-text access is uneven, so the cumulative corpus must do the work; (4) a paper's open-source repository is often the only complete specification of its experimental scheme, but the paper-to-code link is neglected; (5) no tool persists cross-run knowledge retrievably into a finite LLM context.
  We present PARNESS, an open-source framework built on four design moves. (i) A thin DAG kernel with a four-field Agent contract decouples scheduling from domain semantics, so any discipline's loop is expressible as user-editable YAML. (ii) A full-text PDF-parsing and literature-library subsystem indexes paper bodies, figures and tables as typed objects, with graceful abstract-only fall-back. (iii) A knowledge-graph index over papers, ideas, experiments and code repositories, with scenario-typed retrieval (similar / contradictory / cross-domain / counter-intuitive), surfaces a focused slice into each LLM call. (iv) A small extension surface lets any modern coding agent (Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot, OpenCode) add or replace any module. To our knowledge PARNESS is the first open-source system combining declarative pipelines, full-PDF and code-repository indexing, and cross-run knowledge. Source: https://github.com/gtrhythm/PARNESS

</details>


### [51] [Bridging Generation and Training: A Systematic Review of Quality Issues in LLMs for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05267)
*Kaifeng He,Xiaojun Zhang,Peiliang Cai,Mingwei Liu,Yanlin Wang,Chong Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过114篇研究的系统文献综述，建立了训练数据质量问题与LLM生成代码质量问题之间的因果映射框架，提出了涵盖9个生成代码质量维度和代码/非代码数据属性的统一分类法，总结了18种典型传播机制及相应的检测缓解技术，揭示了从被动后过滤到主动数据驱动治理的方法论转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成中频繁产生缺陷（从逻辑错误到安全漏洞），现有研究多将其视为模型层面的局限，但实证证据表明根因在于训练语料的质量问题。然而，训练数据质量与生成代码质量之间的具体传播机制尚未被系统映射，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解这一因果链条。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，筛选了114篇主要研究，构建了统一分类法（9维生成代码质量维度 + 代码/非代码属性分类），形式化定义了18种典型传播映射机制，并综合梳理了数据、模型和生成生命周期各阶段的检测与缓解技术。

Result: 建立了训练数据质量缺陷→生成代码质量缺陷的因果传播框架；揭示了质量保证从反应式、启发式的后生成过滤向主动式、数据驱动的治理与闭环修复转变的方法论趋势；总结了各生命周期阶段的检测与缓解技术现状。

Conclusion: 代码生成质量保证正从模型中心走向数据中心，未来需要整合数据治理与持续评估来构建可靠的代码LLM；同时指出了当前面临的开放挑战和未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate defective outputs in code generation tasks, ranging from logical bugs to security vulnerabilities. While these generation failures are often treated as model-level limitations, empirical evidence increasingly traces their root causes to imperfections within the training corpora. Yet, the specific mechanisms linking training data quality issues to generated code quality issues remain largely unmapped. This paper presents a systematic literature review of 114 primary studies to investigate how training data quality issues propagate into code generation. We establish a unified taxonomy that categorizes generated code quality issues across nine dimensions and training data quality issues into code and non-code attributes. Based on this taxonomy, we formalize a causal framework detailing 18 typical propagation mapping mechanisms. Furthermore, we synthesize state-of-the-art detection and mitigation techniques across the data, model, and generation lifecycles. The reviewed literature reveals a clear methodological shift: quality assurance is transitioning from reactive, heuristic-based post-generation filtering toward proactive, data-centric governance and closed-loop repair. Finally, we identify open challenges and outline research directions for developing reliable LLMs for code through integrated data curation and continuous evaluation. Our repository is available at https://github.com/SYSUSELab/From-Data-to-Code.

</details>


### [52] [Is this Build Failure Related to my Patch? An Empirical Study of Unrelated Build Failures in Continuous Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05564)
*Andie Huang,Daniel Alencar da Costa,Grant Dick,Mariam El Mezouar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文研究了开源项目中持续集成构建失败与代码推送不相关的问题，通过对77,354次构建失败的分析发现，开发者平均花费4小时判断失败是否与自身推送相关。基于371个确认的不相关失败案例，论文提取33个特征并使用正无标记学习（PU Learning）进行预测，在7个Apache项目上取得了0.70-0.88的精确率和0.63-0.97的AUC值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI系统中大量构建并发运行，合法的构建失败可能并非由触发该构建的代码推送引起，开发者需要花费大量时间（中位数4小时）判断失败是否与自己的变更相关，这种不相关的构建失败浪费了开发者的精力。

Method: 对7个Apache开源项目的77,354次CI构建失败进行实证研究；对371个确认不相关的构建失败进行文档分析；从问题报告、问题评论和提交记录中提取33个特征；使用半监督正无标记学习（PU Learning）构建预测模型。

Result: 不相关的测试失败占开发者归类为不相关构建失败的20%；PU学习模型在7个Apache项目上精确率0.70-0.88，召回率0.30-1.00，F1分数0.44-0.91，AUC值0.63-0.97；CI延迟、重复错误消息和前置评论数量是预测不相关构建失败的重要特征。

Conclusion: PU学习能够有效帮助开发者识别那些不太可能由当前推送引起的构建失败，从而减少开发者排查不相关失败的时间开销。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) systems often run many builds concurrently. In this setting, a legitimate build failure may not be caused by the code push that triggered it. Such unrelated build failures can waste developer effort because developers must determine whether the failure is actionable for their current change. We study 77,354 CI build failures from seven open source Apache projects to understand and predict unrelated build failures. We find that developers spend a median of 4 hours identifying whether a failure is related or unrelated to their push. We also perform a document analysis of 371 confirmed unrelated build failures sampled from 10,316 potentially unrelated failures. The analysis shows that unrelated test failures account for 20% of the cases in which developers classify build failures as unrelated. To predict unrelated build failures, we extract 33 features from issue reports, issue comments, and commits associated with the triggering push. We build semi-supervised Positive and Unlabeled (PU) learning models for seven Apache projects. The models achieve precision from 0.70 to 0.88, recall from 0.30 to 1.00, F1-score from 0.44 to 0.91, and AUC from 0.63 to 0.97. Feature importance analysis shows that CI latency, repeated error messages, and the number of preceding comments are useful indicators of unrelated build failures. These results show that PU learning can help developers identify build failures that are unlikely to be caused by their current push.

</details>


### [53] [An Empirical Study of Proactive Coding Assistants in Real-World Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05700)
*Lehui Li,Ruixuan Jia,Guo-Ye Yang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究发现，基于LLM模拟的IDE痕迹数据与实际开发者行为在多样性、时间结构和探索模式上存在显著差异，现有模拟评估会高估真实性能。研究构建了ProCodeBench基准，并证明模拟数据无法替代真实数据，但可作为微调前的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式编码助手的研究受限于缺乏大规模真实开发者行为数据，现有工作常依赖LLM模拟的IDE痕迹数据，但其对真实开发行为的保真度尚不明确。本文旨在系统性地探究模拟与真实之间的差距。

Method: 1) 通过自定义VS Code扩展收集1,246名行业开发者连续三天的真实IDE交互痕迹；2) 构建配对的LLM模拟痕迹作为对照；3) 分析两者在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式上的差异；4) 基于真实数据构建ProCodeBench基准；5) 评估多种LLM、检索增强方法和智能体基线；6) 开展训练实验比较模拟与真实数据的效果。

Result: 1) 模拟痕迹与真实痕迹在行为多样性、时间结构和探索模式上存在显著差异；2) 当前方法在真实IDE痕迹上表现远不可靠，模拟评估会高估真实性能；3) 训练实验表明模拟数据无法替代真实数据，但可在真实微调前作为补充。

Conclusion: 真实开发者行为数据对于评估和训练主动式编码助手至关重要，模拟数据的局限性不容忽视，但可将其作为真实数据微调前的预训练补充。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based coding assistants have made substantial progress, yet most systems remain reactive, requiring developers to explicitly formulate their needs. Proactive coding assistants aim to infer latent developer intent from integrated development environment (IDE) interactions and repository context, thereby reducing interaction overhead and supporting more seamless assistance. However, research in this direction is limited by the scarcity of large-scale real-world developer behavior data. Existing studies therefore often rely on LLM-simulated IDE traces, whose fidelity to real development behavior remains unclear. In this paper, we investigate this simulation-to-reality gap through a large-scale empirical study. We collect real IDE interaction traces from 1{,}246 experienced industry developers over three consecutive days using a custom Visual Studio Code extension, and construct paired LLM-simulated traces for controlled comparison. Our analysis shows that simulated traces differ substantially from real traces in behavioral diversity, temporal structure, and exploratory patterns. Based on the collected data, we introduce \textbf{ProCodeBench}, a real-world benchmark for proactive intent prediction. Experiments with representative LLMs, retrieval-augmented methods, and agentic baselines show that current approaches remain far from reliable under real IDE traces, suggesting that simulation-based evaluation can overestimate real-world performance. Finally, our training study shows that simulated data cannot replace real data, but can complement it when used before real-world fine-tuning. These findings highlight the importance of real developer behavior data for evaluating and training proactive coding assistants.

</details>


### [54] [On Fixing Insecure AI-Generated Code through Model Fine-Tuning and Prompting Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05867)
*Ali Soltanian Fard Jahromi,Amjed Tahir,Peng Liang,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了加固AI生成代码安全性的策略（微调与提示工程），发现安全改进高度依赖策略和模型，且修复漏洞时常引入新的弱点，不存在通用的“防弹”解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成代码的安全问题是其广泛采用的主要障碍，现有缓解策略的有效性在不同模型和设置下差异很大，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 基于常见弱点枚举（CWE）列表，采用微调（fine-tuning）和提示工程（prompting）两种方法对模型输出进行优化，系统评估这些策略在不同模型和编程语言下对安全性的提升效果，并分析弱点的严重性、共现性及修复的副作用。

Result: 安全改进高度依赖具体策略和模型；某些方法虽能减少特定类别的弱点，但常因修复引入新的弱点；没有一种策略能在所有模型和场景下始终消除弱点。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍有效的“防弹”解决方案来保证AI生成代码的安全性，需要针对具体模型和场景选择合适的加固策略。

Abstract: The security of AI-generated code remains a major obstacle to its widespread adoption. Although code generation models achieve strong performance on functional benchmarks, their outputs frequently contain bugs and security weaknesses that undermine their trustworthiness. Prior work has explored a range of approaches to mitigate security issues in AI-generated code, e.g., using static analysis-guided generation and prompt engineering. However, their effectiveness varies widely across models and settings. This paper presents a systematic investigation of strategies for hardening model-generated code against a list of Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE). We assess the extent to which these strategies improve security across models and programming languages, using fine-tuning and prompting approaches for model output refinement. Beyond the prevalence of security weaknesses, we analyse the severity of identified CWEs, their co-occurrence, and the unintended consequences of remediation (i.e., whether fixing certain weaknesses introduces new weaknesses elsewhere in the same code). Our results show that security improvements are highly strategy- and model-dependent. Although some approaches reduce specific classes of weaknesses, they often introduce new weaknesses as side effects of the fixes. Moreover, no strategy consistently eliminates weaknesses across all models and scenarios, highlighting the absence of a universally effective "bulletproof" solution for secure AI-generated code.

</details>


### [55] [Evaluating Non-English Developer Support in Machine Learning for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05902)
*Jonathan Katzy,Yongcheng Huang,Gopal-Raj Panchu,Maksym Ziemlewski,Paris Loizides,Sander Vermeulen,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言代码注释生成中，非英语场景下生成质量显著下降（语言错误增加高达15.1倍），且现有自动评估方法（包括神经指标和LLM-as-a-judge）均无法可靠检测非英语注释中的错误，人类判断仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码生成与评估主要集中于英语，对非英语（多语言开发环境）场景下的模型表现和评估可靠性缺乏系统理解，存在重大研究空白。

Method: 评估5种代码LLM（CodeGemma、CodeLlama、CodeQwen1.5、GraniteCode、StarCoder2）在5种自然语言（荷兰语、英语、希腊语、波兰语、中文）上的注释生成；对12,500条生成注释进行开放编码研究，构建人工标注数据集和26种错误类型分类体系；基于人工标注评估神经指标和LLM-as-a-judge管线的性能。

Result: 非英语场景下生成性能显著下降，语言错误增加高达15.1倍，且出现大量不连贯生成和语义错误。所有自动评估方法（包括经典重叠指标、现成神经指标、扩展神经指标、LLM-as-a-judge）均无法提供可靠一致的评估；神经指标无法区分正确注释与错误输出甚至随机噪声，且倾向于高估非英语质量；LLM-as-a-judge与人工一致性最高，但无法可靠捕获重要的语言相关和语义错误。

Conclusion: 评估和生成是多语言工具化的关键障碍，人类判断在当前阶段仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used in software engineering, but both code generation and its evaluation remain predominantly English-centric. This leaves a major gap in our understanding of how well current tools support multilingual development, where code contains non-English natural language. In this paper, we investigate non-English code comment generation and the reliability of current methods for evaluating such outputs. We evaluate five code LLMs (CodeGemma, CodeLlama, CodeQwen1.5, GraniteCode, and StarCoder2) across five natural languages: Dutch, English, Greek, Polish and Chinese. We further conduct an open-coding study of 12,500 generated comments, from which we derive a publicly released human-annotated dataset and a taxonomy of 26 error types. We use these human annotations, to evaluate the performance of neural metrics, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.
  Our findings show that generative performance deteriorates substantially outside English, with linguistic errors increasing by up to 15.1$\times$, alongside frequent incoherent generations and a rise in semantic errors. More critically, we show that detecting errors in non-English comments underperforms. Across classical overlap-based metrics, off-the-shelf neural metrics, extended neural metrics using newer multilingual, language-specific, and code-specific models, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, no automatic approach provides reliable and consistent assessment. Neural metrics fail to distinguish correct comments from incorrect outputs or even random noise, and tend to overestimate quality in non-English settings. LLM-as-a-judge methods achieve the highest agreement with human annotations but fail to reliably capture important language-related and semantic errors. Overall, our results show that evaluation and generation are key barriers for multilingual tooling, and that human judgment remains indispensable.

</details>


### [56] [Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06111)
*Yujia Chen,Yang Ye,Xiao Chu,Yuchi Ma,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ASTOR是一个面向多任务代码强化学习的框架，通过任务效用驱动的数据调度和策略优化，在单一LLM上同时提升多个编码任务的性能，超越任务专用专家模型9.0%-9.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的RL方法虽能有效后训练LLM编码能力，但为每个任务部署独立专家模型成本高昂，而统一的多任务RL方法将所有编码任务等同对待，依赖固定数据课程和共享优化策略，限制了多任务训练效果。

Method: ASTOR围绕"任务效用"（捕捉各任务学习潜力与跨任务协同的信号）设计两个耦合模块：1) 分层效用路由数据调度模块，分层分配训练预算并优先选择信息量大的提示；2) 自适应效用校准策略优化模块，动态缩放每个任务的KL正则化，匹配各任务当前训练状态的更新约束。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的LLM和四个代表性编码任务上，ASTOR在单一模型上一致提升所有任务性能，超越最佳任务专用专家9.0%-9.5%，超越最强MTRL基线7.5%-12.8%。

Conclusion: ASTOR通过任务效用驱动的协调机制，有效解决了多任务代码RL训练中数据调度和策略优化的差异化需求，实现了单一模型在多个编码任务上的统一高性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards has proven effective at post-training LLMs for coding, yet deploying separate task-specific specialists incurs costs that scale with the number of tasks, motivating a unified multi-task RL (MTRL) approach. However, existing MTRL methods treat all coding tasks uniformly, relying on fixed data curricula under a shared optimization strategy, ultimately limiting the effectiveness of multi-task training. To address these limitations, we propose ASTOR, a multi-tASk code reinforcement learning framework via uTility-driven coORdination. Centered on task utility, a signal capturing each task learning potential and cross-task synergy, ASTOR comprises two coupled modules: 1) Hierarchical Utility-Routed Data Scheduling module hierarchically allocates training budget and prioritizes informative prompts, steering training toward the most valuable data and 2) Adaptive Utility-Calibrated Policy Optimization module dynamically scales per-task KL regularization, matching update constraints to each tasks current training state. Experiments on two widely-used LLMs across four representative coding tasks demonstrate that ASTOR consistently improves a single model across all tasks, outperforming the best task-specific specialist by 9.0%-9.5% and surpassing the strongest MTRL baseline by 7.5%-12.8%.

</details>


### [57] [Breaking, Stale, or Missing? Benchmarking Coding Agents on Project-Level Test Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06125)
*Ye Shang,Quanjun Zhang,Haichuan Hu,Chunrong Fang,Liang Xiao,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TEBench是首个项目级的测试演化基准，包含314个来自Defects4J项目的任务实例，涵盖三种测试演化类型（Test-Breaking、Test-Stale、Test-Missing）。评估显示现有工业智能体框架在识别F1分数上收敛于45.7%-49.4%，其中Test-Stale最具挑战性，揭示了当前方法依赖执行失败信号、缺乏语义推理的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试演化基准工作在方法级粒度且提供预配对输入，绕过了从完整项目中定位受影响测试的任务，且完全排除了编写新测试的需求。缺乏一个项目级、端到端的测试演化基准来评估系统自主识别受影响测试、确定新测试需求并生成对应测试补丁的能力。

Method: 通过四阶段流水线在Defects4J项目上构建TEBench：1) 选择代码变更提交；2) 识别受影响测试；3) 标注三种演化类型（Test-Breaking、Test-Stale、Test-Missing）；4) 生成开发者编写的ground truth。最终得到10个项目上的314个任务实例。评估了三种工业智能体框架（Claude Code、Codex CLI、OpenCode）的七种配置及六种基座模型，加上启发式基线。

Result: 所有七种配置在识别F1上收敛于45.7%-49.4%，表明存在共享的性能天花板。Test-Stale是最具挑战性的类型，平均F1约36%。在更新任务上，配置生成的测试修改可执行性高但与ground truth表面形式差异显著。轨迹分析揭示了反应式的"执行-失败-修复"循环，该方法对breaking测试有效但结构上无法处理stale或missing测试。

Conclusion: 当前测试演化方法存在根本性局限：过度依赖执行失败信号而缺乏主动语义推理，导致无法处理Test-Stale和Test-Missing类型。需要开发不依赖于执行信号的、基于语义理解的测试演化新方法。

Abstract: As production code evolves, the test suite must co-evolve to remain effective. Existing benchmarks for test evolution operate at method-level granularity with pre-paired inputs, bypassing the task of locating affected tests from the full project and excluding the need for new tests entirely. We present TEBench, the first project-level benchmark for test evolution. Given a project repository and a code-changing commit, TEBench requires systems to autonomously identify tests requiring modification, determine where new tests are needed, and produce the corresponding test patch. We construct TEBench through a four-stage pipeline over Defects4J projects, curating 314 task instances from 10 projects with developer-written ground truth. Each instance is annotated with one or more of three evolution types: Test-Breaking (tests that fail), Test-Stale (tests that pass but no longer meaningfully validate updated behavior), and Test-Missing (new tests needed for introduced behavior). We evaluate seven configurations spanning three industrial agent frameworks (Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode) and six base models, alongside a heuristic baseline. All seven configurations converge on an identification F1 of 45.7% to 49.4%, revealing a shared performance ceiling across both frameworks and base models. Test-Stale is the most challenging type, averaging F1 around 36%, since configurations rely on execution failure signals and lack proactive semantic reasoning. On the update task, configurations produce highly executable test modifications whose surface form diverges substantially from ground truth. Trajectory analysis reveals a reactive "execute-fail-fix" loop that succeeds for breaking tests but structurally cannot address stale or missing tests. TEBench is available at https://github.com/iSEngLab/TEBench with a leaderboard at https://tebench-leadership.vercel.app.

</details>


### [58] [BUILD-AND-FIND: An Effort-Aware Protocol for Evaluating Agent-Managed Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06136)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出BUILD-AND-FIND协议，用于评估生成代码仓库的意图可恢复性——即下游智能体能否从仓库中准确、高效地发现原始设计意图，而不仅仅是行为正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程智能体基准只关注生成代码的行为正确性，但仓库级工程已逐步由智能体管理（一个写仓库，后续的检查、审计、扩展）。生成的仓库不仅是任务答案，更是未来的沟通制品。即使强智能体满足可见行为目标，不同仓库在暴露设计意图的清晰度上可能存在差异。

Method: BUILD-AND-FIND协议包含两阶段：Builder根据隐藏的仓库规范创建代码库；Finder仅看到代码库和规范相关的多项选择题。协议分离行为正确性与制品侧意图可恢复性，报告恢复准确率、可重复性、实现覆盖率和检查工作量。设置Question-only和Spec-only对照实验，并通过审计区分遗漏声明与Finder失败。

Result: 在发布的high-prior任务包中，恢复准确率接近饱和，因此检查工作量和Finder特定效应成为主要比较指标。

Conclusion: BUILD-AND-FIND为评估代码仓库作为沟通制品的质量提供了新维度，超越了单纯的行为正确性测试。

Abstract: Most coding-agent benchmarks ask whether generated code behaves correctly. That remains essential, but repository-level engineering is increasingly agent-managed: one agent writes a repository, and later agents inspect, audit, or extend it as working context. In that setting, a generated repository is not only an answer to a task but also a communication artifact for future work. Even when strong agents nearly satisfy the visible behavioral objective, repositories can differ in how clearly they expose the intended behavior and design choices behind that behavior. We introduce BUILD-AND-FIND, a protocol for evaluating whether downstream agents can recover those intended choices from generated repositories, and how much inspection that recovery requires. For each task, a builder sees a hidden repository specification and creates a codebase; a finder sees only the codebase and a specification-traced multiple-choice question bank. The protocol separates behavioral correctness from artifact-side recovery and reports recovery accuracy, repeatability, implementation coverage, and inspection effort. Accuracy and stability act as gates: effort is interpreted only when recovery succeeds reliably. Among artifacts from which the same intent can be recovered, lower effort by the same finder suggests that the artifact makes that intent easier to locate. Question-only and spec-only controls quantify generic priors and specification access, while audits separate omitted claims from finder failures and check whether correct answers cite artifact evidence. In the released high-prior task pack, recovery accuracy is near saturation, so inspection effort and finder-specific effects provide the main panel-local comparison.

</details>


### [59] [SiblingRepair: Sibling-Based Multi-Hunk Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06209)
*Xinyu Liu,Jiayu Ren,Yusen Wang,Qi Xin,Xiaoyuan Xie,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SiblingRepair是一种基于LLM的多hunk自动程序修复技术，专门针对代码中相似错误（兄弟缺陷）的修复。它通过令牌和嵌入匹配搜索兄弟候选，利用LLM识别相关兄弟并采用同步修复和迭代修复两种策略生成一致性补丁，在Defects4J和GHRB基准上显著优于现有SOTA技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有兄弟修复技术Hercules存在三个主要局限：对兄弟代码位置和提交历史的强假设、基于AST的刚性兄弟匹配、以及基于模板的不灵活补丁生成，限制了其在实际场景中的适用性和修复效果。

Method: SiblingRepair方法包括：（1）基于令牌和嵌入的代码匹配搜索兄弟候选，不依赖失效测试覆盖或提交历史；（2）利用LLM识别与故障相关的兄弟；（3）两种互补策略生成一致补丁——同步修复（联合修复兄弟）和迭代修复（逐步分析候选构建补丁）；（4）保留早期可疑位置的优秀补丁，组合为通用多hunk补丁。

Result: 在Defects4J和GHRB基准上的评估表明，SiblingRepair显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多hunk修复技术，并展现了良好的修复效率、兄弟检测与修复组件的有效性，以及LLM数据泄漏对结果的有限影响。

Conclusion: SiblingRepair通过LLM驱动的语义理解克服了Hercules的局限性，为自动化兄弟修复和通用多hunk修复提供了更灵活有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Developers often make similar mistakes across code locations implementing related functionalities. These locations, called siblings, share similar issues and require similar fixes. Accurately identifying siblings and consistently repairing them are crucial for automated program repair. Hercules is a SOTA technique designed for sibling repair. However, it is limited by strong assumptions about sibling locations and commit-history availability, rigid AST-based sibling matching, and inflexible template-based patch generation. To address these limitations, we present SiblingRepair, a new LLM-based multi-hunk APR technique specialized for sibling repair. Starting from a suspicious location identified by spectrum-based fault localization, SiblingRepair searches for semantically related sibling candidates using token- and embedding-based code matching, without restricting discovery to failing-test coverage or commit history. It then uses an LLM to identify failure-relevant siblings and generate consistent patches through two complementary strategies: simultaneous repair, which jointly repairs siblings, and iterative repair, which progressively analyzes candidates for patch construction. SiblingRepair further preserves promising patches generated from earlier suspicious locations and combines them into generalized multi-hunk patches. We evaluate SiblingRepair on the Defects4J and GHRB benchmarks. The results show that SiblingRepair substantially outperforms SOTA multi-hunk repair techniques including Hercules. Our evaluation further demonstrates its repair efficiency, the effectiveness of its sibling detection and repair components, and limited impact of the LLM data leakage on the results. Overall, SiblingRepair advances automated sibling and general multi-hunk repair.

</details>


### [60] [Correct Code, Vulnerable Dependencies: A Large Scale Measurement Study of LLM-Specified Library Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06279)
*Chengjie Wang,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Tianyue Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文首次大规模研究了LLM生成Python代码中第三方库版本选择的安全与兼容性风险。通过对10个LLM在1000个任务上的评估，发现模型普遍倾向于指定存在已知CVE的版本（36.70%-55.70%），且多数漏洞在模型知识截止前已公开。版本选择存在系统性偏差，而非孤立错误。静态兼容率仅19.70%-63.20%，动态测试通过率仅6.49%-48.62%。外部锚定的版本约束可显著降低漏洞暴露和兼容性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码中第三方库版本选择可能带来安全与兼容性风险，但此前缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 构建PinTrace基准（1000个Stack Overflow编程任务），评估10个LLM的版本指定行为；分析CVE暴露、兼容性（静态依赖解析+动态测试）及版本选择偏差。

Result: 版本指定率26.83%-95.18%（直接提示）vs 6.45%-59.19%（清单文件）；36.70%-55.70%任务含已知CVE，其中62.75%-74.51%为严重或高危；72.27%-91.37%的CVE在模型知识截止前已公开；静态兼容率19.70%-63.20%；动态通过率6.49%-48.62%。

Conclusion: LLM版本选择是一个被忽视的、系统性的风险面，外部锚定的版本约束可缓解该问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now largely involved in software development workflows, and the code they generate routinely includes third-party library (TPL) imports annotated with specific version identifiers. These version choices can carry security and compatibility risks, yet they have not been systematically studied.
  We present the first large-scale measurement study of version-level risk in LLM-generated Python code, evaluating 10 LLMs on PinTrace, a curated benchmark of 1,000 Stack Overflow programming tasks. LLMs tend to specify version identifiers when directly prompted at 26.83%-95.18%, while down to 6.45%-59.19% in creating a manifest file directly. Among the specified versions, 36.70%-55.70% of tasks contain at least one known CVE, and 62.75%-74.51% of them carry Critical or High severity ratings. In 72.27%-91.37% of cases, the associated CVEs were publicly disclosed before the model's knowledge cutoff. The statistics show all models converge on the same small set of risky release versions, indicating a systemic bias rather than isolated model error. Static compatibility rates range from 19.70% to 63.20%, with installation failure as the dominant cause. The dynamic test cases confirm the pattern by 6.49%-48.62% pass rates. Further experiments confirm that these failures are attributable to version selection rather than code quality, and that externally anchored version constraints substantially reduce both vulnerability exposure and compatibility failures. Our findings reveal LLM version selection as a first-class, previously overlooked risk surface in LLM-based development. We disclosed these findings to the community of the evaluated models, and several confirmed the issue. All the code and dataset have been released for open science at https://github.com/dw763j/PinTrace.

</details>


### [61] [Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Backend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06445)
*Francesco Dente,Dario Satriani,Paolo Papotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM智能体在多文件后端代码生成中处理结构约束的能力，发现随着结构需求累积，智能体性能显著下降（即"约束衰减"），在约定密集型框架（如FastAPI、Django）中表现更差，数据层缺陷是主要错误根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试常忽略非功能性的结构约束（如架构模式、数据库映射等），奖励功能正确但结构随意的解决方案，缺乏对智能体在严格结构约束下生成生产级代码能力的系统评估。

Method: 固定统一的API契约，在80个绿地生成任务和20个功能实现任务（跨越8个Web框架）上进行实验，通过端到端行为测试和静态验证器的双重评估来隔离结构复杂度的影响。

Result: 发现"约束衰减"现象：结构需求累积导致性能大幅下降；强配置从基线到完全指定任务平均损失30个百分点的断言通过率；框架敏感性分析显示在约定密集型环境（FastAPI、Django）中表现显著较差；数据层缺陷是主要错误根因。

Conclusion: 同时满足功能需求和结构约束对编码智能体而言仍是一个关键的开放挑战。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents demonstrate strong performance in autonomous code generation under loose specifications. However, production-grade software requires strict adherence to structural constraints, such as architectural patterns, databases, and object-relational mappings. Existing benchmarks often overlook these non-functional requirements, rewarding functionally correct but structurally arbitrary solutions. We present a systematic study evaluating how well agents handle structural constraints in multi-file backend generation. By fixing a unified API contract across 80 greenfield generation tasks and 20 feature-implementation tasks spanning eight web frameworks, we isolate the effect of structural complexity using a dual evaluation with end-to-end behavioral tests and static verifiers. Our findings reveal a phenomenon of constraint decay: as structural requirements accumulate, agent performance exhibits a substantial decline. Capable configurations lose 30 points on average in assertion pass rates from baseline to fully specified tasks, while some weaker configurations approach zero. Framework sensitivity analysis exposes significant performance disparities: agents succeed in minimal, explicit frameworks (e.g., Flask) but perform substantially worse on average in convention-heavy environments (e.g., FastAPI, Django). Finally, error analysis identifies data-layer defects (e.g., incorrect query composition and ORM runtime violations) as the leading root causes. This work highlights that jointly satisfying functional and structural requirements remains a key open challenge for coding agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

### [62] [Making AI Drafts Count: A Quality Threshold in Audio Description Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05348)
*Lana Do,Shasta Ihorn,Charity M. Pitcher-Cooper,Sanjay Mirani,Gio Jung,Hyunjoo Shim,Zhenzhen Qin,Kien T. Nguyen,Vassilis Athitsos,Ilmi Yoon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过GenAD（遵循无障碍准则的AD生成流水线）和RefineAD（人工编辑界面）研究AI草稿质量对音频描述（AD）编辑过程的影响。实验表明，高质量AI草稿可将完成时间缩短一半以上并显著降低认知负荷，而低质量基线草稿收益有限，揭示了AI辅助存在最低质量阈值，且该阈值随视觉复杂度升高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI生成草稿的质量如何影响人工编辑过程，尤其是草稿质量与编辑效率、认知负荷之间的关系，以及是否存在有效AI辅助所需的最低质量阈值。

Method: 构建GenAD（结合无障碍指南和视频上下文信息的AD生成流水线）和RefineAD（支持文本、时间、交付方式编辑的界面）；进行被试内实验，对比从零创作与编辑不同质量AI草稿的效果。

Result: GenAD草稿将完成时间缩短一半以上并显著降低认知负荷；基线低质量草稿仅带来微弱收益；定性分析表明质量阈值具有内容依赖性，视觉复杂度越高所需草稿质量越高。

Conclusion: 有效的AI辅助应针对目标内容设定合适的质量阈值，而非简单提供AI草稿即可。提出了"质量阈值"设计原则。

Abstract: Audio description (AD) narrates visual elements in video for blind and low-vision audiences. Recent work has shown that giving novice describers an AI-generated draft to start from helps produce higher-quality AD and lowers the barrier to entry. What remains an open question is how draft quality shapes the editing process. We investigate this through GenAD, an AD generation pipeline that incorporates accessibility guidelines and contextual video information, and RefineAD, an editing interface for human revisions. Human-AI contributions are measured across text, timing, and delivery. In a within-subjects study, we compared authoring from scratch against editing AI drafts of varying quality. GenAD drafts cut completion time by more than half and significantly reduced cognitive load. In contrast, baseline drafts generated from simple, unguided prompts offered only modest benefits, pointing to a minimum quality threshold for effectiveness. Qualitative findings suggest this threshold is content-dependent; as visual complexity increases, so does the quality needed from AI drafts. We propose this as a design principle: effective AI assistance should clear a quality threshold suited to the target content, rather than simply be present.

</details>


### [63] [Every(bot) Makes Mistakes: Coding Big Five Personalities, Context, and Tone into an LLM Chatbot Recovery Code Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05391)
*Rachel Hill,Tom Owen,Julian Hough*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化的恢复代码，将LLM聊天机器人的任务上下文与大五人格特质、语气和三阶段恢复指令进行映射，用于提升人机交互中的错误恢复质量。实验表明，采用该编码训练的Claude Sonnet 4.6模型在恢复质量上比基线提升27.8%，其中适当性维度表现最佳（83.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别探索了LLM恢复中的语气、上下文和人格特质等设计维度，但缺乏将这些变量整合为结构化指导框架的工作，导致人机交互中的错误恢复缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 采用探索性方法论，设计恢复代码将四种常见LLM聊天任务上下文与大五人格特质（尽责性、宜人性、开放性、外向性）、语气及三阶段恢复指令进行映射；构建包含三个维度（恢复质量、语气对齐、适当性）和九个子维度的评估量规；采用组间设计，条件A为基线（无编码训练），条件B为编码训练，通过8个LLM评估器对恢复响应进行评分。

Result: 编码条件下恢复响应平均性能提升27.8%（76.7% vs 48.9%），适当性维度表现最强（83.3%），人格适当性显著提升（75% vs 50%），提供解释能力大幅提升（60% vs 20%）。

Conclusion: 结构化的人格、上下文和语气感知恢复代码能够被LLM聊天机器人成功学习和应用，从而在不同上下文任务中有效提升错误恢复质量。

Abstract: Despite careful design involving classifiers, parameters, and safeguarding, errors during human/AI interaction are not rare. Poor error recovery can disrupt interaction flow, damage user trust, and decrease user engagement. Whilst existing work has explored LLM recovery, tone, context, and personality as separate design dimensions, no existing work has combined these variables into a structured guidance framework. This paper presents a recovery code that maps four common LLM chatbot task contexts to associated personality traits (four Big Five personalities: Conscientiousness, Agreeableness, Openness, and Extraversion), tones, and three-stage recovery instructions. A recovery evaluation rubric was also designed, comprising three dimensions (Recovery quality, Tone alignment, and Appropriateness) and nine sub-dimensions. The methodology is exploratory, with no participants used. A between-subjects design was employed across two conditions: Condition A (baseline, uncoded), four separate Claude Sonnet 4.6 agents received no recovery code training; Condition B (coded), four separate Claude Sonnet 4.6 models were trained on the recovery code. Identical 'user' prompts and error scenarios were used across both conditions. Eight LLM evaluator agents assessed the recovery responses using the evaluation rubric, producing scores out of 5 for each sub-dimension.
