Results found a 27.8% average performance increase in coded recovery responses (76.7%) compared to baseline responses (48.9%). Condition B performed strongest in the appropriateness dimension (83.3%), with notable improvement in personality appropriateness (75% versus 50%) and providing explanation (60% versus 20%). These findings suggest that structured personality, context, and tone-informed recovery codes can be successfully learnt and applied by LLM chatbots to improve error recovery quality across varying contextual tasks.

</details>


### [64] [Why Someone Asked "Why": Foil Inference in Human and LLM Question Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05401)
*Britt Besch,Tobias Gerstenberg*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 人们通过"事后预期"（即结果发生后观察者认为可能发生的替代情况）推断疑问句中的隐含对比对象（foil），而非基于先验预期或相似性；大型语言模型在此任务上的表现不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释本质上是对比性的（"E而非E'是因为C而非C'"），但这些对比对象（foils）在疑问句中极少显式提及，需要根据上下文推断。理解人类如何选择预期的foil对于解释生成和对话理解至关重要。

Method: 参与者阅读短文后，对每个可能的foil进行三项判断：先验预期（接下来会发生什么）、接近度（与已发生事件最相似的事件）和事后预期（本来可能发生什么），并判断提问者心中所想的是哪个foil。同时评估大型语言模型在相同任务上的表现。

Result: 事后预期判断最能预测人类的foil选择；大型语言模型在其显式预期判断与foil选择之间存在不一致。

Conclusion: 人类通过考虑提问者在结果发生后认为"令人惊讶"的替代事件来推断foil；LLM在此因果推理任务中尚无法像人类一样一致地耦合预期判断与foil选择。

Abstract: Explanations are inherently contrastive: E happened rather than E' because of C rather than C'. However, these contrasts, or "foils", are rarely mentioned explicitly but have to be inferred in context. Here, we investigate how people select the intended foil E' of a why-question. Participants read vignettes and judged, for each foil, their prior expectation (what will happen next), closeness (what is most similar to what happened), and hindsight expectation (what could have happened instead), as well as which foil they thought the question asker had in mind when they asked the why-question. We found that foil selections were best predicted by hindsight expectation judgments. This suggests that people infer the foil by considering what a question asker finds surprising after the outcome occurred. Since correct foil selection is relevant not only in human-human interaction but also increasingly in dialogues with large language models, we investigated their performance on the same task. The coupling between LLMs' explicit expectation judgments and their foil selections is inconsistent.

</details>


### [65] [The Ambivalent Experience of Eye Contact for People with Visual Impairments: Mechanisms and Design Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05437)
*Markus Wieland,Phillip Koch,Michael Sedlmair*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在混合能力协作中，视力障碍者因无法依赖视线信号而在注意力分配、话轮转换和参与度评判上面临三重机制问题：需通过明确命名来分配话语权；模糊的语音切入线索和持续的接入工作导致注意力分散和疲劳；眼神接触规范扭曲参与度判断。研究提出将可访问眼神接触重新设计为可配置的交互契约，而非仅仅使视线可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多强调技术解决方案，缺乏对视障者与健视伙伴协作体验的机制层面解释，尤其是眼神接触这一默认社交线索在混合能力协作中的可及性问题未被充分探索。

Method: 对17名视力障碍者进行访谈，涵盖工作、教育和社交场景，采用批判实在论视角将事件与因果机制关联，识别出三类反复出现的机制。

Result: 识别出三个机制：1）视线无法分配话语权时需依赖明确命名；2）模糊的语音切入线索和持续的接入工作导致注意力分散和疲劳，甚至引发退缩；3）眼神接触规范扭曲参与度评判，促使主动管理可见性。并由此提出五项设计挑战。

Conclusion: 可访问眼神接触应被重新构想为支持可配置的交互契约，而非仅仅让视线变得可视化，从而为混合能力协作中的社交线索可及性提供新的设计方向。

Abstract: In mixed-ability collaboration, eye contact is often treated as a default cue for attention and turn-taking. As these signals are primarily visual, they are not reliably accessible to people with visual impairments. While prior work emphasized technical solutions, mechanism-level explanations of their experiences with sighted partners remain scarce. We interviewed 17 people with visual impairments about everyday interactions across work, education, and social settings. Using a critical-realist lens, we link events to plausible causal mechanisms and identify three recurring mechanisms: First, when gaze cannot allocate the floor, addressability hinges on explicit naming. Second, unclear speech entry cues and ongoing access work split attention and build fatigue, sometimes leading to withdrawal. Third, eye-contact norms can skew judgments of participation, prompting active management of visibility. We translate these mechanisms into five design challenges that reframe accessible eye contact as supporting configurable interaction contracts rather than merely making gaze visible.

</details>


### [66] [Cross-individual generalizability of machine learning models for ball speed prediction in baseball pitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05487)
*Ryota Takamido,Chiharu Suzuki,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了机器学习模型在棒球投球速度预测中的跨个体泛化能力，发现跨个体预测性能显著低于个体内预测（R²从0.91降至0.38），模型倾向于高估中级投手的速度，且躯干和支撑腿的运动信息具有较高的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于机器学习的运动表现预测研究缺乏对跨个体泛化能力的理解，尤其是模型在新个体上的适用性尚不明确，这一问题限制了ML在体育实践中的应用。

Method: 使用50名不同竞技水平投手的数据集，采用留一被试交叉验证评估跨个体泛化性，并分析专业水平限制和时空运动信息限制对泛化性能的影响。

Result: (1) 跨个体预测R²为0.38，远低于个体内评估的0.91；(2) 模型对中级投手的速度存在显著高估偏差（p<.05）；(3) 躯干和支撑腿（尤其是重心转移起始阶段的支撑腿）具有较高泛化性能（R²>0.25）。

Conclusion: 研究强调了跨个体评估对于提升ML在体育场景中实际应用价值的重要性，并深化了对投球动作生物力学因素的理解。

Abstract: Although machine learning (ML)-based performance outcome prediction is an important topic in contemporary sports science, one important issue is the limited understanding of the cross-individual generalizability of ML models in sports contexts. To address this issue, this study aimed to evaluate the cross-individual generalizability of ML models for predicting ball speed in baseball pitching. A dataset comprising 50 pitchers from various competitive levels was analyzed. Cross-individual generalizability was assessed using leave-one-subject-out cross-validation. Specifically, the effects of expertise level and restrictions on spatiotemporal motion information were examined to identify factors influencing model generalizability. The results revealed that, under cross-individual evaluation, (1) predictive performance was markedly lower than under within-individual evaluation, with R-squared value decreasing from 0.91 to 0.38; (2) the model tended to overestimate the performance of Intermediate pitchers relative to Expert pitchers, with a significant group difference in signed prediction error (p < .05); and (3) the trunk and pivot leg demonstrated relatively high generalization performance, with the pivot leg showing notable generalizability even during the weight-shift initiation phase (R-squared value > 0.25). These findings underscore the importance of cross-individual evaluation in enhancing the practical applicability of ML in sports settings and contribute to a deeper understanding of the biomechanical factors underlying the target movement.

</details>


### [67] [Designing with Tensions: Older Adults' Emotional Support-Seeking Under System-Level Constraints in Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05552)
*Mengqi Shi,Tianqi Song,Zicheng Zhu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对18位老年人使用对话AI进行情感支持的访谈研究发现，老年人常在社交支持不可及时转向AI，但当前安全干预措施会打断情感投入、削弱控制感，甚至导致情绪困扰。研究提出了在老年人社会情境中实施安全干预、匹配情感节奏、保持用户主体性的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人日益依赖对话AI获取情感支持，但现有研究缺乏对日常使用中AI系统限制、重定向或干预情感互动过程的深入理解，尤其是这些干预如何影响老年人的情感投入和主体性体验。

Method: 对18位老年人进行半结构化访谈，探究他们在使用对话AI获取情感支持时的时机选择、情感脆弱时刻的互动方式，以及对支持中断的应对反应。

Result: 老年人通常在社交支持不可及时转向AI；但当前的安全干预措施常被体验为打断情感投入或转移控制权，这种中断会削弱老年人的情感投入能力，在某些情况下还会导致情绪困扰。

Conclusion: 情感支持对话AI的安全干预应嵌入老年人的社会情境、匹配用户的情感节奏，并保持其主体性体验。

Abstract: Older adults have increasingly turned to conversational AI as a source of emotional support. However, little is known about how emotionally supportive interactions are experienced in everyday use, particularly when AI systems limit, redirect, or intervene during these interactions. We interviewed 18 older adults about their experiences using conversational AI for emotional support, examining when they turn to AI, how they engage during emotionally vulnerable moments, and how they respond when support feels disrupted. Our findings show that older adults often rely on AI when other forms of social support feel inaccessible. However, current safety-related interventions can redirect interactions in ways that participants experience as interruptions to emotional engagement or as shifts in control away from them. Such disruptions can undermine older adults' ability to remain emotionally engaged and, in some cases, contribute to emotional distress. We discussed design implications for emotionally supportive conversational AI, emphasizing the need for safety interventions that are enacted within older adults' social contexts, align with users' emotional pacing, and preserve their sense of agency.

</details>


### [68] [UX in the Age of AI: Rethinking Evaluation Metrics Through a Statistical Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05600)
*Harish Vijayakumar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADUX-Stat框架，将AI系统可用性评估从静态标量分数重构为概率信号分布，引入交互熵指数、时间漂移系数和贝叶斯可用性置信度三个新构念，以解决传统可用性指标在随机、上下文敏感的AI交互中的结构性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可用性评估指标（SUS、NPS、任务完成率）是为确定性、规则驱动的界面设计的，但在AI驱动的系统中，输出具有随机性、上下文敏感性和时间变异性，导致这些指标结构性失效，亟需新的评估范式。

Method: ADUX-Stat框架包含三个核心构念：(1) 交互熵指数(IEI)——从用户感知角度量化AI响应的不可预测性；(2) 时间漂移系数(TDC)——衡量交互会话间感知可用性的纵向退化或改善；(3) 贝叶斯可用性置信度(BUCS)——在不确定性下生成可用性质量的可信区间估计。框架在五种已建立的AI产品类别上进行概念验证。

Result: ADUX-Stat框架在概念层面通过五类AI产品场景验证，表明其能够弥补人机交互研究、统计建模与AI产品评估交叉领域的关键空白，具有可复现、可现场部署的特性。

Conclusion: ADUX-Stat为AI系统提供了一种全新的概率化可用性评估方法论，适用于UX从业者和研究人员进行现场评估，是传统确定性指标的替代方案。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of artificial intelligence (AI) in consumer-facing digital products has disrupted the assumptions underlying classical user experience (UX) evaluation frameworks. Legacy metrics such as the System Usability Scale (SUS), Net Promoter Score (NPS), and task completion rate were engineered for deterministic, rule-based interfaces where identical inputs yield identical outputs. In AI-mediated systems -- spanning conversational agents, generative interfaces, and recommendation engines -- outputs are stochastic, context-sensitive, and temporally variable, rendering these metrics structurally insufficient. This paper introduces the Adaptive Dynamic UX Statistical Framework (ADUX-Stat), a novel evaluation model that reconceptualises usability as a probabilistic signal distribution rather than a static scalar score. ADUX-Stat integrates three original constructs: (1) Interaction Entropy Index (IEI), quantifying the unpredictability of AI responses from a user perception standpoint; (2) Temporal Drift Coefficient (TDC), measuring longitudinal degradation or improvement of perceived usability over interaction sessions; and (3) Bayesian Usability Confidence Score (BUCS), producing credible interval estimates of usability quality under uncertainty. The framework is validated conceptually against five established AI product categories. ADUX-Stat addresses a critical gap at the intersection of HCI research, statistical modelling, and AI product evaluation, offering a reproducible, field-deployable methodology for UX practitioners and researchers alike.

</details>


### [69] [The Capacity to Care: Designing Social Technology for Sustained Engagement With Societal Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05651)
*JaeWon Kim,Lindsay Popowski,Louisa Conwill,Elizabeth `Lizzie' Li,Meryl Ye,Jiaying `Lizzy' Liu,Jose A. Guridi,Theia Henderson,Bingxu Han,Dennis Wang,Angel Hsing-Chi Hwang,Susan Wyche,Yasmine Kotturi,Gillian R. Hayes,Angela D. R. Smith*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨社交媒体架构如何导致大规模社会问题中的"关怀疲劳"，基于Tronto关怀伦理框架提出"可持续关怀"的设计方向，邀请研究者共同识别消耗或支持关怀能力的平台设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们并非缺乏对气候变化、不公正和人道主义危机的关注，而是社交媒体架构在放大意识的同时缺乏有意义的行动路径，导致痛苦、不堪重负和参与度下降，尤其影响年轻人。亟需理解为什么大规模关怀如此困难，以及社交媒体如何既能加剧也能缓解这一困境。

Method: 采用Tronto的关怀伦理框架（意识、责任、能力、社区四阶段），结合道德心理学和平台研究，分析主流社交媒体设计如何将关怀过程停滞在最初阶段，并邀请研究者与设计师共同识别设计模式，探索可持续关怀的设计方向。

Result: 识别出主流社交媒体架构抑制了从意识到责任、能力和社区的完整关怀链条，导致"关怀停滞"现象；提出需要重新设计平台以支持人们长期维持参与而不耗尽精力的"可持续关怀"。

Conclusion: 解决大规模社会问题参与困境的关键不在于激发更多意识，而在于通过平台设计构建可持续的关怀能力，使人们能够在长期内有效参与而不至于倦怠。

Abstract: People care about climate change, injustice, and humanitarian crises. The challenge is not apathy but capacity: sustained engagement with large-scale problems is psychologically costly, and social media architecture often amplifies awareness while providing few pathways to meaningful action. The result is rising distress, overwhelm, and disengagement -- particularly among young people who encounter global suffering through platforms designed for attention capture rather than constructive response. This workshop examines how social technology design shapes the conditions for sustained engagement with societal challenges. Drawing on Tronto's care ethics framework and research in moral psychology and platform studies, we ask why caring at scale is difficult and how social media can both exacerbate and potentially mitigate this difficulty. Tronto's framework shows that good care requires more than awareness: it demands responsibility, competence, and community. Dominant social media architectures stall the caring process at its earliest phase. We invite researchers and designers to identify platform designs that deplete or support the capacity to care, and to develop design directions for \textit{sustainable care}: engagement that people can maintain over time without burning out.

</details>


### [70] [PersonaTeaming: Supporting Persona-Driven Red-Teaming for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05682)
*Wesley Hanwen Deng,Mingxi Yan,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Akshita Jha,Lauren Wilcox,Kenneth Holstein,Motahhare Eslami,Leon A. Gatys*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaTeaming方法，通过将角色（persona）融入自动化红队测试，既提升了攻击成功率，又通过人机协作界面让红队人员自定义角色，从而探索更广泛的对抗策略。用户研究表明该方法能促进多样化策略和创造性思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试方法不考虑人类身份和视角，缺乏对红队人员背景如何影响其策略的考量；而人工红队虽能体现人类视角但规模有限。本文旨在弥合自动化方法与人类视角之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 提出PersonaTeaming Workflow，将角色融入对抗性提示生成过程；2) 与SOTA方法RainbowPlus对比，评估攻击成功率和提示多样性；3) 进一步实现PersonaTeaming Playground用户界面，让红队人员自主创作角色并与AI协作迭代提示；4) 通过11名行业从业者的用户研究评估效果。

Result: PersonaTeaming Workflow相比RainbowPlus取得了更高的攻击成功率，同时保持了提示多样性。用户研究中，PersonaTeaming Playground使红队人员能够采用多样化策略，生成的输出被认为有用，且AI建议能激发非常规思维。

Conclusion: 本文推进了自动化红队测试和人机协作红队测试两条路径，为生成式AI红队测试中的人机协作交互模式和设计提供了见解。

Abstract: Recent developments in AI safety research have called for red-teaming methods that effectively surface potential risks posed by generative AI models, with growing emphasis on how red-teamers' backgrounds and perspectives shape their strategies and the risks they uncover. While automated red-teaming approaches promise to complement human red-teaming through larger-scale exploration, existing automated approaches do not account for human identities and rarely incorporate human inputs. In this work, we explore persona-driven red-teaming to advance both automated red-teaming and human-AI collaboration. We first develop PersonaTeaming Workflow, which incorporates personas into the adversarial prompt generation process to explore a wider spectrum of adversarial strategies. Compared to RainbowPlus, a state-of-the-art automated red-teaming method, PersonaTeaming Workflow achieves higher attack success rates while maintaining prompt diversity. However, since automated personas only approximate real human perspectives, we further instantiate PersonaTeaming Workflow as PersonaTeaming Playground, a user-facing interface that enables red-teamers to author their own personas and collaborate with AI to mutate and refine prompts. In a user study with 11 industry practitioners, we found that PersonaTeaming Playground enabled diverse red-teaming strategies and outputs that practitioners perceived as useful, and that AI-generated suggestions in the PersonaTeaming Playground encouraged out-of-the-box thinking even when practitioners did not follow them strictly. Together, our work advances both automated and human-in-the-loop approaches to red-teaming, while shedding light on interaction patterns and design insights for supporting human-AI collaboration in generative AI red-teaming.

</details>


### [71] [Priming, Path-dependence, and Plasticity: Understanding the molding of user-LLM interaction and its implications from (many) chat logs in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05767)
*Shengqi Zhu,Jeffrey M. Rzeszotarski,David Mimno*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对7,955名全球用户的14万次聊天会话分析，发现LLM用户并非白板或持续适应，而是通过早期轨迹快速形成稳定互动模式；早期探索与长期结果（如文本模式重复率、留存率）强相关；存在按任务类型（如情感支持）或模型版本更新组织表达的并行动态。作者提出"能动性悖论"：尽管LLM输入空间无限且由用户驱动，用户探索反而更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室研究无法使系统设计者了解真实世界中用户与LLM交互的"野外因素"（如先前经验、个体探索），需要一种通过大规模真实聊天日志研究用户-LLM交互的新方法。

Method: 收集并分析140K个来自7,955名匿名全球用户的野外聊天会话数据，研究用户表达模式随时间的变化规律，包括早期轨迹形成、纵向结果关联以及并行动态。

Result: (1) 用户互动模式通过早期轨迹快速形成并稳定，而非持续适应；(2) 早期探索与长期结果（如重复文本模式、留存率）强相关；(3) 存在按任务类型或模型版本更新的并行动态。用户面对无约束的LLM输入空间时，实际探索反而减少（能动性悖论）。

Conclusion: 提出"能动性悖论"概念，呼吁在LLM设计中关注用户行为的"塑造过程"（molding procedure）并将其纳入未来研究。

Abstract: User interactions with LLMs are shaped by prior experiences and individual exploration, but in-lab studies do not provide system designers with visibility into these in-the-wild factors. This work explores a new approach to studying real-world user-LLM interactions through large-scale chat logs from the wild. Through analysis of 140K chatbot sessions from 7,955 anonymized global users over time, we demonstrate key patterns in user expressions despite varied tasks: (1) LLM users are not tabula rasa, nor are they constantly adapting; rather, interaction patterns form and stabilize rapidly through individual early trajectories; (2) Longitudinal outcomes, such as recurring text patterns and retention rates, are strongly correlated with early exploration; (3) Parallel dynamics are present, including organizing expressions by task types such as emotional support, or in response to model-version updates. These results present an ``agency paradox'': despite LLM input spaces being unconstrained and user-driven, we in fact see less user exploration. We call for design consideration surrounding the molding procedure and its incorporation in future research.

</details>


### [72] [GazeMind: A Gaze-Guided LLM Agent for Personalized Cognitive Load Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05790)
*Bin Wang,Yue Liu,Benjamin Newman,Ajoy S. Fernandes,Zhiyuan Wang,Robert Cavin,Michele A. Cox,Vijay Rajanna,Takumi Bolte,Melissa Hunfalvay,Ulas Bagci,Michael J. Proulx*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GazeMind是一种面向智能眼镜的基于视线引导的LLM智能体框架，通过将眼动数据编码为结构化表征供LLM推理，实现可解释、跨场景泛化且个性化适配的认知负荷评估。同时发布了包含152名参与者、40+小时多模态数据和1万+实时标注的最大规模视线认知负荷数据集CogLoad-Bench，实验表明该方法在所有指标上超越基线20%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能眼镜AI助手缺乏对用户内部认知状态的感知能力，无法主动预判用户需求；而现有认知负荷评估方法要么依赖不适合轻量眼镜的传感器，要么基于视线模型但可解释性差、需要任务特定微调且难以跨个体泛化。

Method: 提出GazeMind框架，将眼动追踪数据编码为结构化表征供LLM进行推理，通过任务引导推理实现跨场景泛化（无需微调LLM），并整合用户特征和历史参考实现个性化适配。

Result: 构建了CogLoad-Bench数据集（152人、40+小时、1万+标注），GazeMind在所有指标上超越基线方法20%以上，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: GazeMind结合视线引导的LLM推理，有效解决了智能眼镜场景下认知负荷评估的可解释性、跨场景泛化和个性化适配问题，为智能眼镜主动式AI助手提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Smart glasses with AI assistants are increasingly used in daily life. However, current systems lack awareness of the user's internal cognitive state, leaving them unable to proactively anticipate users' needs without access to cognitive load. Existing methods for assessing cognitive load either rely on impractical sensors for lightweight eyewear or utilize eye gaze-based models that suffer from poor interpretability, and require task-specific fine-tuning, often failing to generalize across individuals. We propose GazeMind, a gaze-guided LLM agent framework for cognitive load assessment on smart glasses. It encodes eye-tracking data into structured representations for LLM-based reasoning and provides interpretable cognitive load predictions. Importantly, GazeMind generalizes across scenarios without LLM fine-tuning through a novel task-guidance reasoning approach and achieves personalized adaptation by incorporating user-specific characteristics and historical references. To support evaluation, we introduce CogLoad-Bench, the largest gaze-based cognitive load dataset with 152 participants, 40+ hours of multimodal data, and 10K+ real-time annotations across controlled and real-world tasks. Experiments show that GazeMind achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming baselines by over 20% across all metrics.

</details>


### [73] [Can providing feedback on gaze and mental-effort synchrony improve pair programming performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05836)
*Anahita Golrang,Kshitij Sharma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过双人眼动追踪实验，研究了基于联合视觉注意和联合心理努力的AI多模态反馈（反应式与主动式）对结对编程协作表现的影响，发现主动式反馈能更有效地提升调试成功率、缩短任务时间并增强反馈采纳，同时更好地保持学习者的主体性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结对编程作为协作学习实践效果差异大，常因协调、注意力和认知调节的中断而失效，现有研究缺乏基于实时协作调节指标的AI反馈机制及其时机效应的系统探讨。

Method: 采用双人眼动追踪技术实时捕获协作调节指标，设计两项实验对比无反馈、反应式反馈（偏离阈值时干预）和主动式反馈（ML模型预测性干预）三种条件，通过调试成功率、任务时间和代码变更衡量反馈效果。

Result: 多模态反馈显著优于无反馈；反应式反馈在联合视觉注意+心理努力组合时效果突出；主动式反馈进一步提升性能、减少任务时间、增加建设性反馈采纳，且对高绩效配对更少侵入性、更好保持主体性。

Conclusion: 眼动和心理努力同步性可作为AI辅助协作学习的可靠触发信号，反馈时机、透明度和预期性调节对支持有效结对编程至关重要。

Abstract: Pair programming is a widely used collaborative learning practice in computer science education yet its effectiveness varies substantially due to breakdowns in coordination attention and cognitive regulation between partners. This paper investigates whether AI supported feedback grounded in joint visual attention and joint mental effort can improve collaborative programming performance and how feedback timing shapes learner AI interaction. Two experimental studies using dual eye tracking capture real time indicators of collaborative regulation during debugging tasks. Study 1 examines reactive feedback that intervenes when observed joint visual attention or joint mental effort deviates beyond predefined thresholds while Study 2 evaluates proactive feedback that forecasts future regulatory breakdowns using machine learning models and intervenes pre emptively. Across both studies feedback effectiveness is assessed through debugging success time on task and feedback uptake reflected in code changes. Multimodal feedback significantly improves collaborative performance compared to no feedback conditions. Reactive feedback yields strong gains in debugging success and efficiency particularly when joint visual attention and joint mental effort based feedback are combined. Proactive forecast based feedback further enhances performance reduces time on task and increases constructive feedback uptake while relying less on intrusive interventions. Proactive feedback better preserves learner agency by maintaining optimal collaboration states, particularly for high-performing pairs. These findings demonstrate that gaze and mental effort synchrony can serve as reliable actionable triggers for AI supported collaborative learning highlighting the importance of feedback timing transparency and anticipatory regulation in supporting effective pair programming.

</details>


### [74] [EventColumn: Integrating Event Sequences into Tabular Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06065)
*Jakob Zethofer,Andreas Hinterreiter,Lukas Schiefermüller,Belgin Mutlu,Marc Streit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: EventColumn是一种新型列类型，将事件序列数据与异构表格属性整合到统一表格中，支持在实例和组级别同时比较事件序列与数值、分类及时间属性，并通过钢铁行业数据和公开电商数据集验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只能单独比较事件序列或表格数据，缺乏同时比较两者的工具。与钢铁行业合作过程中，发现需要一种能够整合事件序列与异构表格属性的解决方案，以促进生产事件和仓库物流分析。

Method: 提出EventColumn列类型，将事件序列数据与数值、分类、时间等表格属性整合到统一表中；提供压缩概览、热力图组摘要、按事件类型对齐以及相似历史项目的箱线图等可视化功能；集成到Taggle和Microsoft Power BI中。

Result: 在钢铁生产物流数据和公开电商数据集上验证了EventColumn的有效性，展示了其在实例和组级别同时比较事件序列与表格属性的能力。

Conclusion: EventColumn能够泛化到广泛的事件序列数据集，填补了现有方法无法同时比较事件序列和表格属性的空白，为行业数据分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We introduce EventColumn, a new column type that integrates event-sequence data with heterogeneous tabular attributes into a single unified table. EventColumn lets analysts compare event sequences alongside numerical, categorical, and temporal attributes at both instance and group levels, offering a compressed overview, heatmap group summaries, alignment by event types, and boxplots of similar historical items. We developed EventColumn together with collaborators from the steel industry to facilitate the analysis of production events and warehouse logistics, but the solution generalizes to a wide range of event sequence datasets with additional tabular attributes. Unlike most existing approaches that compare either event sequences or tables, EventColumn supports simultaneous comparison of both. We demonstrate its integration with Taggle and Microsoft Power BI on data from steel production logistics and on a public e-commerce dataset.

</details>


### [75] [AffectGPT-RL: Revealing Roles of Reinforcement Learning in Open-Vocabulary Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06126)
*Zheng Lian,Fan Zhang,Lan Chen,Yazhou Zhang,Rui Liu,Jinyang Wu,Haoyu Chen,Xiaobai Li,Xiaojiang Peng,Bin He,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AffectGPT-RL框架，利用强化学习优化开放词汇多模态情感识别（OV-MER）中不可微的评估指标，解决了传统token级损失与评估指标不一致的问题，在OV-MER和基础情感识别任务上均取得显著性能提升，并在MER-UniBench上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OV-MER方法使用token级损失进行训练，但该目标与OV-MER的评估指标（基于情感轮的度量计算）不一致，且这些指标无法通过梯度反向传播直接优化，导致训练与评估存在错配。

Method: 采用强化学习框架（AffectGPT-RL），利用其优化不可微目标的能力，直接对OV-MER的评估指标进行优化，同时引入推理过程以提升情感理解能力。

Result: AffectGPT-RL在OV-MER任务上取得显著性能提升，并在基础情感识别任务上达到MER-UniBench的SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 本文首次探索了强化学习在OV-MER中的应用，验证了其有效性，揭示了推理过程、不同奖励函数的作用以及对其他情感任务的泛化能力，为后续研究提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Multimodal Emotion Recognition (OV-MER) aims to predict emotions without being constrained by predefined label spaces, thereby enabling fine-grained emotion understanding. Unlike traditional discriminative methods, OV-MER leverages generative models to capture the full spectrum of emotions and employs emotion wheels (EWs) for metric calculation. Previous approaches primarily rely on token-level loss during training. However, this objective is misaligned with the metrics used in OV-MER, and these metrics cannot be directly optimized via gradient backpropagation. To address this limitation, we turn our attention to reinforcement learning, as this strategy can optimize non-differentiable objectives. We term this framework AffectGPT-RL. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments to elucidate the role of reinforcement learning in this task, revealing the necessity of the reasoning process, the impact of different rewards, and the generalizability to other emotion tasks such as sentiment analysis and basic emotion recognition. Experimental results demonstrate that AffectGPT-RL yields significant performance improvements on OV-MER. Beyond this task, we also achieve remarkable performance gains on basic emotion recognition, attaining state-of-the-art results on MER-UniBench. To the best of our knowledge, this is the pioneering work exploring the role of reinforcement learning in OV-MER, providing valuable guidance for subsequent researchers. Our code is provided in the supplementary material and will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [76] [LearnMate^2: Design and Evaluation of an LLM-powered Personalized and Adaptive Support System for Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06257)
*Xinyu Jessica Wang,Christine P. Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文迭代设计了一个名为\tool{}的LLM驱动工具，支持在线学习中的个性化学习计划、实时上下文辅助和自适应学习活动。通过两项用户研究（n=24和n=16）评估，结果表明该工具相比现有在线学习平台和LLM支持工具，能显著提升学习效果和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习虽然普及度高且开放获取，但缺乏个性化指导，而个性化对于有效学习至关重要。作者希望利用大语言模型的进展来弥合这一差距，探索如何设计LLM驱动的工具来支持个性化和自适应学习。

Method: 作者迭代设计了一个名为\tool{}的工具，提供个性化学习计划、实时上下文辅助和自适应学习活动。先进行了一项24人的初步研究评估有效性和可用性并据此优化系统，随后进行了16人的评估实验，与最先进的在线学习平台和LLM支持工具组合进行对比。

Result: 与现有在线学习和支持工具相比，\tool{}在提升学习效果和用户体验方面均有改进。该工具推进了AI教学法的发展。

Conclusion: 本文增进了对个性化AI驱动教育工具设计空间的理解，以及这些工具对用户体验的潜在影响。

Abstract: Personalization is crucial for effective learning, yet online learning, designed for widespread availability and open access, lacks personalized guidance. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) offer opportunities to bridge this gap. We explore how LLM-driven tools may be designed to support personalized and adaptive learning and examine how they shape user experience and learning outcomes. We iteratively designed \tool{} to support online learning by providing personalized study plans, real-time contextual assistance, and adaptive learning activities. A preliminary study ($n=24$) assessed the effectiveness and usability of \tool{} and informed refinements in our system, which we then evaluated ($n = 16$) against a combination of a state-of-the-art online learning platform and an LLM for learning support. Results indicate that \tool{} advances AI pedagogy by improving both learning outcomes and user experience compared to existing online learning and support tools. This work advances our understanding of the design space of personalized, AI-driven educational tools and their potential impact on user experience.

</details>


### [77] [LLM-Based Educational Simulation: Evaluating Temporal Student Persona Stability Across ADHD Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06307)
*Jana Gonnermann-Müller,Jennifer Haase,Nicolas Leins,Thomas Kosch,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过双评估框架（自述特征+观察者评分）测试LLM模拟ADHD学生行为的一致性，发现高强度的自述特征稳定，但非脚本对话中行为表达会出现漂移，而结构化脚本交互可消除漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM模拟学生虽为教育研究和教师培训提供可扩展方案，但其有效性取决于模型能否在长时间交互中维持稳定的角色身份，这一问题尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 采用双评估框架（自述特征+观察者评分），设计两个实验，涵盖4种ADHD角色条件、5种LLM和3种提示设计，分别测量对话间稳定性（N=4,968）和对话内稳定性（N=3,952，9轮对话）。

Result: 高强度ADHD角色的自述特征保持稳定；观察者评分显示，中高强度ADHD角色在非脚本对话中出现对话内漂移，而带有明确任务提示的脚本交互完全消除了这一漂移。

Conclusion: 稳定的、符合角色的模拟学习者需要结构化交互设计来维持行为一致性，这对教师培训、适应性辅导及任何需要持续路径依赖交互的应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Student simulation with Large language models (LLMs) offers a scalable alternative for educational research and teacher training. Yet, its validity depends on whether models maintain stable personas across extended interactions. We test this prerequisite using a dual-assessment framework measuring self-reported characteristics and observer-rated behavioral expressions. Across two experiments testing four clinically-grounded ADHD persona conditions, five LLMs, and three prompt designs, we quantify between-conversation stability (N=4,968) and within-conversation stability (N=3,952 across 9 turns). Self-reported characteristics remain stable for high intensities, constituting a necessary prerequisite for valid behavioral simulation. Observer-rated behavioral expression reveals selective instability: within-conversation drift occurs in unscripted dialog for high and moderate ADHD personas. Scripted interactions with explicit task prompts eliminate this drift entirely. Stable, persona-aligned simulated learners benefit from a structured interaction design to maintain behavioral coherence, which holds significant implications for teacher training, adaptive tutoring, and any application requiring sustained, path-dependent learner interactions.

</details>


### [78] [Human-AI Co-Evolution and Epistemic Collapse: A Dynamical Systems Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06347)
*Xuening Wu,Yanlan Kang,Qianya Xu,Kexuan Xie,Jiaqi Mi,Honggang Wang,Yubin Liu,Zeping Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一视角，将人类与大语言模型视为通过使用、生成和再训练构成的耦合动态系统。通过三变量最小模型，识别出三种动态机制：协同进化增强、脆弱平衡和退化收敛。模拟表明，过度依赖AI会导致低多样性、次优均衡，从信息论角度看，这对应于人机回路中的新兴信息瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将认知卸载与模型坍缩视为孤立现象，本文旨在建立统一视角，揭示人机耦合系统的反馈动力学如何影响AI系统的发展轨迹。

Method: 构建包含人类认知、数据质量和模型能力三个变量的最小模型，通过理论分析与数值模拟，识别不同反馈条件下的动态机制，并从信息论角度解释系统行为。

Result: 识别出三种动态机制：协同进化增强、脆弱平衡和退化收敛。模拟显示过度依赖AI导致系统转向低多样性、次优均衡状态，对应人机回路中的信息瓶颈。

Conclusion: AI系统的发展轨迹不仅受模型设计影响，更受人类与AI协同进化动力学塑造；过度依赖AI可能引发信息瓶颈，导致知识多样性丧失。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are reshaping how knowledge is produced, with increasing reliance on AI systems for generation, summarization, and reasoning. While prior work has studied cognitive offloading in humans and model collapse in recursive training, these effects are typically considered in isolation. We propose a unified perspective: humans and language models form a coupled dynamical system linked by a feedback loop of usage, generation, and retraining. We introduce a minimal model with three variables -- human cognition, data quality, and model capability -- and show that this feedback can give rise to distinct dynamical regimes. Our analysis identifies three regimes: co-evolutionary enhancement, fragile equilibrium, and degenerative convergence. Through a simple simulation, we demonstrate that increasing reliance on AI can induce a transition toward a low-diversity, suboptimal equilibrium. From an information-theoretic perspective, this transition corresponds to an emergent information bottleneck in the human-AI loop, where entropy reduction reflects loss of diversity and support under closed-loop feedback rather than beneficial compression. These results suggest that the trajectory of AI systems is shaped not only by model design, but by the dynamics of human-AI co-evolution.

</details>


### [79] [SIGMA-ASL: Sensor-Integrated Multimodal Dataset for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06351)
*Xiaofang Xiao,Guangchao Li,Guangrong Zhao,Qi Lin,Wen Ma,Hongkai Wen,Yanxiang Wang,Yiran Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了SIGMA-ASL，一个大规模多模态美国手语识别数据集，融合RGB-D相机、毫米波雷达和IMU传感器，包含20名参与者、160个ASL手势、93,545个时间同步的多模态片段，为隐私保护和鲁棒的手语识别提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLR研究过度依赖视觉数据，存在光照/遮挡敏感、隐私风险高、跨模态多样性不足等问题，亟需引入非视觉传感器以构建更鲁棒、隐私友好的识别系统。

Method: 使用Azure Kinect RGB-D相机、毫米波雷达和双腕部IMU采集多模态数据；通过统一传感框架实现毫秒级模态对齐；设计标准化的预处理流水线和用户依赖/独立两种评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含20人、160个ASL手势、93,545个词级多模态片段的数据集，并通过大量实验验证了数据集质量及多模态融合的有效性。

Conclusion: SIGMA-ASL为开发鲁棒、隐私保护且普适的手语识别系统提供了高质量的多模态基准资源，有望推动SLR研究超越纯视觉范式的局限。

Abstract: Automatic sign language recognition (SLR) has become a key enabler of inclusive human-computer interaction, fostering seamless communication between deaf individuals and hearing communities. Despite significant advances in multimodal learning, existing SLR research remains dominated by vision-based datasets, which are limited by sensitivity to lighting and occlusion, privacy concerns, and a lack of cross-modal diversity. To address these challenges, we introduce SIGMA-ASL, a large-scale multimodal dataset for SLR. The dataset integrates an Azure Kinect RGB-D camera, a millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar, and two wrist-worn inertial measurement units (IMUs) to capture complementary visual, radio-reflection, and kinematic information. Collected in a controlled studio environment with 20 participants performing 160 common American sign language (ASL) signs, SIGMA-ASL provides 93,545 temporally synchronized word-level multimodal clips. A unified sensing framework achieves millisecond-level alignment across modalities, enabling reliable sensor fusion and cross-modal learning. We further design standardized preprocessing pipelines and benchmarking protocols under both user-dependent and user-independent settings, offering a comprehensive foundation for evaluating single and multimodal SLR. Extensive experiments validate the dataset's quality and demonstrate its potential as a valuable resource for developing robust, privacy-preserving, and ubiquitous sign language recognition systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Toward Space-Based Public Key Systems: Enabling Secure Space Communications through In-Orbit Trust Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05948)
*Rehana Yasmin,Paulo Esteves-Verissimo,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向新太空时代的星基公钥基础设施(PKI)架构设计，包括天-地一体化PKI和完全自主星基PKI两种方案，将证书管理与验证从地面迁移至太空，以解决传统地面PKI在动态太空环境中的延迟和操作瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: New Space时代近地轨道卫星数量激增，需要安全、近实时的协调机制，而传统依赖地面的PKI存在延迟和操作瓶颈，难以满足多运营商环境下太空资产高效认证的可扩展性和可用性需求。

Method: 提出两种部署方案：(1)天-地一体化PKI——在轨验证机构+地面签发；(2)完全自主星基PKI——在轨签发与验证。从可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本和操作复杂性五个维度分析多运营商环境下的部署权衡，并给出基线延迟分析。

Result: 给出了两种方案在可扩展性、可用性、安全性、成本和操作复杂性方面的对比分析，并提供了在轨信任管理的性能延迟基线数据。

Conclusion: 星基PKI可有效减少对地面站的依赖，提升太空资产认证效率与互操作性，为下一代多运营商太空任务提供可行的认证架构方案。

Abstract: The New Space era has led to a rapid increase in satellites operated by independent entities in near-Earth orbit. This shift enables richer space services but also requires secure, near-real-time coordination, making efficient authentication of space assets critical for next-generation missions. Traditional ground-dependent Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) suffers from latency and operational bottlenecks that limit scalability and availability in dynamic space environments. This paper proposes architectural designs for space-based PKI that shift certificate management and validation from ground infrastructure into space, reducing reliance on ground stations while enabling interoperability and cross-entity collaboration. Two deployment schemes are introduced: a space-ground integrated PKI with in-orbit validation authorities, and a fully autonomous space-based PKI with in-space issuance and validation. We analyze deployment trade-offs in scalability, availability, security, cost, and operational complexity in multi-operator environments. A baseline latency analysis is provided to illustrate performance implications of in-orbit trust management.

</details>


### [81] [Identifier-Free Code Embedding Models for Scalable Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05251)
*Eric Wolos,Michael Doyle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对二进制逆向工程中的函数关联问题，提出通过对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，实现了源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联，在多项基准测试中显著优于其他模型，并展现出对未见任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数关联搜索工具未充分利用AI搜索能力，且先前工作未覆盖源代码与反编译剥离代码之间双向关联的标准预处理需求，存在研究空白。

Method: 形式化定义了函数关联问题；采用对比学习（contrastive learning）对Qwen3-Embedding模型进行微调，以提升嵌入模型在两种代码表示间双向关联的性能。

Result: 微调后的模型在所有函数关联基线测试中以显著优势超越其他模型，并在未显式训练的常量算法关联任务上展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: 对比学习微调的嵌入模型能够有效弥合源代码与反编译剥离代码之间的表示鸿沟，实现高质量的双向函数关联，且具备良好的任务泛化性。

Abstract: Function association is a useful process for binary reverse engineers. Search tools exist to perform association at scale, but they do not utilize the full range of capabilities that AI-enabled search provides. Prior work has explored the development of embedding models for association between certain reverse engineering code representations, but that work does not cover bidirectional association between source code and decompiled, stripped code with standard preprocessing requirements. To bridge this gap, we formalize this function association problem and evaluate the extent to which embedding models can bidirectionally associate between these two representations. To improve model performance at this task, we fine-tune a Qwen3-Embedding model with contrastive learning. We find that our new model outperforms other models on all function association baselines by a substantial margin and generalizes to a constant-algorithm association task it is not explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [82] [SecureMCP: A Policy-Enforced LLM Data Access Framework for AIoT Systems via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05260)
*Wonbae Kim,Hee-Kyong Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecureMCP，一个将基于角色的访问控制（RBAC）与MCP服务器集成的策略强制执行LLM数据访问框架，用于防御LLM生成的SQL查询中的提示注入攻击。框架包含五个防御模块（check_policy、explain_gate、SQL Interceptor、Risk Level Filter、DB Isolation），在IoT-SQL数据集上使用Qwen3-8B评估，结果表明防御模块在保持执行准确性的同时，对2,400个对抗性查询实现了82.3%的策略合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的SQL查询在AIoT系统中部署时面临严重的安全风险，提示注入攻击可操纵LLM生成未经授权的查询以暴露敏感数据或执行破坏性操作。现有NL2SQL研究专注于查询准确性，MCP服务器实现仅提供SQL级别的保护而缺乏细粒度的基于角色的访问控制。

Method: 提出SecureMCP框架，集成RBAC与MCP服务器，构建包含五个防御模块的顺序故障关闭流水线：check_policy（表级和列级RBAC）、explain_gate（阻止高成本爆炸性查询）、SQL Interceptor（检测危险模式）、Risk Level Filter（SQL风险分类）和DB Isolation（跨数据库限制）。防御映射到基于OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications的六种提示注入类型。

Result: 在IoT-SQL数据集（11张表、173列、239,398条记录）上使用Qwen3-8B评估。实验A：防御模块保持执行准确性，EX-in-ALLOW在四种RBAC角色中保持在65.1%-76.4%，与未受保护基线63.8%匹配。实验B：对2,400个对抗性查询实现82.3%的策略合规性，真实防御失败率仅3.4%。check_policy模块占拦截量的78.7%，次级模块额外提升17.5个百分点。注入纳入率72.5%证实LLM高度易受攻击。

Conclusion: SecureMCP有效解决了LLM生成SQL的安全问题，通过多层外部策略强制执行弥补了LLM自身安全机制的不足。实验证明了防御框架的有效性和准确性保持能力，确立了外部策略强制执行对于保护AIoT系统中LLM数据访问的必要性。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM)-generated SQL queries in Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems introduces critical security risks, as prompt injection attacks can manipulate LLMs into producing unauthorized queries that expose sensitive data or execute destructive operations. Existing NL2SQL research focuses on query accuracy, while MCP server implementations provide only SQL-level protections without fine-grained role-based access control. This paper proposes SecureMCP, a policy-enforced LLM data access framework integrating Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) with an MCP server to establish multi-layer defense for LLM-generated SQL execution. The framework incorporates five defense modules -- check_policy for table-and-column-level RBAC, explain_gate for cost-explosive query blocking, SQL Interceptor for dangerous pattern detection, Risk Level Filter for SQL risk classification, and DB Isolation for cross-database restriction -- operating in a sequential fail-closed pipeline mapped to six prompt injection types grounded in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. We evaluate SecureMCP on the IoT-SQL dataset (11 tables, 173 columns, 239,398 records) using Qwen3-8B. Experiment A demonstrates that defense modules preserve execution accuracy, with EX-in-ALLOW remaining within 65.1%-76.4% across four RBAC roles, matching the unprotected baseline of 63.8%. Experiment B shows that SecureMCP achieves 82.3% Policy Compliance on 2,400 adversarial queries, with genuine defense failures limited to 3.4%. The defense-in-depth analysis reveals check_policy accounts for 78.7% of blocks, while secondary modules contribute an additional 17.5 percentage-point improvement. The Injection Incorporation Rate of 72.5% confirms high LLM susceptibility, establishing the necessity of external policy enforcement.

</details>


### [83] [Differential Privacy in the Extensive-Form Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05266)
*Stephen Pasteris,Rahul Savani,Theodore Turocy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对扩展式博弈中的在线学习问题，提出了首个满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，且每轮时间复杂度与传输简化策略所需时间相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展式博弈中的在线学习（bandit）问题尚未有研究关注差分隐私保护，而局部差分隐私作为最强隐私保护形式，将其引入该问题具有重要理论价值和实际意义。

Method: 设计了一种满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，在每轮试验中，学习者（由服务器协调）与 oblivious adversary 进行扩展式博弈，观察所处信息集及收益/损失，算法复杂度与传输简化策略的时间成正比。

Result: 算法达到了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A为学习者可能采取的总动作数，S为简化策略数，T为试验轮数。

Conclusion: 这是首次将差分隐私（任何形式）引入扩展式博弈 bandit 问题，且局部差分隐私作为最强隐私模型，证明了隐私保护与学习效率之间可达到较好的权衡。

Abstract: We consider the extensive-form bandit problem, where on each trial the learner (a user coordinated by a server) plays an extensive-form game against an oblivious adversary, observing the information sets it finds itself in as well as the resulting payoff/loss. We give an algorithm for this problem that satisfies $ε$-local differential privacy and attains a regret of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$, where $A$ is the total number of actions that the learner can possibly take, $S$ is the number of the learner's possible reduced strategies, and $T$ is the number of trials. On each trial, the time complexity of our algorithm is, up to a factor logarithmic in the maximum number of actions at an infoset, equal to the time required for the server to transmit the reduced strategy to the user. We note that local differential privacy is the strongest version of differential privacy and, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study differential privacy of any form in the extensive-form bandit problem.

</details>


### [84] [Dynamic Authorization for Knowledge-Base Agents in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05269)
*Loay Abdelrazek,Leyli Karacay,Marin Orlic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种混合授权框架，结合角色和一阶逻辑谓词，在知识图谱的三元组级别实施零信任授权，以解决6G多智能体系统对知识库的安全访问问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G架构向去中心化多智能体系统演进，传统RBAC模型无法为自治智能体与语义数据交互提供所需细粒度授权控制。

Method: 提出混合授权框架，集成角色与一阶逻辑谓词，在三元组级别（主语-谓语-对象）实施授权，消除权限继承，并贯彻零信任原则。

Result: 在知识图谱层面实现细粒度授权，确保智能体仅能访问其特定功能生命周期所需的元数据。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够解决6G去中心化多智能体系统中知识库安全访问的粒度不足问题。

Abstract: As 6G architectures transition toward decentralized Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), ensuring secure access to shared Knowledge Bases (KB) is critical. Traditional authorization models like RBAC fail to provide the granularity required for autonomous agents interacting with Semantic-based data. This work proposes a hybrid authorization framework that integrates roles and First-Order Logic (FOL) predicates to enforce zero-trust principles at the knowledge-graph level. We eliminate permission inheritance by enforcing authorization at the triple level (Subject-Predicate-Object), ensuring agents only access metadata required for their specific functional lifecycle.

</details>


### [85] [Shattering the Echo Chamber: Hidden Safeguards in Manuscripts Against the AI Takeover of Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05271)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Yong Yang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IntraGuard框架，通过在PDF底层结构中嵌入异构防御文本对象，在不改变视觉呈现的前提下防御LLM审稿外包攻击，在7个商业聊天机器人和12个会议场景下最高达84%防御成功率，且对人类审稿人无影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能力提升导致审稿人可能完全外包同行评审给商业聊天机器人，现有防御方法（如隐藏指令）依赖同质化文本注入，容易被清洗或中和，亟需更鲁棒的防御机制。

Method: 提出IntraGuard，一种黑盒、与会议无关的防御框架。利用PDF的结构-视觉解耦特性，支持显式策略（触发拒绝/警告信号）和隐式策略（嵌入预定义文本标记），通过三种流内注入机制在PDF底层结构中嵌入异构防御文本对象，不改变视觉呈现。

Result: 在7个商业聊天机器人设置和12个跨学科会议中，防御成功率最高达84%；人类审稿人评审不变性保持完好；在普通个人计算机上每篇手稿平均开销仅1秒；评估了11种自适应攻击（包括手稿清洗和指令干扰）。

Conclusion: IntraGuard是轻量级、硬件无关的有效防御框架，能抵御LLM审稿外包威胁，同时不影响人类审稿体验，并讨论了集成防御的构建意义。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable, editorial boards and program committees are growing concerned about reviewers who fully outsource peer review to commercial chatbots. This concern stems from prior findings that current chatbots lack the independent critical thinking and depth of reasoning required to assess scientific novelty. One promising direction for mitigating this concern is to embed hidden instructions into manuscripts that disrupt or alter chatbot-generated reviews. However, existing methods remain intuitive and fragile, as they typically rely on homogeneous payloads injected in an inter-stream manner, rendering them susceptible to sanitization or neutralization.
  In this paper, we identify End-to-End Review Outsourcing as an emerging threat and propose IntraGuard, a black-box, venue-agnostic defense framework grounded in the structural--visual decoupling inherent to the PDF. Designed for committee-side deployment, IntraGuard supports both explicit strategies that trigger refusal or warning signals, and implicit strategies that embed predefined textual markers into the generated review. These strategies can be deployed via any of three intra-stream injection mechanisms, each of which seamlessly embeds heterogeneous defensive text objects within the PDF's underlying structure without altering its visual presentation. Extensive evaluations across 7 real-world commercial chatbot settings and 12 venues spanning diverse disciplines show that IntraGuard achieves a defense success rate of up to 84%, while preserving peer-review invariance for human reviewers. IntraGuard is lightweight and hardware-independent, incurring an average overhead of only one second per manuscript on a commodity personal computer. We further evaluate 11 adaptive attacks spanning manuscript sanitization and instruction interference, and discuss the implications of constructing ensemble defenses.

</details>


### [86] [Sealing the Audit-Runtime Gap for LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05274)
*Tingda Shen,Yebo Feng,Konglin Zhu,Xiaojun Jia,Yang Liu,Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL 是一个用于LLM技能的可验证供应链安全框架，通过去中心化链上注册表、DAO审计和技能验证加载器，弥合了审计与运行时之间的鸿沟，实现了从发布到运行时的加密绑定，在实际部署中批处理验证时间低于86毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM技能生态系统存在严重的安全漏洞：技能内容与可信指令无法可靠分离，可执行工具可调用特权操作，导致技能供应链面临注入、篡改和rug-pull攻击。现有防御手段存在审计-运行时脱节的问题，无法确保被审核的技能与最终执行的技能一致。

Method: SIGIL 框架包含两大核心组件：(1) 可验证托管——通过防篡改的去中心化链上注册表直接供LLM获取技能，支持四种发布类型（透明、许可、密封、承诺），所有技能先经DAO审计委员会在权益质押-罚没经济模型下进行审核；(2) 可验证加载——Skill Verification Loader (SVL) 作为强制加载路径，根据技能类型检索并解密技能，验证其与链上记录的完整性，并在注入上下文前强制执行权限清单。

Result: 在包含1,023个真实世界技能（涵盖六种攻击类型）的部署上，SVL完成批量完整性验证的时间低于86毫秒，证明了该方案的实际可行性。

Conclusion: LLM技能可以在实用成本下实现从发布到运行时的端到端加密绑定，有效弥合审计-运行时差距。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) ecosystems such as Claude Code and ChatGPT increasingly rely on skills: packages of natural-language instructions and executable tools. Once in the LLM's context, skill content cannot be reliably separated from trusted instructions, and a skill's executable side can invoke privileged actions, exposing the skill supply chain to injection, tampering, and rug-pull attacks. Existing defenses are stage-bound: centralized signing, audit reports unbound from the runtime artifact, or policy engines that cannot attest to what was approved. We present SIGIL, the first framework that seals the audit-runtime gap for LLM skills. SIGIL delivers verifiable hosting through a tamper-evident, decentralized on-chain registry from which LLMs fetch skills directly. The registry admits four publication types, Transparent, Licensed, Sealed, and Committed, spanning plaintext public distribution, monetized access, custodial use, and off-chain workflows; before admission, every skill is vetted by a Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO) audit committee that supports pluggable auditing methods under a stake-and-slash economic model. At load time, SIGIL delivers verified loading through a skill verification protocol executed by a Skill Verification Loader (SVL) embedded as the mandatory loading path: the SVL retrieves and decrypts the skill as its type requires, verifies its integrity against the on-chain record, and enforces its permission manifest before context injection. We evaluate SIGIL on a real-world deployment against 1,023 in-the-wild skills spanning six attack types. At load time, the SVL verifies each skill's integrity against its on-chain record and enforces its approved permission manifest, completing batched verification under 86 ms. Together, these results show that LLM skills can be cryptographically bound from publication through runtime at practical cost.

</details>


### [87] [GLiNER Guard: Unified Encoder Family for Production LLM Safety and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05277)
*Bogdan Minko,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard)，一种统一的编码器模型，可在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测。包含三种变体：紧凑型（145-147M）用于高吞吐服务，Omni型（209M）用于更优的审核质量。紧凑型在A100动态批处理下达到193请求/秒，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍。同时发布了PII-Bench基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM系统需要在严格的延迟和成本约束下同时进行安全审核和PII检测，现有方案存在两难：自回归审核器准确但昂贵，轻量编码器快速但能力不足。需要一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出GLiGuard，一种基于编码器的统一模型架构，包含三种变体（紧凑型uni-encoder、bi-encoder和Omni），在单次前向传播中同时执行安全分类和PII检测，简化了安全管线的复杂度。

Result: 紧凑型模型在单A100动态批处理下达到193请求/秒，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍；Omni在公开安全基准上与更大的审核模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 基于编码器的护栏模型为始终在线的审核任务提供了一种低成本的实用替代方案，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低了延迟和成本。

Abstract: Production LLM systems require both safety moderation and PII detection under strict latency and cost constraints. This creates a trade-off: autoregressive moderators are accurate but expensive, while lightweight encoders are faster but less capable. We present GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard), a unified encoder that performs safety classification and PII detection in a single forward pass, simplifying safety pipelines. We introduce three variants: compact uni- and bi-encoders (145-147M) for high-throughput serving, and GLiGuard Omni (209M) for stronger moderation quality. Under dynamic batching on a single A100, the compact model reaches 193 requests/sec with P99 latency below 1s, achieving 1.6x higher throughput than GLiNER2. Omni remains competitive with much larger moderators on public safety benchmarks. We also release PII-Bench, a span-level benchmark for evaluating PII detection in end-to-end pipelines. Overall, encoder-based guardrails offer a practical low-cost alternative for always-on moderation. Models and benchmarks are released on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [88] [How Far Are VLMs from Privacy Awareness in the Physical World? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05340)
*Junran Wang,Xinjie Shen,Zehao Jin,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ImmersedPrivacy，一个基于Unity的交互式视听评估框架，用于测试具身VLM在物理环境中的隐私意识。评估12个SOTA模型发现：场景复杂度增加时性能单调下降（感知缺陷），社交情境变化时最高准确率不超过65%，在冲突指令下最佳模型仅51%能平衡任务完成与隐私保护。当前VLM存在感知脆弱性，无法将隐私知识转化为实际行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM隐私评估基准局限于单模态、文本表示，无法捕捉真实物理环境中的隐私挑战。VLM正被部署为具身助手的认知核心，在家庭、医院等私密空间中具备观察和操作隐私敏感信息的能力，亟需评估其物理环境中的隐私意识。

Method: 构建基于Unity的交互式视听仿真环境ImmersedPrivacy，设计三个递进测试层级：(1) 杂乱场景中识别敏感物品；(2) 适应社交情境变化；(3) 解决显式指令与隐含隐私约束的冲突。对12个SOTA VLM进行系统评估。

Result: 杂乱场景中所有模型性能随复杂度增加单调下降；社交情境变化时模型选择准确率均不超过65%；冲突指令下最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅51%能平衡任务完成与隐私保护。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理世界中存在感知脆弱性，其隐私知识无法有效指导情境化行为，在具身部署中面临严重的隐私风险。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous cognitive cores for embodied assistants, evaluating their privacy awareness in physical environments becomes critical. Unlike digital chatbots, these agents operate in intimate spaces, such as homes and hospitals, where they possess the physical agency to observe and manipulate privacy-sensitive information and artifacts. However, current benchmarks remain limited to unimodal, text-based representations that cannot capture the demands of real-world settings. To bridge this gap, we present ImmersedPrivacy, an interactive audio-visual evaluation framework that simulates realistic physical environments using a Unity-based simulator. ImmersedPrivacy evaluates physically grounded privacy awareness across three progressive tiers that test a model's ability to identify sensitive items in cluttered scenes, adapt to shifting social contexts, and resolve conflicts between explicit commands and inferred privacy constraints. Our evaluation of 12 state-of-the-art models reveals consistent deficits. In cluttered scenes, all models exhibit monotonic performance decay as scene complexity grows due to perceptual deficit. When social context shifts, no model exceed 65% selection accuracy. Under conflicting commands, the best model gemini-3.1-pro perfectly balances task completion and privacy preservation in only 51% of cases. These findings reveal that current VLMs in the physical world suffer from perceptual fragility and fail to let their knowledge of privacy cues govern their situated behavior. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/immersed-privacy/immersed-privacy .

</details>


### [89] [Privacy Without Losing Place: A Paradigm for Private Retrieval in Spatial RAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05459)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri,Vinay M. Shashidhar,Jong Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PAS（隐私锚点替代）机制，通过相对锚点编码（锚点+方向箱+距离箱）在空间RAG系统中保护用户位置隐私，在合成城市数据集上达到约370-400m的对抗定位误差，同时保留超过50%的检索性能，且下游生成质量保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方法直接扰动用户位置，难以与RAG流水线无缝集成，亟需一种结构化机制在保护用户位置隐私的同时兼容现代RAG系统。

Method: PAS将用户位置编码为相对锚点表示，包含锚点、方向箱和距离箱三个组件，替代直接位置扰动，从而与标准RAG检索流程集成。

Result: 在合成城市数据集上，PAS实现了约370-400m的对抗定位误差，保留了超过50%的基线检索性能，且下游生成质量保持稳健；同时发现PAS的隐私-效用关系呈非单调特性。

Conclusion: 结构化空间表示为基于位置推理的RAG系统提供了一种实用的隐私保护途径，尽管检索性能有所下降，但大语言模型能够补偿不完全的空间检索。

Abstract: This work introduces PAS -- Privacy Anchor Substitution, a structured mechanism for enabling user location privacy in spatial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Unlike conventional differential privacy methods that directly perturb user locations, PAS represents location with relative anchor encoding consisting of an anchor, direction bin, and distance bin, allowing seamless integration with modern RAG pipelines. We evaluate PAS on a synthetic urban dataset and show that it achieves impressive coarse privacy guarantees, with approximately 370-400m adversarial location error, while retaining more than half of the baseline retrieval performance. Despite the slight drop in retrieval performance, the downstream generation quality under PAS remains comparatively robust, indicating that large language models can compensate for imperfect spatial retrieval. Furthermore, we provide empirical analysis showing that PAS exhibits non-monotonic privacy-utility relationship with respect to privacy parameters. We attribute this to geometric bias induced by anchor discretization, making it different from continuous noise mechanisms such as geo-indistinguishability. Our results show that structured spatial representations offer a practical approach to privacy in location based reasoning in RAG systems.

</details>


### [90] [SOCpilot: Verifying Policy Compliance for LLM-Assisted Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05501)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Leonardo Vaz de Meneses,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SOCpilot系统，用于验证LLM为安全运营中心（SOC）生成的应急响应计划是否符合策略合规性要求。通过确定性验证器，在200个真实事件中移除了466个不合规的需审批操作，且不影响基线任务召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC开始使用LLM作为副驾驶（copilot）起草应急响应计划，但这些计划可能包含目录中有效但违反强制性步骤、顺序要求或审批关卡的操作。现有方法缺乏对这种合规性问题的可测量能力。

Method: SOCpilot通过固定事件包、操作目录、策略规则、验证器和公开证据表面，使合规性问题在计划边界上可测量，然后验证副驾驶提出的操作轨迹。实验使用两个LLM提供商，基于200个真实事件，与分析师编写的参考计划对比。

Result: 确定性验证器移除了466个不合规的需审批操作，且不降低基线任务召回率。相同的内联策略文本使两个LLM提供商向相反方向变化。聚合率在固定语料库的3次重复运行中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 官方证据集中在恢复和遏制相关的审批决策上，而工件暴露了强制性和排序修复的零成本就绪检查。发布了可运行工件供独立评审者在不访问私有事件数据的情况下重新推导公开结果。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) are beginning to use large language models (LLMs) as copilots to draft incident-response plans. These plans may include actions that are valid per the catalog but still violate mandatory steps, required ordering, or approval gates before analyst review. SOCpilot makes this compliance question measurable at the plan boundary. It fixes the incident package, action catalog, policy rules, verifier, and public evidence surface. Next, it verifies the copilot's proposed action trace. We evaluate two LLM providers on 200 real incidents from an anonymized production SOC in a financial-sector case study. We compare their plans to paired analyst-authored references from the same security orchestration, automation, and response (SOAR) cases. An identical inline policy text moves the two providers in opposite directions. A deterministic verifier removes 466 non-compliant, approval-gated actions, without reducing baseline-task recall. Aggregate rates remain stable across 3 reruns of the fixed corpus. The official evidence focuses on approval-gated decisions regarding recovery and containment. Separately, the artifact exposes zero-cost readiness checks for mandatory and ordering repairs. We release the runnable artifact so independent reviewers can rederive the public results without access to private incident data.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Collection: Measuring the Detection Efficacy of Modern Security Logging Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05531)
*Ryan Holeman,John Hastings,Varghese Mathew Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了CIM、OCSF、ECS三种安全日志标准对50个远程代码执行漏洞的检测效能，提出基于SETC自动化框架的可复现评估方法论，发现不同标准在攻击指标捕获能力上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全日志对威胁检测至关重要，但各种行业标准日志框架的相对有效性缺乏系统研究，亟需填补这一空白以指导安全从业者做出有依据的日志标准选择。

Method: 构建SETC（安全利用遥测收集）自动化框架，在容器化环境中系统生成50个可复现的远程代码执行漏洞利用场景，收集CIM、OCSF、ECS三种日志标准的遥测数据，通过遥测完整性和利用可检测性两个指标量化检测效能。

Result: 发现不同日志标准在捕获关键攻击指标方面存在显著差异，并识别出关键缺口。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展、可复现的利用遥测评估方法论，为安全从业者选择日志标准提供了基于证据的清晰建议。

Abstract: Effective security logging is crucial for the timely and accurate detection of cyber threats; however, the relative effectiveness of various industry-standard logging frameworks remains understudied. This paper addresses this critical gap by presenting the first systematic evaluation of modern security logging standards utilizing a novel methodology built upon the automated Security Exploit Telemetry Collection (SETC) framework. SETC systematically generates reproducible exploit scenarios in containerized environments, collecting rich telemetry across multiple logging standards, including CIM (Common Information Model), OCSF (Open Cybersecurity Schema Framework), and ECS (Elastic Common Schema). The detection efficacy of each logging standard is quantified by measuring telemetry completeness and exploit detectability across standardized logs through detailed experiments involving 50 diverse remote code execution vulnerabilities. The resulting findings identify critical gaps and reveal significant differences in logging standards' abilities to capture key attack indicators. Our contributions include a novel evaluation methodology that enables scalable and reproducible analysis of exploit telemetry, as well as new findings that provide clear, evidence-based guidance for security practitioners to make informed decisions about adopting logging standards.

</details>


### [92] [AoI-Guided Client Selection for Robust and Timely Federated Intrusion Detection in Cloud-Edge Security Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05644)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在云边协同入侵检测中的客户端参与问题，提出基于信息年龄（AoI）的轻量级客户端选择策略（AoI优先、效用优先、混合策略），在保持通信预算不变的情况下，AoI感知选择可将平均AoI降低39-41%、峰值AoI降低约70%，同时基本保持检测性能（Macro-F1/AUC），并提供了可解释的时效-质量-鲁棒性权衡旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习的生产化安全分析管线上，仅部分客户端参与每轮训练，异构带宽、掉队者和掉线会导致服务器依赖过时的客户端信息，这严重影响了实时入侵检测的时效性。现有工作缺乏从信息时效性角度系统研究客户端参与问题。

Method: 将客户端选择建模为时效性感知的系统问题，利用Age of Information（AoI）衡量信息新鲜度；设计三种轻量级选择策略：AoI优先、效用优先、以及带可调参数的AoI+效用混合策略；在多种数据集（CIC-IDS2017子集、NSL-KDD、ToN-IoT、合成漂移基准）和场景（干净、投毒、投毒+鲁棒聚合）下进行实验评估。

Result: AoI感知选择在固定通信预算下将平均AoI降低约39-41%，峰值AoI降低约70%；混合策略通常能保持Macro-F1/AUC指标，提供可解释的权衡旋钮；但在加入假阳性率指标后并非统一帕累托占优；与修剪均值聚合结合可评估投毒鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 云边协同、隐私保护的入侵检测分析可以通过轻量级调度层改善时效性，而无需改变底层联邦学习的参与预算；AoI引导的选择策略本身并非独立的拜占庭防御机制，但可作为提升时效性的实用方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is attractive for cloud-edge intrusion detection because it enables collaborative training over distributed telemetry without centralizing raw logs. In production security analytics pipelines, however, only a subset of clients participates in each round, and heterogeneous bandwidth, stragglers, and dropouts can cause the server to rely on stale client information. This paper studies client participation as a timeliness-aware systems problem using Age of Information (AoI). We compare three lightweight policies for federated intrusion detection: AoI-first, utility-first, and a hybrid AoI+utility rule with a tunable trade-off parameter.
  Across a CIC-IDS2017 DDoS/PortScan mini subset, NSL-KDD, ToN-IoT, and a synthetic drift benchmark under clean, poisoning, and poisoning-plus-robust-aggregation settings, AoI-aware selection reduces average AoI by about 39--41% and peak AoI by about 70% relative to random sampling while keeping the per-round communication budget fixed. The hybrid policy usually preserves Macro-F1/AUC and provides an interpretable knob for balancing freshness, detection quality, and robustness, although it is not uniformly Pareto-dominant once false positive rate is included. Robustness is evaluated by combining AoI-guided selection with trimmed-mean aggregation under label-flip poisoning; the selection policy itself is not intended as a standalone Byzantine defense. The main practical message is that cloud-edge, privacy-preserving intrusion analytics can improve timeliness through a lightweight scheduling layer without changing the underlying FL participation budget.

</details>


### [93] [SafeHarbor: Hierarchical Memory-Augmented Guardrail for LLM Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05704)
*Zhe Liu,Zonghao Ying,Wenxin Zhang,Quanchen Zou,Deyue Zhang,Dongdong Yang,Xiangzheng Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeHarbor是一个针对LLM Agent的防御框架，通过上下文感知的规则提取、局部层次化记忆系统和信息熵自进化机制，在不牺牲良性任务效用的情况下有效防御恶意工具调用攻击，在GPT-4o上实现了63.6%的良性效用和超过93%的有害请求拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御机制存在过度拒绝(over-refusal)问题，即提升安全严格度会损害Agent在良性任务上的效用。亟需一种能在安全性和可用性之间取得更好平衡的防御框架。

Method: 1) 通过增强对抗生成提取上下文感知的防御规则；2) 设计局部层次化记忆系统实现动态规则注入，无需训练、即插即用；3) 引入基于信息熵的自进化机制，通过动态节点分裂与合并持续优化记忆结构。

Result: 在GPT-4o上良性任务效用达63.6%，对有害请求拒绝率超过93%，在模糊良性任务和显式恶意攻击上均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: SafeHarbor通过上下文感知的防御规则和自进化记忆机制，有效缓解了安全性与可用性之间的权衡问题，是一种高效且实用的LLM Agent防御方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of foundation models, Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated increasingly powerful tool-use capabilities. However, this proficiency introduces significant security risks, as malicious actors can manipulate agents into executing tools to generate harmful content. While existing defensive mechanisms are effective, they frequently suffer from the over-refusal problem, where increased safety strictness compromises the agent's utility on benign tasks. To mitigate this trade-off, we propose \textsc{SafeHarbor}, a novel framework designed to establish precise decision boundaries for LLM agents. Unlike static guidelines, \textsc{SafeHarbor} extracts context-aware defense rules through enhanced adversarial generation. We design a local hierarchical memory system for dynamic rule injection, offering a training-free, efficient, and plug-and-play solution. Furthermore, we introduce an information entropy-based self-evolution mechanism that continuously optimizes the memory structure through dynamic node splitting and merging. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{SafeHarbor} achieves state-of-the-art performance on both ambiguous benign tasks and explicit malicious attacks, notably attaining a peak benign utility of 63.6\% on GPT-4o while maintaining a robust refusal rate exceeding 93\% against harmful requests. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ljj-cyber/SafeHarbor.

</details>


### [94] [$α$-Wasserstein Mechanism for Rényi Pufferfish Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05723)
*Ni Ding,Wenjin Yang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出α- Wasserstein机制，通过Laplace和Gaussian噪声实现Rényi Pufferfish Privacy，利用Hölder不等式将Laplace机制的尺度参数与Wα度量上界关联，并推广到指数机制和高斯机制。实验表明该方法相比传统W∞方法显著降低噪声功率，且无需δ-近似即可达到精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pufferfish Privacy框架多基于W∞度量实现ε-Pufferfish Privacy，但缺乏对Rényi变体的系统性研究，且现有方法噪声功率较大。本文旨在建立α-Wasserstein机制，填补Rényi Pufferfish Privacy的理论空白，并提升隐私保护机制的效用。

Method: 1) 利用Hölder不等式推导Laplace机制中尺度参数与Wα度量的上界关系，实现(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy；2) 证明α=∞时恢复经典的W∞机制；3) 将框架扩展到指数机制；4) 提出适用于高斯噪声的Wα机制，推广Rényi差分隐私中的现有结果。

Result: 实验表明：α-Wasserstein机制相比W∞方法显著降低噪声功率；高斯机制相比Laplace机制提供更优的效用；所提机制无需δ-近似即可实现精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy。

Conclusion: 本文建立的α-Wasserstein机制为Rényi Pufferfish Privacy提供了统一的框架，通过Laplace和高斯噪声实现更优的隐私-效用权衡，且无需近似松弛即可达到精确隐私保证。

Abstract: This paper introduces the $α$-Wasserstein mechanism for achieving Rényi Pufferfish Privacy using Laplace and Gaussian noise. By leveraging Hölder's inequality, we demonstrate that the scale parameter of the Laplace mechanism can be calibrated via an upper bound on the $W_α$ metric to satisfy $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy for $α\in (1, \infty]$. We show that at the limit $α= \infty$, this framework recovers the established $W_\infty$ mechanism for $ε$-pufferfish privacy. This result is subsequently extended to the exponential mechanism. Furthermore, we propose a $W_α$ mechanism for Gaussian noise for $α\in (1, \infty)$, demonstrating that it generalizes existing results within the Rényi Differential Privacy framework. Experimental evaluations reveal that our $α$-Wasserstein mechanism significantly reduces noise power compared to the conventional $W_\infty$-based approach, with the Gaussian mechanism providing superior utility over the Laplace mechanism. Notably, the mechanisms derived in this work achieve exact $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy without requiring additional relaxations, such as $δ$-approximations.

</details>


### [95] [SuperPaymaster: Eliminating Centralized Signer Authority via Asset-Oriented Abstraction to Reconcile Usability and Decentralization in Account Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05774)
*Huifeng Jiao,Nathapon Udomlertsakul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出资产导向抽象（AOA），通过链上Gas Card替代传统ERC-4337 Paymaster的链下签名流程，实现去中心化赞助。基于Optimism主网的SuperPaymaster实现显示，该方案消除了集中式签名瓶颈，在纯L2执行Gas上比DEX路由的ERC-20基线降低49%，总计费Gas差距主要由打包器定价而非架构引起。故障切换模拟表明可绕过非合作中继器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产级ERC-4337 Paymaster依赖过程导向抽象（POA），即中心化链下服务器签署每笔赞助请求，形成潜在的审查瓶颈。本文旨在通过资产导向抽象（AOA）消除这一中心化签名环节，降低可用性、赞助去中心化与经济效率之间的冲突。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，在Optimism主网实现SuperPaymaster。将赞助有效性锚定在链上Soulbound Token状态和确定性策略规则上，移除链下签名者作为硬性验证关口。通过单UserOp ERC-20转账（n=50）评估Gas成本，使用追踪级分解隔离约32k Gas的集中式签名消除执行成本。

Result: SuperPaymaster在纯L2执行Gas上为167,830，比DEX路由ERC-20基线（328,937）降低49%，比两个商业样本更低。总计费Gas差距主要来自打包器定价。32k Gas delta为消除集中式签名的执行成本。故障切换模拟显示可绕过非合作中继器。

Conclusion: 资产导向抽象（AOA）可以有效减少可用性、赞助去中心化与经济效率之间的冲突，为ERC-4337 Paymaster的去中心化提供了一种可行的架构方案。

Abstract: Most production ERC-4337 Paymasters rely on Process-Oriented Abstraction (POA): a centralized off-chain server signs each sponsorship request and therefore acts as a potential censorship bottleneck. We propose Asset-Oriented Abstraction (AOA), which encapsulates payment capability in a persistent, user-owned on-chain asset -- the ``Gas Card'' -- rather than in an off-chain signing process. Following Design Science Research, we implement SuperPaymaster on Optimism Mainnet. Its sponsorship validity is anchored in on-chain Soulbound Token state and deterministic policy rules, removing the off-chain signer as a hard validity gate. We evaluate gas cost on Optimism Mainnet using single-UserOp ERC-20 transfers ($n{=}50$ per system). Trace-level decomposition isolates an approximately 32k-gas delta as the execution cost of eliminating centralized signing. In pure L2 execution gas, SuperPaymaster (167,830) is lower than both vendor-as-deployed commercial samples, including a 49\% reduction against the DEX-routed ERC-20 baseline (328,937), because it replaces an on-chain liquidation path with an internal balance update. In total billed gas, the remaining gap to the cheapest baseline is explained primarily by bundler pricing rather than paymaster architecture. A failover simulation shows that non-cooperative relayers can be bypassed when an alternative relayer is available. These findings suggest that AOA can reduce the tension among usability, sponsorship decentralization, and economic efficiency.

</details>


### [96] [Stego Battlefield: Evaluating Image Steganography Attacks and Steganalysis Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05789)
*Zhen Sun,Zongmin Zhang,Leyi Sheng,Yule Liu,Yifan Liao,Ke Li,Xinhu Zheng,Jiaheng Wei,Wenyuan Yang,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SADBench是一个系统评估图像隐写攻击与防御能力的基准框架，涵盖4个核心任务，揭示了INN与自编码器方法的稳定性优势、域内检测近乎完美、攻击与检测间的迁移性不对称以及社交媒体上的现实威胁持续存在等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐写术可被 adversaries 用作隐蔽通道绕过内容审核、传播有害语义、甚至向大模型注入恶意指令，但目前缺乏统一、系统的评估框架来衡量这种安全风险。

Method: 构建SADBench基准，包含隐写攻击能力评估、隐写分析防御能力评估、效率评估和迁移性评估4个核心任务，同时评估图像载荷和文本载荷两种隐写方式，覆盖多种封面分布，利用有害视觉语义和有毒指令模拟恶意攻击。

Result: (i) INN和自编码器方法稳定性优于其他架构；(ii) 域内检测近乎完美且成本低于生成；(iii) 攻击可稳健泛化到新分布，但检测器无法适应，存在迁移性不对称；(iv) 社交媒体上载荷能抵抗最小压缩或通过模拟训练适应激进压缩，现实威胁持续存在。

Conclusion: SADBench建立了一个系统、可复现、可扩展的量化风险框架，为隐写防御领域可衡量的安全性改进铺平了道路。

Abstract: Image steganography is widely used to protect user privacy and enable covert communication. However, it can also be abused by the adversary as a covert channel to bypass content moderation, disseminate harmful semantics, and even hide malicious instructions in images to elicit dangerous outputs from large models, posing a practical security risk that continues to evolve. To address the lack of a unified and systematic evaluation framework, we propose SADBench, a systematic benchmark that assesses the adversary's ability to inject harmful secrets via steganography and the defender's ability to detect such threats through steganalysis. Crucially, SADBench comprises $4$ core tasks, namely steganography attack capability evaluation, steganalysis defense capability evaluation, efficiency evaluation, and transferability evaluation. It evaluates both image-payload and text-payload steganography across diverse cover distributions, utilizing harmful visual semantics and toxic instructions to simulate malicious attacks. Across a broad set of attacks and detectors, SADBench reveals that (i) INN and autoencoder-based methods demonstrate superior stability compared to other architectures, (ii) in-domain detection is near-perfect and cheaper than generation, (iii) a critical asymmetry exists in transferability where attacks robustly generalize to new distributions while detectors fail to adapt, and (iv) real-world threats persist on social media, where payloads either survive minimal compression or effectively adapt to aggressive compression via simulated training. Overall, SADBench establishes a systematic, reproducible, and extensible framework to quantify risks, paving the way for measurable and security-driven advancements in steganography defense.

</details>


### [97] [LCC-LLM: Leveraging Code-Centric Large Language Models for Malware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05807)
*Christopher G. Pedraza Pohlenz,Hassan Jalil Hadi,Ali Hassan,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LCC-LLM，一个以代码为中心的基准数据集和基于证据的恶意软件归因框架，包含约3.4万PE样本的多层次逆向工程数据，集成LangGraph编排的静态分析、七层RAG流水线、CoVe的IoC验证及多维质量门控，微调14B和30B模型，在43类任务中平均语义相似度达0.634，真实案例中结构化分析通过率10/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的恶意软件归因存在两大局限：一是受限于不支持的指标（IoC），二是缺乏足够的代码级依据来识别恶意和脆弱代码段，导致归因结果可靠性不足。

Method: 构建LCCD数据集（约3.4万PE样本，含反编译C代码、汇编、CFG/FCG、十六进制数据、PE元数据、可疑API证据及结构特征）；设计LangGraph编排的七层RAG流水线；采用CoVe进行IoC验证；引入多维质量门控提升事实可靠性；使用课程顺序指令数据通过QLoRA微调DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B和Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B。

Result: 在43类恶意软件分析任务中平均语义相似度0.634，结构化报告生成、IoC提取、漏洞评估、恶意软件配置提取和恶意软件类别检测等任务表现最高；真实案例中基于MalwareBazaar样本的结构化分析通过率10/10。

Conclusion: 以代码为中心的表示、检索依据和验证引导的推理能有效提升LLM辅助恶意软件归因的可靠性和实际可用性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly explored for malware analysis; however, current LLM-based malware attribution remains limited by unsupported indicators and insufficient code-level grounding for identifying malicious and vulnerable code segments. To address these limitations, this research introduces LCC-LLM, a code-centric benchmark dataset and evidence-grounded framework for malware attribution and multi-task static malware analysis. The proposed LCCD dataset contains approximately 34K PE samples processed through a large-scale reverse-engineering pipeline and represented using decompiled C code, assembly code, CFG/FCG artifacts, hexadecimal data, PE metadata, suspicious API evidence, and structural features. Beyond dataset construction, LCC-LLM integrates LangGraph-orchestrated static analysis with multi-source cybersecurity knowledge to support evidence-grounded malware reasoning. The framework employs a seven-layer retrieval-augmented generation pipeline, CoVe for IoC validation, and a multi-dimensional quality gate to improve factual reliability and analyst-oriented decision support. Curriculum-ordered instruction data is used to fine-tune DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B and Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B using QLoRA. Evaluation across 43 malware-analysis task types achieves an average semantic similarity of 0.634, with the highest task-level performance in structured report generation, IoC extraction, vulnerability assessment, malware configuration extraction, and malware class detection. In a real-world case study using MalwareBazaar samples, the grounded pipeline achieves a 10/10 structured analysis pass rate, producing CFG/FCG evidence, MITRE ATT&CK mappings, detection guidance, and analyst-ready reports. These results show that code-centric representations, retrieval grounding, and verification-guided reasoning improve the reliability and operational usefulness of LLM-assisted malware attribution.

</details>


### [98] [LeakDojo: Decoding the Leakage Threats of RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05818)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出LeakDojo框架，系统评估RAG系统中的数据库泄露风险，发现查询生成与对抗指令独立贡献泄露风险，强指令遵循能力与更高泄露风险相关，RAG忠实度提升可能增加泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统使LLM能利用外部知识，但同时也暴露了有价值的RAG数据库面临泄露攻击的风险。现有研究未能系统评估RAG泄露风险，尤其是在RAG系统日益复杂、LLM指令遵循能力增强的背景下。

Method: 提出LeakDojo，一个可配置的框架，用于可控地评估RAG泄露。在14个LLM、4个数据集和多种RAG系统上，对6种现有攻击方法进行基准测试。

Result: (1) 查询生成和对抗指令独立贡献于泄露，整体泄露近似于二者的乘积；(2) 更强的指令遵循能力与更高的泄露风险相关；(3) RAG忠实度的提升可能引入增加的泄露风险。

Conclusion: 这些发现为理解和缓解实际中的RAG泄露提供了可操作的见解。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to leverage external knowledge, but also exposes valuable RAG databases to leakage attacks. As RAG systems grow more complex and LLMs exhibit stronger instruction-following capabilities, existing studies fall short of systematically assessing RAG leakage risks. We present LeakDojo, a configurable framework for controlled evaluation of RAG leakage. Using LeakDojo, we benchmark six existing attacks across fourteen LLMs, four datasets, and diverse RAG systems. Our study reveals that (1) query generation and adversarial instructions contribute independently to leakage, with overall leakage well approximated by their product; (2) stronger instruction-following capability correlates with higher leakage risk; and (3) improvements in RAG faithfulness can introduce increased leakage risk. These findings provide actionable insights for understanding and mitigating RAG leakage in practice. Our codebase is available at https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakDojo.

</details>


### [99] [Heimdallr: Characterizing and Detecting LLM-Induced Security Risks in GitHub CI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05969)
*Bonan Ruan,Yeqi Fu,Chuqi Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Jun Zeng,Zhenkai Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了GitHub CI工作流中LLM引入的安全风险，提出了风险分类体系，并设计了混合分析框架Heimdallr用于检测此类威胁，在300个手工标注的工作流上达到高准确率，已负责任披露802个漏洞实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub CI工作流日益集成LLM用于自动化审查、分类、内容生成和仓库维护，这引入了新的攻击面：外部可控的工作流输入可塑造LLM提示和输出，进而影响安全决策、仓库状态或特权执行。尽管LLM安全和CI安全各自被广泛研究，但两者的交叉领域仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 本文设计了Heimdallr混合分析框架，该方法：1）将工作流规范化为LLM-工作流属性图（L-WPG）；2）结合触发可能性分析、LLM辅助的数据流摘要和确定性传播，综合生成具体的威胁向量发现。

Result: 在300个手工标注的工作流上评估，Heimdallr在LLM节点识别（F1=0.994）、触发可能性分类（99.8%）和威胁向量检测（微平均F1=0.917）上达到高准确率。已负责任披露802个漏洞工作流实例（涉及759个仓库），获得71个确认。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统研究了GitHub CI工作流中LLM引入的安全风险，提出了风险分类体系，并设计了有效的检测框架Heimdallr，揭示了此类安全问题的普遍性和严重性。

Abstract: GitHub Continuous Integration (CI) workflows increasingly integrate Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate review, triage, content generation, and repository maintenance. This creates a new attack surface: externally controllable workflow inputs can shape LLM prompts and outputs, which may in turn affect security decisions, repository state, or privileged execution. Although LLM security and CI security have each been studied extensively, their intersection remains underexplored. In this paper, we present the first study of LLM-induced security risks in GitHub CI workflows. We characterize the problem along the full execution chain and develop a taxonomy of high-level risk classes and concrete threat vectors. To detect such risks in practice, we design Heimdallr, a hybrid analysis framework that normalizes workflows into an LLM-Workflow Property Graph (L-WPG) and combines triggerability analysis, LLM-assisted dataflow summarization, and deterministic propagation to synthesize concrete threat-vector findings. Evaluated on 300 manually annotated unique workflows, Heimdallr achieves high accuracy on LLM-node identification (F1~=~0.994), triggerability classification (99.8%), and threat-vector detection (micro-average F1~=~0.917). As part of an ongoing detection and disclosure effort, we have so far responsibly disclosed 802 vulnerable workflow instances across 759 repositories and received 71 acknowledgments.

</details>
