### [100] [LoopTrap: Termination Poisoning Attacks on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05846)
*Huiyu Xu,Zhibo Wang,Wenhui Zhang,Ziqi Zhu,Yaopeng Wang,Kui Ren,Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出终止投毒（Termination Poisoning）攻击，通过恶意提示干扰LLM智能体的自评估循环，使其无法完成终止判断，导致无限计算。基于8个智能体和60个任务的实证研究，发现不同智能体具有可迁移的行为特征。进一步提出自动化红队框架LoopTrap，通过行为画像、自适应陷阱合成和自我改进机制，在8个主流智能体上实现平均3.57倍（最高25倍）的步骤放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过自我评估循环判断任务是否完成，这种自驱动循环在带来自主性的同时也引入了安全风险——攻击者可通过注入恶意提示扭曲智能体的终止判断，使其认为任务未完成而持续执行，造成无限计算消耗。现有研究缺乏对这一威胁的系统性定义和自动化攻防方法。

Method: 1) 定义并系统表征终止投毒攻击，设计10种代表性攻击策略；2) 在8个LLM智能体和60个任务上进行实证研究，分析不同智能体的行为特征与易受攻击模式；3) 提出LoopTrap框架：通过轻量探测构建目标智能体在四个脆弱性维度的行为画像，基于画像自适应选择最有效的攻击策略并自评分优化注入点，成功陷阱存入可复用技能库，失败尝试通过自我反思迭代改进。

Result: 在8个主流智能体上，LoopTrap实现平均3.57倍的步骤数放大，最高达到25倍。

Conclusion: LLM智能体的自评估循环存在可被利用的安全漏洞，不同智能体的行为特征具有可迁移性，可作为攻击设计的指导原则。LoopTrap框架能够自动化地发现和利用这些漏洞，为大规模红队测试提供了超越人工模板的有效方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents solve complex tasks by operating in iterative execution loops, where they repeatedly reason, act, and self-evaluate progress to determine when a task is complete. In this work, we show that while this self-directed loop facilitates autonomy, it also introduces a critical risk: by injecting malicious prompts into the agent's context, an adversary can distort the agent's termination judgment, making it believe the task remains incomplete and leading to unbounded computation.To understand this threat, we define and systematically characterize it as Termination Poisoning and design 10 representative attack strategies. Through a empirical study spanning 8 LLM agents and 60 tasks, we demonstrate that different LLM agents exhibit distinct behavioral signatures that determine which strategies succeed. These transferable patterns can serve as principled guidance for crafting effective attacks against previously unseen agents and tasks, enabling scalable red-teaming beyond manually designed templates. Building on these insights, we introduce LoopTrap, an automated red-teaming framework that synthesizes target-specific malicious prompts by exploiting agent behavioral tendencies. LoopTrap first constructs a behavioral profile of the target agent along four vulnerability dimensions via lightweight probing. It then performs adaptive trap synthesis, routing to the most effective strategy and selecting optimal injections via a self-scoring mechanism. Finally, successful traps are abstracted into a reusable skill library, while failed attempts are refined through self-reflection, ensuring continuous improvement. Extensive evaluation shows that LoopTrap achieves an average of 3.57$\times$ step amplification across 8 mainstream agents, with a peak of 25$\times$.

</details>


### [101] [SkillScope: Toward Fine-Grained Least-Privilege Enforcement for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05868)
*Jiangrong Wu,Yuhong Nan,Yixi Lin,Huaijin Wang,Yuming Xiao,Shuai Wang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillScope是一个针对LLM Agent技能中最小权限违规的细粒度检测与约束框架，通过图分析建模和重放验证实现高精度检测与有效约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能生态中，技能可能执行超出用户当前任务最小必要范围的高影响动作，违反最小权限原则。现有检测方法无法处理这种任务条件性（task-conditioned）问题——同一动作在不同用户提示下可能必要或过度。

Method: SkillScope采用基于图的分析方法，将指令级流程和代码级操作建模为细粒度的动作节点；提取潜在的过度权限候选，通过基于重放的分析在图实例化的用户任务下进行验证，并通过控制流权限约束来限制已验证的过度权限动作。

Result: SkillScope在技能过度权限检测上达到94.53% F1；在真实环境中验证了7,039个存在过度权限行为的技能；在权限约束评估中，将触发的过度权限动作实例减少88.56%，同时保持了合法任务的完成。

Conclusion: SkillScope通过图分析和重放验证，实现了Agent技能中最小权限原则的细粒度执行，在检测和约束过度权限方面效果显著。

Abstract: Agent Skills have become a practical way to extend LLM agents by packaging metadata, natural-language instructions, and executable resources into reusable capability bundles. However, this growing Skill ecosystem introduces a new compliance risk: a Skill may perform high-impact actions that exceed the minimum necessary scope of the user's current task, thereby violating least-privilege. Existing skill detection approaches are insufficient for this problem because it is inherently task-conditioned: the same action may be necessary under one user prompt but over-privileged under another. In this paper, we present SkillScope, a framework for fine-grained least-privilege enforcement in Agent Skills. SkillScope adopts a graph-based analysis approach that models instruction-level procedures and code-level operations as fine-grained action nodes. It extracts potential over-privilege candidates, validates them under graph-instantiated user tasks through replay-based analysis, and constrains validated over-privileged actions via control-flow privilege constraining. We evaluate SkillScope through effectiveness experiments and large-scale real-world measurement. SkillScope achieves 94.53% F1 for skill over-privilege detection. In the wild, SkillScope validates 7,039 Skills with over-privileged behaviors, showing that least-privilege violations are prevalent in current Skill ecosystems. In the privilege-constraining evaluation, SkillScope reduces triggered over-privileged action-in-task instances by 88.56% while preserving legitimate task completion.

</details>


### [102] [PragLocker: Protecting Agent Intellectual Property in Untrusted Deployments via Non-Portable Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05974)
*Qinfeng Li,Yuntai Bao,Jianghui Hu,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Huifeng Zhu,Yier Jin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PragLocker是一种针对LLM智能体提示词的保护方案，通过语义锚定与噪声注入构建功能保持的混淆提示，使其仅能在目标LLM上有效工作，大幅降低跨LLM的可移植性，同时保持目标性能并抵御自适应攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的提示词是宝贵的知识产权，但在不可信部署环境下，攻击者可以复制并复用到其他专有LLM上，造成经济损失。现有方法无法同时满足主动性、运行时保护、可用性和不可移植性四个关键要求。

Method: PragLocker通过两步构建功能保持的混淆提示：1) 使用代码符号锚定语义；2) 利用目标模型反馈注入噪声，使混淆后的提示仅能在目标LLM上正常工作。

Result: 在多个智能体系统、数据集和基础LLM上的实验表明，PragLocker大幅降低了跨LLM的可移植性，同时保持了目标性能，并对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PragLocker有效解决了LLM智能体提示词保护中的四个关键挑战，提供了一种实用的提示词保护方案。

Abstract: LLM agents rely on prompts to implement task-specific capabilities based on foundation LLMs, making agent prompts valuable intellectual property. However, in untrusted deployments, adversaries can copy and reuse these prompts with other proprietary LLMs, causing economic losses. To protect these prompts, we identify four key challenges: proactivity, runtime protection, usability, and non-portability that existing approaches fail to address. We present PragLocker, a prompt protection scheme that satisfies these requirements. PragLocker constructs function-preserving obfuscated prompts by anchoring semantics with code symbols and then using target-model feedback to inject noise, yielding prompts that only work on the target LLM. Experiments across multiple agent systems, datasets, and foundation LLMs show that PragLocker substantially reduces cross-LLM portability, maintains target performance, and remains robust against adaptive attackers.

</details>


### [103] [Safety Anchor: Defending Harmful Fine-tuning via Geometric Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05995)
*Guoxin Lu,Letian Sha,Qing Wang,Peijie Sun,Hao Zhou,Hua Dai,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR)方法，通过将防御焦点从冗余的参数空间转移到作为几何瓶颈的unembedding层，将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的隐藏状态，从而在持续有害微调下仍能维持安全响应。实验表明仅使用单个安全锚即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时保持良性下游任务的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐防御方法在参数、梯度或内部表示上施加约束，但在持续有害微调下可以被有效绕过，原因是高维参数空间存在固有冗余性，攻击者利用与防御约束正交的优化轨迹来恢复有害能力，同时欺骗性地遵守安全限制。

Method: 提出Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR)，将防御焦点从冗余的参数空间转移到unembedding层（几何瓶颈），通过将有害查询的最终隐藏状态锚定到安全对齐模型的最终隐藏状态，使模型在持续有害微调下仍能保持安全响应。

Result: 仅使用单个安全锚即可将有害分数降至10以下，同时在良性下游任务上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SBR通过聚焦于几何瓶颈（unembedding层）而非冗余参数空间，能够有效抵御持续的有害微调攻击，证明了这种防御范式的有效性。

Abstract: The safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains vulnerable to Harmful Fine-tuning (HFT). While existing defenses impose constraints on parameters, gradients, or internal representations, we observe that they can be effectively circumvented under persistent HFT. Our analysis traces this failure to the inherent redundancy of the high-dimensional parameter space: attackers exploit optimization trajectories that are orthogonal to defense constraints to restore harmful capabilities while deceptively adhering to safety restrictions. To address this, we propose Safety Bottleneck Regularization (SBR). SBR shifts the defensive focus from the redundant parameter space to the unembedding layer, which serves as a geometric bottleneck. By anchoring the final hidden states of harmful queries to those of the safety-aligned model, SBR enables the model to maintain safe responses even under persistent HFT. Extensive experiments confirm SBR's effectiveness, demonstrating that utilizing just a single safety anchor is sufficient to reduce the Harmful Score to $<$10 while preserving competitive performance on benign downstream tasks.

</details>


### [104] [Secure Seed-Based Multi-bit Watermarking for Diffusion Models from First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06153)
*Enoal Gesny,Eva Giboulot*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于理论分析的生成式图像水印评估框架，通过将模型依赖部分与决策机制解耦，实现了安全、鲁棒性和保真度的精确比较，并提出了可任意调节三者权衡的SSB水印方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式图像水印技术的评估高度依赖特定模型架构的实证实验，缺乏严格的安全性定义，导致无法对方法性能形成明确结论。

Method: 将水印系统中模型依赖部分与决策机制解耦，建立基于安全性、鲁棒性和保真度的形式化评估框架，通过特征曲面实现系统间精确比较；并在此基础上提出SSB方法，推广了先前的种子嵌入方法。

Result: SSB方法能够在特征曲面上达到任意安全-鲁棒-保真度权衡区间，无需依赖具体生成模型进行昂贵实证评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计具有理论保证的现代水印系统开辟了新路径，消除了对成本高昂的实证评估的依赖。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of generative image models has led to the development of specialized watermarking techniques, particularly in-generation methods such as seed-based embedding. However, current evaluations in this area remain largely empirical, making them heavily reliant on the specific model architectures used for generation and inversion. This prevents any clear conclusion on the performance of any method, especially regarding security, for which a rigorous definition is lacking. Against this approach, we argue that the effectiveness of a watermarking scheme should be established purely through a thorough theoretical analysis. This is enabled by decoupling the model-dependent part from the actual decision mechanism of the watermarking system. Using this decoupling, we introduce a formal evaluation framework based on security, robustness, and fidelity. This allows precise comparisons between watermarking systems through a characteristic surface representing the trade-off between these three quantities, independent of any generative model. Based on this framework, we propose SSB, a novel watermarking method that generalizes previous seed-based methods by allowing to reach any security-robustness-fidelity regime on its characteristic surface. This work opens the door to the design of modern watermarking systems with theoretical guarantees that do not necessitate any costly empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [105] [Stateful Agent Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06158)
*Zhengchunmin Dai,Jiaxiong Tang,Liantao Wu,Peng Sun,Honglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型智能体的**有状态后门攻击**，能够在权限隔离环境下跨多个会话持久化执行攻击。攻击通过持久组件维护状态，单次触发注入后即可自主增量执行。作者将其建模为Mealy机，提出分解框架实现独立数据构建，并在四个模型上取得80%-95%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM智能体的后门攻击是**无状态的**，行为固定且局限于单个会话内，无法在权限隔离的多会话环境中持续执行恶意行为。本文旨在突破这一限制，实现跨会话的持久化后门攻击。

Method: 将攻击建模为**Mealy机**（状态转移系统），提出分解框架实现每个转移的独立数据构建。利用持久化组件（如外部存储、环境变量等）维护攻击状态，单次触发注入后，后续会话可基于当前状态自主执行预设的攻击行为序列。

Result: 主要攻击实例在四个模型上达到**80%-95%的攻击成功率**。每转移分析验证了分解框架的有效性。两种扩展变体（不同拓扑结构和持久组件）也表现出一致的效果。

Conclusion: 有状态后门攻击显著拓展了LLM智能体后门攻击的攻击面，打破了单会话限制，对权限隔离环境下的智能体安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks on Large Language Model-based agents remain stateless, executing fixed behaviors confined to a single session. We propose a stateful agent backdoor that extends the attack lifecycle across multiple sessions under permission isolation. The attack maintains state through persistent components, enabling autonomous, incremental execution across sessions following a one-time trigger injection. Formally, we model the attack as a Mealy machine and derive a decomposition framework that enables independent per-transition data construction. We instantiate this framework with a primary attack and two extensibility variants. The primary instantiation achieves an attack success rate of 80\%--95\% across four models, with per-transition analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of the decomposition. Extensibility variants with alternative topologies and persistent components demonstrate consistent effectiveness. Code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stateful_agent_backdoor-E89F.

</details>


### [106] [Profiling for Pennies: Unveiling the Privacy Iceberg of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06232)
*Jiahao Chen,Qi Zhang,Ruixiao Lin,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Qingming Li,Tong Zhang,Junhao Li,Yuwen Pu,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM集成平台在隐私保护方面的严重不足：平台既未在技术层面也未在政策层面有效解决公众隐私担忧。作者提出了PrivacyIceberg三层隐私风险分类体系，并开发了IcebergExplorer审计工具，仅需最小PII作为搜索种子，即可在10分钟内以低于3美元的成本重建高保真个人档案，事实准确率超过90%。同时识别了导致隐私泄露的六大根源，并提出了针对LLM供应商、个人和数据发布者的多利益相关方应对措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从LLM训练流程出发，强调通过记忆机制暴露个人身份信息（PII），而从以人为本的视角进行的隐私研究仍然不足。作者试图填补这一空白，通过人类意识和LLM集成平台实践的视角，实证研究现实世界中的隐私感知。

Method: 1. 实证调查现实世界中人类隐私感知与LLM集成平台实践之间的差异；2. 提出PrivacyIceberg三层隐私风险分类体系（显式搜索、上下文推断、深度聚合）；3. 开发IcebergExplorer审计工具，以最小PII为种子重建个人档案；4. 识别隐私泄露的六大根本原因。

Result: 平台在技术或政策层面未能解决公众隐私担忧；IcebergExplorer能在10分钟内以低于3美元的成本实现超过90%的事实准确率。

Conclusion: 提出了针对LLM供应商、个人和数据发布者的多利益相关方应对措施，以应对LLM集成平台带来的隐私风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized how information are collected, aggregated, and reasoned. However, this enables a novel and accessible vector of privacy intrusion: the automated and in-depth personal profiling; this engenders a chilling effect of "peepers everywhere". Existing research primarily unfolds from the training pipeline of LLM, emphasizing the exposure of Personally Identifiable Information (PII) through memorization, while privacy studies from a human-centric perspective remain underexplored. To fill this void, we empirically investigate privacy perception in the real world through the lens of human awareness and the practices of LLM-integrated platforms, revealing a significant dissonance: platforms fail to technically or policy-wise address public privacy concerns. To facilitate a systematic and quantifiable study of privacy risk, we propose the PrivacyIceberg, which categorizes real-world human privacy risks into three tiers: explicitly searched, contextually inferred, and deeply aggregated, based on the sophistication of LLM exploitation. We developed IcebergExplorer to audit privacy exposure, utilizing minimal PII as a search seed to reconstruct high-fidelity profiles, achieving over 90% factual accuracy within 10 minutes at a cost under $3, for real-world scenarios. Additionally, we identify six root causes contributing to such privacy disclosures and propose multi-stakeholder countermeasures for LLM vendors, individuals, and data publishers.

</details>


### [107] [Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Solution-Oriented Windows Event Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06330)
*Siraaj Akhtar,Saad Khan,Simon Parkinson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了微调后的小语言模型（SLMs）作为大语言模型（LLMs）的轻量级替代方案，用于事件日志分析并提供可操作的修复建议。通过构建大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集并微调多个模型，实验表明微调后的SLMs在识别问题和提供修复方案上持续优于LLMs，且计算资源需求更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在事件日志分析中虽有潜力，但存在计算开销大、依赖云基础设施以及安全方面的局限，且大多现有方法仅能识别问题而无法提供可操作的修复方案。

Method: 首先使用高性能LLM构建大规模合成Windows事件日志数据集（包含修复动作），然后采用LoRA参数高效微调技术对多个SLMs和LLMs进行微调，最后通过与专家评估对比来评价模型性能。

Result: 构建的数据集准确反映了真实场景；微调后的SLMs在识别问题和提供相关修复方案方面持续优于LLMs，同时所需计算资源更少。

Conclusion: SLMs经过特定任务微调后，可以成为事件日志分析中LLMs的实用轻量级替代方案，既能识别问题又能生成解决方案，且计算效率更高。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for event log analysis, but their high computational requirements, reliance on cloud infrastructure, and security concerns limit practical deployment. In addition, most existing approaches focus only on the identification of the problem and do not provide actionable remediation. Small language models (SLMs) present a light-weight alternative that can be fine-tuned for a specific purpose and hosted locally. This paper investigates whether SLMs, when fine-tuned for a specific task, can serve as a practical alternative for event log analysis while also generating solutions. We first create a large-scale synthetic Windows event log dataset that contains remediation actions using a high-performing LLM. We then fine-tune multiple SLMs and LLMs using the LoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning technique and evaluate their performance by comparing with expert assessment. The results show that the dataset accurately reflects real-world scenarios and that fine-tuned SLMs consistently outperform LLMs in identifying issues and providing relevant remediation, while requiring fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [108] [Pop Quiz Attack: Black-box Membership Inference Attacks Against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06423)
*Zeyuan Chen,Yihan Ma,Xinyue Shen,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PopQuiz Attack，一种针对大语言模型的黑盒成员推理攻击方法，通过将目标数据转化为选择题形式来检测模型是否记住了特定训练数据。在六个主流LLM和四个数据集上，该方法平均ROC-AUC达0.873，比现有方法提升20.6%，并揭示了现有防御措施的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在众多应用中表现优异，但其记忆训练数据的能力引发了严重的隐私担忧。现有成员推理攻击方法效果有限，尤其是在黑盒场景下，因此需要更有效的攻击方法来评估和暴露LLM的隐私风险。

Method: 提出PopQuiz Attack，一种黑盒成员推理攻击方法。核心思路是将目标数据转换为选择题风格的多选题，通过分析模型对这些问题的回答来推断数据是否属于训练集。该方法无需访问模型内部参数，仅需模型输出。

Result: 在GPT-3.5、GPT-4o、LLaMA2-7b、LLaMA2-13b、Mistral-7b和Vicuna-7b六个模型以及四个数据集上，平均ROC-AUC达到0.873，比现有方法提升20.6%。攻击成功受到查询复杂度、数据类型、数据结构和训练设置等因素影响。

Conclusion: 现代大语言模型存在持续且显著的隐私漏洞。现有的基于指令、滤波和差分隐私的防御措施虽能降低攻击性能，但无法彻底消除隐私风险，表明LLM的隐私保护仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong performance across many applications, but their ability to memorize and potentially reveal training data raises serious privacy concerns. We introduce the PopQuiz Attack, a black-box membership inference attack that tests whether a model can recall specific training examples. The core idea is to turn target data into quiz-style multiple-choice questions and infer membership from the model's answers. Across six widely used LLMs (GPT-3.5, GPT-4o, LLaMA2-7b, LLaMA2-13b, Mistral-7b, and Vicuna-7b) and four datasets, our method achieves an average ROC-AUC of 0.873 and outperforms existing approaches by 20.6%. We further analyze factors affecting attack success, including query complexity, data type, data structure, and training settings. We also evaluate instruction-based, filter-based, and differential privacy-based defenses, which reduce performance but do not eliminate the risk. Our results highlight persistent privacy vulnerabilities in modern LLMs.

</details>


### [109] [Autonomous Adversary: Red-Teaming in the age of LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06486)
*Mohammad Mamun,Mohamed Gaber,Scott Buffett,Sherif Saad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了语言模型智能体（LMA）在红队行动中的能力，特别是横向移动场景。通过对比三种操作模式（全自主执行、自构计划、专家定义行动计划），发现专家定义模式任务完成率最高，但所有模式均存在频繁失败，主要源于命令调用脆弱、环境不稳定、凭证管理和状态处理错误等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LMA在红队攻防操作中的潜力，特别是支持攻击计划、对手模拟和横向移动等多步骤APT活动的能力，并对其治理和评估方法进行研究。

Method: 基于MITRE ATT&CK框架分析LMA与核心攻击功能的交集；在受控对手模拟环境中，设计两种横向移动场景，形式化为有序任务链并设置显式验证谓词，采用LLM-as-a-Judge范式确保确定性结果验证；比较三种操作模式（全自主执行、自构计划、专家定义行动计划）。

Result: 专家定义行动计划的任务完成率高于其他模式；但所有模式均频繁失败，主要原因包括命令调用脆弱、环境和部署不稳定、凭证管理和状态处理错误。

Conclusion: 当前LMA在红队横向移动场景中的能力有限，尽管专家定义模式表现较好，但整体可靠性不足，需要在命令执行、环境稳定性和状态管理等方面进行改进。

Abstract: Language Model Agents (LMAs) are emerging as a powerful primitive for augmenting red-team operations. They can support attack planning, adversary emulation, and the orchestration of multi-step activity such as lateral movement, a core enabling capability of advanced persistent threat (APT) campaigns. Using frameworks such as MITRE ATT&CK, we analyze where these agents intersect with core offensive functions and assess current strengths and limitations of LMAs with an emphasis on governance and realistic evaluation. We benchmark LMAs across two lateral-movement scenarios in a controlled adversary-emulation environment, where LMAs interact with instrumented cyber agents, observe execution artifacts, and iteratively adapt based on environmental feedback. Each scenario is formalized as an ordered task chain with explicit validation predicates, leveraging an LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm to ensure deterministic outcome verification.
  We compare three operational modalities: fully autonomous execution, self-scaffolded planning, and expert-defined action plans. Preliminary findings indicate that expert-defined action plans yield higher task-completion rates relative to other operational modes. However, failure remains frequent across all modalities, largely attributable to brittle command invocation, environmental and deployment instability, and recurring errors in credential management and state handling.

</details>


### [110] [Privacy by Postprocessing the Discrete Laplace Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06502)
*Quentin Hillebrand,Jacob Imola,Rasmus Pagh,Sia Sejer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明经典离散拉普拉斯机制（几何机制）可通过后处理实现：(1) 对任意次指数函数 f 的无偏估计，(2) 输出与拉普拉斯或阶梯机制相同的分布，从而成为离散数据场景下优于后两者的通用机制。尽管最坏情况下指数时间，但对常见函数可实现线性/多项式时间计算，并通过实验展示了在轮廓估计、熵估计及联邦学习等场景中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为离散拉普拉斯机制不如连续拉普拉斯机制灵活，本文旨在证明该机制通过后处理能实现无偏估计和分布匹配等新功能，从而在离散数据场景下成为更优选择。

Method: 对离散拉普拉斯机制的输出进行后处理：(1) 设计无偏估计器，适用于任意次指数函数 f；(2) 通过后处理使输出分布与拉普拉斯机制或阶梯机制一致。分析估计器方差并与有偏估计的均方误差对比，并针对常见结构化函数给出多项式时间算法。

Result: 离散拉普拉斯机制经后处理可实现无偏估计和分布匹配，方差上界优于有偏估计的均方误差；优化后对常见函数可在线性/多项式时间内计算；在轮廓估计、熵估计和联邦分析中表现出色。

Conclusion: 离散拉普拉斯机制是离散数据（或可离散化且可控 ℓ₁ 灵敏度）场景下的通用机制，应优先于拉普拉斯和阶梯机制使用。

Abstract: We show that an "old dog", the classical discrete Laplace (aka.~geometric) mechanism, can "perform new tricks":
  1. It can be post-processed to yield a simple, unbiased estimator of any subexponential function $f$ of the original data, giving a simple, discrete, multivariate version of the recent unbiasing result for the Laplace mechanism by Calmon et al. (FORC '25).
  2. It can be post-processed to output the same distribution as the Laplace mechanism or the Staircase mechanism with identical privacy parameters.
  Thus, the discrete Laplace mechanism is a versatile mechanism that should be preferred over the Laplace and Staircase mechanisms whenever the data is discrete (or can be made discrete while controlling $\ell_1$-sensitivity).
  We show bounds on the variance of our estimator, compared to the mean square error of the biased estimator that simply evaluates the $f$ on the output of the mechanism. Though our unbiased estimator has exponential running time for worst-case functions, we show that it can often be computed in linear or polynomial time for some common functions exhibiting structure. We showcase the properties of our methods empirically with several use cases including profile and entropy estimation, as well as distributed/federated data analysis applications in which unbiasedness is key to accuracy.

</details>


### [111] [On the Security of Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06508)
*Nanda Rani,Christian Rossow*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了509个顶级安全会议的研究工件，发现其中41.60%存在安全风险代码模式。提出了一套上下文感知安全评估分类法，并开发了SAFE框架，该框架通过分析代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性，以84.80%的准确率和84.63%的F1分数自动区分安全风险与非安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工件评估（AE）主要关注可复现性，忽视了公开发布和复用的工件可能存在的安全风险（如恶意代码模式），导致研究工件可能被误用，需要系统性的安全评估方法。

Method: 收集509个顶级安全会议的研究工件，通过静态分析检测不安全代码模式；提出上下文感知安全评估分类法进行结构化风险分析；开发SAFE框架，结合代码语义、执行上下文和实际可利用性对工具报告的安全发现进行自动分类。

Result: 41.60%的常见工件发现存在安全风险；SAFE框架在区分安全与非安全风险上达到84.80%准确率和84.63%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 安全性应成为工件评估（AE）的重要考量维度，以促进安全且负责任的研究共享；SAFE框架为自动化安全评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Research artifacts are widely shared to support reproducibility, and artifact evaluation (AE) has become common at many leading conferences. However, AE mainly checks whether artifacts work as claimed and can be reproduced. It largely overlooks potential security risks. Since these artifacts are publicly released and reused, they may unintentionally create opportunities for misuse and raise concerns about safe and responsible sharing. We study 509 research artifacts from top-tier security venues and find that many contain insecure code patterns that may introduce potential attack vectors. We propose a taxonomy for context-aware security assessment to enable structured analysis of such risks. We perform static analysis and examine the resulting findings, filtering false positives and identifying real security risks. Our analysis shows that 41.60% of the prevalent findings may pose security concerns under practical usage. To support scalable analysis, we introduce SAFE (Security-Aware Framework for Artifact Evaluation), a first step toward an autonomous framework that analyzes tool-reported findings by considering code semantics, execution context, and practical exploitability. SAFE achieves 84.80% accuracy and 84.63% F1-score in distinguishing security and non-security risks. Overall, our results show that security is also important in AE for promoting safe and responsible research sharing. The source code is available at: https://github.com/nanda-rani/SAFE

</details>


### [112] [FedAttr: Towards Privacy-preserving Client-Level Attribution in Federated LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06596)
*Su Zhang,Junfeng Guo,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedAttr，一种联邦学习场景下的客户端级水印归因协议，通过配对子集差分机制在保护安全聚合隐私的同时，准确识别哪些客户端使用了带水印数据训练模型。理论证明其无偏估计与隐私保障，实验实现100%真阳性率和0%假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印放射性测试方法在集中式LLM微调中有效，但在联邦学习场景下面临挑战：安全聚合机制保护了客户端隐私，却也使得服务器无法识别具体哪个客户端使用了带水印数据进行训练，该问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: FedAttr包含三步：(i) 通过两次安全聚合查询的差分来估计每个客户端的更新；(ii) 利用差分评分机制对估计结果进行水印检测评分；(iii) 通过Stouffer方法跨轮次组合分数。理论上证明了该估计是无偏的且每轮互信息泄露有界(O(d*/N))。

Result: FedAttr在实验中达到100%真阳性率和0%假阳性率，在真阳性率或假阳性率上至少比所有基线方法提升44.4%或19.1%，且额外开销仅为FL训练时间的6.3%。消融研究证实其对协议参数和配置具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FedAttr成功将水印放射性测试方法扩展到联邦学习场景，在保持安全聚合隐私保障和FL性能的前提下，实现了高效准确的客户端级水印归因，为联邦学习中的数据所有权保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Watermark radioactivity testing type of methods can detect whether a model was trained on watermarked documents, and have become key tools for protecting data ownership in the fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Existing works have proved their effectiveness in centralized LLM fine-tuning. However, this type of method faces several challenges and remains underexplored in federated learning (FL), a widely-applied paradigm for fine-tuning LLMs collaboratively on private data across different users. FL mainly ensures privacy through secure aggregation (SA), which allows the server to aggregate updates while keeping clients' updates private. This mechanism preserves privacy but makes it difficult to identify which client trained on watermarked documents. In this work, we propose FedAttr, a new client-level attribution protocol for FL. FedAttr identifies which clients trained on watermarked data via a paired-subset-difference mechanism, while preserving the privacy guarantees of SA and FL performance. FedAttr proceeds in three steps: (i) estimate each client's update by differencing two SA queries, (ii) score the estimate with the watermark detector via differential scoring, and (iii) combine scores across rounds via Stouffer method. We theoretically show that FedAttr produces an unbiased estimator of each client's update with bounded mutual information leakage (i.e., $O(d^*/N)$ per-round update). Moreover, FedAttr empirically achieves 100% TPR and 0% FPR, outperforming all baselines by at least 44.4% in TPR or 19.1% in FPR, with only 6.3% overhead relative to FL training time. Ablation studies confirm that FedAttr is robust to protocol parameters and configurations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在理论上统一了无监督预训练中的互信息技能学习（MISL）与目标条件强化学习（GCRL），揭示两者均属于控制最大化框架，证明了三种GCRL公式本质不等价但共享目标敏感性解释，并建立了MISL目标与下游GCRL任务之间的精确对应关系，为强化学习预训练奠定了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督预训练在GCRL中取得了实证成功，但理论基础薄弱。MISL方法学到的技能为何能支持目标达成任务是一个理论上的谜团，且GCRL和MISL作为统称术语内部存在多种不同定义，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们之间的关系。

Method: 将GCRL和MISL统一归纳为控制最大化问题；识别三种典型GCRL公式化表述并证明其本质不等价性；引入"敏感性"概念统一解释不同GCRL公式；证明MISL目标受下游目标敏感性的界约束，建立MISL方法与下游GCRL任务间的精确对应关系。

Result: 证明了三种GCRL公式化表述在同一个环境中可能诱导不相容的最优策略；揭示了所有GCRL公式的共同本质是目标策略对未来轨迹对指令目标的高度敏感性；建立了每个GCRL公式与特定MISL目标之间的配对关系，使得更多样化的技能带来更高的下游目标敏感性。

Conclusion: 为强化学习预训练提供了理论基础，具有重要的实践意义：当用户关心特定类型的下游任务时，可以据此选择合适的预训练目标。理论表明，不同GCRL任务需要匹配不同MISL预训练目标。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC）框架，用于流式数据下的在线贝叶斯校准，解决模拟器不匹配和非平稳性问题，通过分离无差异粒子更新与条件高斯过程差异更新保持可辨识性，并集成重启机制处理突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典贝叶斯校准存在参数-差异混杂、离线假设及无法处理非平稳演化（渐变漂移与突变）的局限，而数据同化方法缺乏显式系统偏差建模，难以应对突变场景，亟需在线偏差感知校准框架。

Method: BRPC将投影校准扩展至在线场景：(1) 分离无差异粒子更新（校准参数）与条件高斯过程更新（差异函数），保持参数可辨识性；(2) 集成重启机制检测突变并重置校准过程；(3) 建立渐变跟踪性能与重启检测虚警/检测行为的理论保证。

Result: 合成数据与植物仿真基准实验表明：BRPC在渐变场景下提升校准精度；重启增强版BRPC在突变场景下相比滑动窗口贝叶斯校准与数据同化基线，显著提升鲁棒性与预测性能。

Conclusion: BRPC为数字孪生中在线、偏差感知、适应非平稳系统的贝叶斯校准提供了理论完备且实验有效的框架。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [115] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征筛选流水线和人口统计学分层模型，预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）诊断，最终达到0.8461的AUC，证明常规EHR数据可支持人群代表性的CRS风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRS是一种常见且异质性高的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与过敏性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且表型异质性掩盖了风险模式。既往预测研究多依赖单一机构队列，缺乏人群层面的泛化能力。

Method: 利用《All of Us》全国纵向EHR数据，采用混合特征筛选流水线（结合基于患病率的统计筛选与基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征；针对六组成年人性别-生命阶段亚组训练人口统计学分层模型，并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达0.8461，比最佳基线模型提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于在初级保健中实现更早的分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [116] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出顺序智能体微调（SAT），一种无协调器的多智能体训练范式，通过块坐标更新和序列感知的优势估计器实现多个小LLM的协作，在理论上保证单调改进和可插拔不变性，经验上三个4B智能体（总计12B）在AIME24/25上超越Qwen3-32B 3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽有强性能但部署成本过高，现有工作探索使用多个小型LLM协作来匹敌或超越单个大模型，但联合更新多个智能体会引入复合分布偏移，导致训练协调困难和稳定性问题。

Method: 提出SAT（Sequential Agent Tuning），将团队表示为因子化策略，采用块坐标更新（block-coordinate updates）实现无协调器的分散式训练；开发了序列感知的on-policy优势估计器，条件于动态演化的团队策略；结合每个智能体的KL信任域以隔离占据漂移。

Result: 三个4B智能体（总计12B参数）在AIME24/25基准上超越Qwen3-32B平均3.9%；将两个智能体替换为8B版本后，组合得分提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架提供了单调改进的理论保证和可证明的即插即用不变性（任意智能体可升级为更强模型而无需重新训练团队其余部分），验证了多智能体协作训练的可扩展性和稳定性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [117] [Evolutionary fine tuning of quantized convolution-based deep learning models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05228)
*Marcin Pietroń*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用进化策略优化预训练量化模型的权重分配，通过迭代调整少量权重的量化状态来提升模型精度，在VGG、ResNet和自编码器上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在IoT、移动设备等场景中受限于复杂度和内存大小，量化是最流行的压缩技术之一，但基于最近邻取整的量化方法无法保证最优精度，因此需要更高效的量化优化方法。

Method: 采用进化策略作为优化手段，每次迭代只改变一小部分权重的值，将其移动到不同的量化状态，通过适当的算子与参数设置快速提升量化模型的准确率。

Result: 在VGG、ResNet等图像分类与检测任务以及自编码器架构上进行了仿真实验，结果表明所提方法能快速提升量化模型的精度。

Conclusion: 基于最近邻取整的最终量化状态并非最优，进化策略能够有效优化权重在量化状态间的分配，显著改善量化后模型的准确性。

Abstract: Deep learning models are the most efficient models in many machine learning tasks. The main disadvantage when using them in IoT, mobile devices, independent autonomous or real-time systems is their complexity and memory size. Therefore, much research has concentrated on compression techniques of deep learning architectures. One of the most popular technique is quantization. In most of the works, the quantization is done based on the nearest neighbour quantization technique. This work focuses on improving the quantization efficiency in pretrained and quantized models. This approach has the potential to improve the final accuracy of quantized models. The main postulate of the work is that final quantization states of the network based on nearest neighbour rounding does not guarantee optimal accuracy. In the presented work, the evolution strategy is used as an optimization approach. The evolution in each iteration changes the values of the small percentage of weights. It shifts theirs values to different quantization states. The work shows that proposed evolution with an appropriate set of operators and parameters can fast improve the accuracy of the quantized models. The results are presented for popular architectures such as VGG and Resnet for image classification and detection. Additionally, simulations were carried out for the autoencoder architecture.

</details>


### [118] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立混沌理论与预测多重性之间的严格理论联系，提出面向混沌系统的时域约束Rashomon集框架，通过Lyapunov指数刻画模型多样性随预测时域的变化规律，并开发基于下游效用的决策对齐模型选择算法，在多个混沌系统中将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（预测等价模型的共存）与混沌动力学（初始条件敏感依赖）是机器学习中两个独立发展的基本挑战，但它们在概念上具有深层联系——混沌系统中预测模型随时间的指数发散会根本性地改变预测等价性的本质。现有研究未能桥接这两个领域，缺乏在混沌动态环境下指导模型选择的理论原则。

Method: 1) 提出时域约束Rashomon集（horizon-constrained Rashomon sets）理论框架，刻画混沌系统中预测模型多样性随预测时域的演化规律；2) 证明有效Rashomon集以最大Lyapunov指数确定的速率随提前时间指数收缩；3) 引入Lyapunov加权度量，为预测分歧提供更紧的界；4) 开发决策对齐模型选择算法，基于下游效用而非单纯预测精度从近似最优模型中选择。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等实际应用中，所提框架将决策质量提升18-34%，同时保持具有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严格理论连接，为在安全关键的混沌领域中部署机器学习提供了原理性指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [119] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过在线性注意力中引入动量机制和分块并行算法，解决了线性递归中信息快速衰减和收敛次优的问题，在400M和1.3B参数规模上取得了优于Mamba2、GDN和Transformer的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型（如Mamba2、GDN）将线性递归视为闭式在线SGD，但朴素SGD更新存在信息快速衰减和收敛次优的问题。动量优化器虽能自然缓解，但难以同时保证训练效率和效果。

Method: 1) 提出基于动量规则的步进式分块并行算法，通过几何重排更新系数实现高效训练；2) 从动力系统视角将动量递归分析为二阶系统，引入复共轭特征值，据此设计稳定门控约束；3) 基于Triton内核实现，保持与Mamba2、KDA相当的训练吞吐量。

Result: 在400M和1.3B参数模型上的大量实验表明，MDN在多种下游评估基准上持续优于Transformer、Mamba2和GDN等强基线模型。

Conclusion: 动量机制能有效改善线性注意力中的信息衰减和收敛问题，结合分块并行算法可在不牺牲训练效率的前提下提升模型性能，为线性注意力模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [120] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了概念操控的形式化理论框架，证明了标准概念擦除是LEACE的特例，并在此基础上提出了更一般的概念操控方法MidSteer，实现了有向最小干扰变换，在多种任务、模态和架构上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前中间表示操控（steering）在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了实证成功，但缺乏全面的理论框架。

Method: 1. 建立操控与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的理论联系；2. 提出概念切换的理论框架LEACE-Switch，刻画其最优仿射解的假设条件；3. 提出更一般的仿射框架MidSteer，放宽假设条件，实现有向最小干扰变换。

Result: MidSteer在视觉扩散模型和大型语言模型等多种任务、模态和架构上表现出色。

Conclusion: 填补了概念操控缺乏理论框架的空白，提出了更通用且有效的概念操控方法MidSteer。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [121] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种训练递归智能体的强化学习方法，使智能体能够递归地生成子任务并委派给新实例，通过分治策略实现推理时扩展，在训练效率、上下文窗口扩展、任务泛化及运行时间方面均优于单智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体系统在处理长上下文和复杂任务时面临扩展瓶颈，需要一种能够通过递归分治策略自然扩展推理能力的智能体训练方法。

Method: 提出RAO，一种强化学习框架，训练智能体在推理时递归生成子智能体并委派子任务，通过强化学习教会智能体何时以及如何委派和通信。

Result: 递归智能体在训练效率、超出模型上下文窗口的任务扩展、更难任务的泛化能力以及实际运行时间方面均优于单智能体系统。

Conclusion: RAO是一种有效的训练递归智能体的方法，能够通过递归分治策略实现更好的扩展性、泛化能力和效率。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [122] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了TurboQuant与早期工作DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021)和EDEN (ICML 2022)之间的关系。TurboQuant是EDEN的特例(S=1)且次优，biased EDEN优于TurboQuant_mse，unbiased EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod（通常节省1比特以上）。实验全面验证EDEN在所有设置下均优于TurboQuant。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清TurboQuant与EDEN/DRIVE之间的关系，指出TurboQuant的方法实质上是EDEN的特例且存在多处次优选择，纠正可能存在的文献混淆。

Method: 通过理论分析指出：1) TurboQuant_mse是EDEN在固定标量尺度参数S=1时的特例；2) TurboQuant_prod将biased (b-1)-bit EDEN与unbiased 1-bit QJL残差量化链式组合，存在三处次优选择；3) 指出TurboQuant的分析方法与EDEN存在相似性。并通过实验对比验证。

Result: biased EDEN（优化S）准确率高于TurboQuant_mse；unbiased EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod（2-bit EDEN优于3-bit TurboQuant_prod）；在所有实验设置中EDEN均优于TurboQuant。

Conclusion: TurboQuant是EDEN的次优特例，EDEN在理论和实验上均全面优于TurboQuant。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [123] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏自编码器（SAE）中特征组合的不稳定性问题，提出了一个几何分析框架。通过将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，在球面字典模型下推导了组合崩塌的渐近阈值，揭示了ReLU整流在组合中产生的棘轮效应，并在CLEVR数据集上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器虽能有效解耦特征叠加并实现激活控制，但组合式特征激活（同时激活多个语义隐变量）的理论基础尚未充分探索。现有线性表示假设忽略了过完备字典中存在的非线性干扰效应，亟需理论层面的解释与约束分析。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，基于球面字典模型，利用高斯平均宽度（统计维度）推导组合崩塌的渐近阈值；分析高偏置下ReLU整流将微观相关性引起的方差波动转化为系统性漂移，形成棘轮效应累积干扰。

Result: 推导了组合式特征激活的崩塌阈值；证明了ReLU整流引入的棘轮效应导致干扰随组合数量累积增长；在CLEVR数据集上验证了层级相关性会加速组合崩塌的转变过程。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于特征组合的激活控制的可扩展性受到几何约束，指出需要设计能够显式管理干扰的机制，而非依赖简单的线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [124] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机化Hadamard变换（RHT）在替代均匀随机旋转（URR）时的理论保证。证明了两层RHT可使坐标边际分布接近高斯分布，在压缩方案中渐近匹配URR；三层RHT可确保向量量化（VQ）的协方差衰减；并提出了基于输入矩的线性时间自适应方法动态调整RHT层数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URR是量化预处理中的常见步骤，实践中常用RHT替代以利用快速算法，但单个RHT在最坏情况下性能不佳，单个坐标分布可能远离期望的beta/高斯分布。需要研究多个RHT组合能否弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 理论分析两层RHT后坐标边际分布与标准高斯的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离上界（O(d^{-1/2})）；分析三层RHT的坐标协方差衰减；提出基于输入矩的线性时间自适应检测方法。

Result: 两层RHT在DRIVE和QUIC-FL等压缩方案中渐近匹配URR性能；三层RHT确保VQ码本的期望误差与URR相差一个随维度消失的加性项；线性时间检测可动态调整RHT层数。

Conclusion: 多层RHT组合能够弥补单层RHT在最坏情况下的不足，不同应用场景需要不同层数保证；实际输入通常非对抗，可通过高效检测自适应调整。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [125] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现预训练权重会削弱现有UEs方法的有效性，并提出了一种分层欺骗策略（SSC）来克服这一限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可学习示例（UEs）研究主要评估从零训练的设定，对其在广泛使用的预训练-微调（PF）范式下的行为缺乏探索。

Method: 提出浅层语义伪装（SSC）策略，通过将生成过程限制在语义有效子空间中，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。

Result: SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练（SF-Pretrain）等具有挑战性的训练范式下，仍能一致地保持数据的不可学习性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了基于预训练的不可学习学习中的关键空白，为保护个人数据在模型训练中的隐私提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [126] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLAD是一个结合了聚类联邦学习(CFL)和新型双模式微架构(DM²A)的物联网入侵检测框架，通过联合无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类，有效解决了设备异质性和标签稀缺两大瓶颈，在80%无标签客户端场景下实现了30%的检测性能提升和一半的通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习入侵检测系统难以泛化到异构设备行为，且无法利用边缘环境中大量未标记数据，亟需一种既能处理设备异质性又能利用无标签数据的统一框架。

Method: 提出CLAD框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 双模式微架构(DM²A)，由共享编码器和两个分支组成，同时支持无监督异常检测和有监督攻击分类；(2) 动态聚类组件，将流量模式相似的设备分组，防止全局模型发散。两者结合使框架能从有标签和无标签客户端同时学习。

Result: 在80%客户端无标签的场景下，相比基线方法实现30%的检测性能相对提升，且通信成本减半。

Conclusion: 通过精心结合聚类联邦学习和双模式架构，CLAD确保不丢弃任何数据并保留不同设备的操作模式，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [127] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的效率瓶颈（拖后腿效应），提出MACS框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制，分别解决视觉token信息异构性和模态动态性导致的负载不均衡问题，无需重新训练即可显著提升多模态基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中因拖后腿效应存在严重效率瓶颈，现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法处理两个独特挑战：视觉token的信息异构性（冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待）和模态动态性（不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配）。

Method: 提出MACS（Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling）训练无关推理框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵加权负载机制（Entropy-Weighted Load），通过量化视觉token的语义价值来解决信息异构性；(2) 动态模态自适应容量机制（Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity），根据输入的实时模态组成分配专家资源。

Result: 在多种多模态基准上，MACS显著优于现有方法，为MoE多模态大模型在专家并行推理中的高效部署提供了鲁棒解决方案。

Conclusion: MACS是一种无需训练的高效推理框架，通过考虑模态信息的语义价值和实时模态组成来优化专家并行负载均衡，有效解决了MoE多模态大模型的效率瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [128] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种监督内化方法，通过让模型自动从失败推理轨迹中识别、修正和重用过程级学习信号，实现了在仅有结果监督条件下对推理策略进行细粒度优化，并将这一思想抽象为一种新的训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理强化学习面临的核心挑战不仅是结果级监督的稀疏性，更在于如何将序列末端反馈转化为可指导中间推理步骤的细粒度学习信号。现有方法要么依赖结果级奖励进行序列级优化（难以精确信用分配），要么依赖外部构建的过程监督（成本高且难以持续扩展）。

Method: 将推理强化学习重新理解为"将结果监督内化为过程监督"的问题，提出监督内化方法：模型通过自动识别、修正和重用失败的推理轨迹，从中提取过程级学习信号，在仅有结果监督的条件下实现更细粒度的策略优化。

Result: 实现了在仅有结果监督条件下的细粒度策略优化，为推理强化学习中的信用分配提供了新路径。

Conclusion: 该工作将监督内化思想抽象为一种新的训练范式，模型在强化学习过程中持续生成并精炼自身的过程监督，开辟了一条不同于外部过程监督的细粒度信用分配新路径。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [129] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT，一种在线数据重加权框架，通过自适应每个样本的学习率来动态调整训练过程中的样本重要性，替代了传统的离线数据筛选和混合方法。实验表明，在指令微调和大规模预训练中，ADAPT在相同计算量下持续优于离线方法和先前在线方法，展现出更强的跨基准泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练中的数据筛选和混合方法采用离线范式，与训练过程脱节，存在工程开销大、模型/任务迁移时需要重新运行整个流程的问题。此外，离线方法通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，常以牺牲数据多样性为代价，损害模型泛化能力。

Method: ADAPT将数据筛选重新定义为在线重加权问题，在训练过程中通过基于相似性的质量信号为每个样本自适应调整学习率（即损失加权），动态调整样本重要性，而不改变训练样本数量。该方法作为隐式课程学习器，随模型演变逐步从粗粒度模式转向细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在相同FLOPs下一致优于离线数据筛选/混合方法以及先前的在线方法，实现了更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: ADAPT作为一种动态在线数据重加权框架，有效解决了离线数据管线的脆弱性问题，通过隐式课程学习机制在保持数据多样性的同时提升了模型泛化性能，为LLM训练中的数据策展提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [130] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过估计每个Transformer层中参数的任务特定重要性来调节梯度，缓解LLM在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法（数据回放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLM内部知识分布的语义感知能力，无法区分哪些参数应当保留或更新，导致灾难性遗忘问题严重。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架：估计每个Transformer层中任务特定的逐参数重要性分数，利用这些分数调节梯度——对先前任务重要的参数更新幅度小，不重要的参数保持塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准上，该方法一致优于基线方法，在更好地保留旧任务知识的同时，保持新任务的竞争性表现。

Conclusion: 归因引导的参数重要性估计能有效区分需要保留和更新的参数，实现旧任务知识保持与新任务学习之间的平衡，为LLM持续学习提供了一种无需数据回放的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [131] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法提升7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现需要找到与目标蛋白强结合且选择性高的配体，但候选配体通过率不足5%，且希望方法能适用于无先验数据的新型蛋白质，因此需要高效迭代筛选算法以减少测试次数。

Method: 提出SPADE算法，采用新颖的配体选择策略，从零开始迭代选择和测试候选配体，旨在用尽可能少的测试找到足够多的高质量配体。

Result: SPADE平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体；在逐项比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度方面均显著优于现有方法，为药物发现中的配体筛选提供了一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [132] [Conditional Diffusion Under Linear Constraints: Langevin Mixing and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05387)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar,Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于预训练扩散模型的零样本条件采样（如图像修复、超分辨率），分析了现有投影方法因忽略切线方向分数匹配导致的偏差问题，提出了基于法向-切向分解的信息论误差上界，并设计了投影Langevin初始化+引导反向去噪的新方法，实验效果优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影式条件采样方法仅强制测量一致性，但未正确分配可行集上的概率质量，导致条件采样存在偏差。作者旨在从理论上分析偏差来源并设计无偏的零样本条件采样方法。

Method: 1. 对分数函数进行法向-切向分解，证明观测方向分数由测量精确确定，仅切向条件分数未知。2. 证明用无条件切向分数替代条件切向分数的误差上界由条件互信息（观测与未观测分量之间）控制，该互信息与维度无关。3. 基于理论提出投影Langevin初始化 + 引导反向去噪方法。

Result: 在图像修复和超分辨率实验中，所提方法优于强投影基线方法。

Conclusion: 条件采样偏差可归因于初始化和路径上的分数不匹配误差，投影Langevin初始化能有效缓解这一问题，信息论视角为设计更好的条件扩散采样器提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study zero-shot conditional sampling with pretrained diffusion models for linear inverse problems, including inpainting and super-resolution. In these problems, the observation determines only part of the unknown signal. The remaining degrees of freedom must be sampled according to the correct conditional data distribution. Existing projection-based samplers enforce measurement consistency by correcting the observed component during reverse diffusion. However, measurement consistency alone does not determine how probability mass should be distributed along the feasible set, and this can lead to biased conditional samples.
  We analyze this issue through a normal--tangent decomposition of the score function. For Gaussian noising, the observed-direction score is exactly determined by the measurement; only the tangent conditional score is unknown. We prove that the error from replacing this score by the unconditional tangent score is upper bounded by a dimension-free conditional mutual information between observed and unobserved components. This gives an information-theoretic decomposition into initialization and pathwise score-mismatch errors. Motivated by the theory, we propose a projected-Langevin initialization followed by guided reverse denoising, which outperforms a strong projection-based baseline in inpainting and super-resolution experiments.

</details>


### [133] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，将多图VRP中的路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，通过预编码边聚合、非自回归架构和分层强化学习，在保持解质量的同时大幅提升训练和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法大多局限于欧几里得空间或简单图，而多图（并行边代表不同旅行选项）场景下已有方法存在严重的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出NEPF方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构用于边阶段；采用分层强化学习方法联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在6种VRP变体上的实验表明，NEPF在解质量上匹配或超越当前最优方法，同时在训练和推理速度上显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF有效解决了多图VRP场景下的可扩展性问题，实现了解质量与效率的双重优势。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [134] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了含事件的状态依赖微分代数方程（DAE）的可微参数优化问题，提出了两种梯度计算方法：一种是通过仿真自动微分的方法，另一种是显式离散伴随方法。两种方法均能处理隐式代数变量、参数依赖的事件时间以及重置映射带来的不连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含事件的状态依赖DAE广泛应用于受代数方程约束且存在模式切换、冲击或状态重置的系统中，但梯度计算面临代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖参数和重置映射引入不连续性三大挑战。

Method: 将学习问题建模为带DAE动力学、代数约束、守护方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题，提出两种梯度计算策略：(1) 通过仿真自动微分方法，利用隐函数定理对代数求解进行微分，并通过分段可微积分处理事件；(2) 显式离散伴随方法，将前向仿真表示为事件分割的残差系统，通过求解平滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 两种方法均能为前向仿真所选事件路径提供梯度，且在固定事件顺序和横向守护穿越条件下有效。明确了伴随方法中的残差项是等式约束而非启发式惩罚项。

Conclusion: 从梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂度和局部有效性四个维度对比了两种方法，为含事件DAE系统的可微参数学习提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [135] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN是一种分布鲁棒对抗训练框架，通过f-散度 ambiguity set动态重加权对抗样本，优化最坏情况下的对抗损失，显著降低大语言模型攻击成功率，同时保持模型效用和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs即使经过对齐和安全训练仍易受对抗性提示攻击，而传统对抗训练计算成本高、难以扩展。需要一种高效、可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法。

Method: 提出WARDEN框架：基于f-散度 ambiguity set动态重加权对抗样本，优化经验数据分布周围散度球内的最坏情况对抗损失。利用凸对偶形式将目标函数转化为KL散度下的log-sum-exp形式，通过动态参数控制重加权强度。

Result: 在多种LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低攻击成功率，计算和效用成本与CAT、CAPO、MixAT等基线方法相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN提供了一种实用且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，通过信息论目标函数有效平衡了安全性、模型效用和计算效率三者之间的关系。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [136] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了条件生成压缩感知中提示（prompt）的作用，证明了匹配提示的Christoffel采样可保持近最优的恢复界，而提示失配会产生兼容性惩罚；实验表明提示可重塑采样分布并影响图像恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索生成压缩感知中条件提示的作用，将提示区分为设计采样分布和定义恢复模型两种角色，并理论分析其影响。

Method: 针对ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器推导稳定恢复界；提出提示匹配的Christoffel采样；用Stable Diffusion进行实验验证。

Result: 提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持近最优的Christoffel复杂度常数；提示失配导致显式的兼容性惩罚；提示能有效重塑采样分布并影响恢复质量。

Conclusion: 提示应被视为具有感知、逼近和恢复三种不同效应的设计变量。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery.
