[2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [211] [Bandit Learning in General Open Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06202)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放系统中智能体可随时到达和离开的在线学习问题，提出了统一的开系统多臂赌博机框架，引入预训练度、稳定性和全局动态遗憾等新概念，并开发了具有可证明保证的全局UCB学习方法，证明了遗憾上界与下界的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开系统赌博机学习工作对智能体到达和离开模式施加了结构性假设，这些假设在实践中经常被违反。需要为一般开系统建立统一的学习范式，应对新智能体带来的内生非平稳性、信息积累速度受智能体模式影响、以及新智能体导致遗憾随时间范围扩大等挑战。

Method: 提出统一的开系统赌博机问题模型，包含异质奖励和一般智能体模式；引入预训练度量化新智能体进入时携带的信息量、稳定性衡量新智能体对系统的影响、全局动态遗憾比较所有活跃智能体的累积期望奖励与变化的最优臂；开发全局UCB学习方法并提供可证明的保证。

Result: 遗憾界表明进入不确定性通过预训练度线性进入遗憾；在稳定系统中，遗憾由识别持久最优臂所需时间以及智能体模式决定；通过困难实例的下界证明了这些依赖关系的紧致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般开系统在线学习建立了理论基础，揭示了预训练度、稳定性和智能体模式对学习性能的基本影响，为实际开系统应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital platforms have highlighted the prevalence of open systems, where agents can arrive and depart over time. While bandit learning in open systems has recently received initial attention, existing work imposes structural assumptions that are frequently violated in practice. A learning paradigm for general open systems creates fresh challenges: newly arriving agents induce endogenous non-stationarity; agent patterns determine how quickly information accumulates; and new agents make regret scale further with the time horizon. To this end, we formulate a unified open-system bandit problem with general dynamics, including heterogeneous rewards and general agent patterns. We introduce new concepts to capture the inherent complexities: the \emph{pre-training degree} of new agents quantifies how much information an agent carries upon entry, \emph{stability} measures the impact of new agents on the system, and \emph{global dynamic regret} compares the cumulative expected reward of all active agents with that of the varying optimal arms. We develop certified global-UCB learning methodologies with provable guarantees. Our regret bounds reveal that entry uncertainty enters linearly via the pre-training degree, while in stable regimes, regret is governed by the time needed to identify a persistent optimal arm, as well as by the agent patterns. We further show that these dependencies are tight via lower bounds in hard instances.

</details>


### [212] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在极限学习框架下研究对比式（contrastive）辨识与生成问题：学习者只能观测到无标签的、来自不同类别的样本对，而无法得知每个样本的具体标签。论文在无噪声场景下给出了可辨识类的精确几何刻画（对比封闭维度），建立了与正例学习、生成学习的严格层级关系；并发现有限对抗扰动下对比学习的鲁棒性反直觉地强于正例学习。统一技术工具是"公共交叉图"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的极限辨识与生成学习主要关注正例或全标签数据，但许多自然监督信号本质上是关系性的（如对比对），而非单点标签。作者希望填补这一空白，系统性地研究当学习器只能观测到"两个样本标签不同"这一关系信息时，能否在极限意义下完成辨识（识别目标假设）与生成（生成目标支持集中的新元素）。

Method: 论文采用组合形式化方法，在Gold[1967]的极限辨识模型和Kleinberg & Mullainathan[2024]的极限生成模型基础上，引入对比式呈现（contrastive presentation）：流式输入无序对{x,y}，满足h(x)≠h(y)，但学习器不知道哪个是正例。无噪声场景下：(1) 给出对比可辨识类的精确刻画（Angluin tell-tale条件的几何细化）；(2) 定义对比封闭维度（contrastive closure dimension），刻画一致对比生成所需样本复杂度；(3) 建立对比生成与正例辨识之间的不可比层级。有噪声场景下：证明存在在任意有限腐败预算下仍可被单个预算无关算法辨识的类，但仅一个腐败的正例就会导致其不可辨识。统一工具是"公共交叉图"，编码成对歧义、族级生成障碍和腐败缺陷。

Result: (1) 对比可辨识类具有精确的几何特征（Angluin条件的单行细化）；(2) 对比封闭维度精确刻画了一致对比生成的样本复杂度；(3) 对比生成与正例辨识在层级上不可比较；(4) 在有限对抗腐败下，对比辨识的鲁棒性显著强于正例辨识，存在反直觉的"韧性反转"。

Conclusion: 对比式监督信号虽然信息量看似更少（缺乏标签归属），但在极限学习框架下具有独特的辨识能力和更强的抗腐败鲁棒性。公共交叉图为理解对比学习在辨识、生成和噪声场景下的统一行为提供了新的组合工具。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [213] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆注入（Memory Inception, MI），一种无需训练的大模型控制方法，通过在选定层插入文本导出的KV bank来在潜在注意力空间中进行引导，相比提示词工程和激活控制方法，在人格引导、可更新引导和结构化推理任务上取得更优的控制-漂移权衡，同时大幅减少KV缓存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型控制方法存在两难：提示词控制力强但会缓存引导token到每一层，导致长交互中信息冗余；激活控制（如CAA）虽紧凑但控制力弱，且不支持大型结构化提示。因此需要一种兼具提示词控制力与激活控制紧凑性的方法。

Method: 记忆注入（MI）是一种无需训练的方法，在选定层插入从文本导出的键值（KV）bank，将引导视为选择性KV分配，仅在模型路由到的层注入潜在槽位，而非将提示内容物化到整个提示缓存中。

Result: 在人格引导任务上，MI在控制-漂移权衡上达到最优，与提示词竞争并持续优于CAA；在可更新引导任务中，支持对话中途行为切换且无需重写可见转录，在Qwen3上取得最高对齐度；在结构化推理任务中，MI在HARDMath和PHYSICS上10/12个subject×mode单元中优于可见提示词，同时将内容匹配的KV存储减少高达118倍。

Conclusion: MI是一种强大的引导方法，特别适用于引导是持久性、结构化或在可见转录中代价高昂的场景，为LLM控制提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [214] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于高斯平滑Bellman方程的软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），解决DPG在稀疏/离散奖励下因评论员动作梯度不可导导致的学习不稳定问题。实例化为Soft DDPG算法，在连续控制基准测试中保持竞争力，在离散奖励场景下显著优于标准DDPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DPG依赖评论员对动作的可微性，但稀疏或离散奖励导致Q函数非光滑，策略梯度定义不良、学习不稳定。

Method: 提出高斯平滑Bellman方程，定义新的动作价值函数，导出软确定性策略梯度（Soft-DPG），消除对评论员动作梯度的显式依赖。

Result: 在密集奖励设置下与DDPG竞争力相当，在离散奖励变体中DDPG对不规则评论员曲面敏感时，Soft DDPG获得明显提升。

Conclusion: Soft-DPG框架有效解决了非光滑Q函数下的策略梯度学习问题，为处理实际控制中的稀疏/离散奖励提供了原则性替代方案。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [215] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态推荐系统面临的新型逃避型推广攻击，首次发现跨模态梯度失配现象，并提出基于多模态协调的非目标对抗训练方法（UAT-MC）来增强系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统利用视觉和文本信号缓解数据稀疏性，但同时也更容易受到逃避型推广攻击。现有防御主要集中于单模态场景和投毒型威胁，对逃避型攻击的探索不足。

Method: 提出UAT-MC方法，将所有物品视为潜在目标来处理逃避型攻击中目标未知的挑战，并引入梯度对齐机制显式修正多模态间的梯度失配，确保跨模态扰动同步。

Result: 大量实验表明，UAT-MC在防御-准确性权衡下，显著提升了针对推广攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持了可接受的推荐性能。

Conclusion: 通过协调多模态梯度方向，UAT-MC有效解决了多用户推广场景下的跨模态梯度失配问题，为多模态推荐系统的对抗防御提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [216] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种校准诊断协议，结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层，用于区分图语言模型是学习结构规律还是记忆训练图。实验表明，大尺度下模型能够获取结构规律，而小尺度下性能与记忆无异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚合保真度指标无法区分图语言模型是否真正学习了结构规律，还是仅仅记忆了训练图，需要更精细的诊断方法。

Method: 开发了结合频繁子图挖掘、图级bootstrap基线和三级频率分层的校准诊断协议，在五个TU基准上对LLaMA式图语言模型进行分析。

Result: 小尺度下模型性能与bootstrap记忆基线难以区分；大尺度（375万图）下逐字记忆大幅下降而秩相关保持高位；高频模式复现良好，低频模式覆盖差；该现象在两种图序列化方式下均成立。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在足够大的尺度下能够超越单纯记忆，获取结构性规律，但这主要体现在高频模式上，低频模式仍是挑战。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [217] [Cumulative-Goodness Free-Riding in Forward-Forward Networks: Real, Repairable, but Not Accuracy-Dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06240)
*Amirhossein Yousefiramandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现前向-前向（FF）训练中存在的“层搭便车”现象：在累积 goodness 变体下，后续层因前层已部分分离任务而导致梯度指数级衰减。提出了三种局部修正方法（逐块、硬度门控、深度缩放），显著改善深层分离统计量（提升4-45倍），但对最终准确率影响不足1%。实验表明该病理虽可修复，但并非限制FF训练精度的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FF训练的累积goodness变体中，后层会继承前层已部分分离的任务，导致梯度信号随前层正裕度累积而指数衰减，产生“层搭便车”现象，需研究该病理及其影响。

Method: 形式化分析softplus FF准则下梯度衰减速率；提出三种局部修正方法：逐块（per-block）、硬度门控（hardness-gated）、深度缩放（depth-scaled），在不依赖反向传播梯度前提下恢复当前层分离度量。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100上，深层分离统计量提升4-45倍，非退化训练流程准确率变化不足1个百分点；Tiny ImageNet验证了相同定性差距；校准实验表明架构和数据增强对最终准确率影响更大。

Conclusion: 累积搭便车是真实存在且可修复的优化病理，但在所研究的FF训练规则、架构和数据集下，它并非限制可达到准确率的主导因素。

Abstract: Forward-Forward (FF) training allows each layer to learn from a local goodness criterion. In cumulative-goodness variants, however, later layers can inherit a task that earlier layers have already partially separated. We formalize this phenomenon as layer free-riding: under the softplus FF criterion, the class-discrimination gradient reaching block $d$ decays exponentially with the positive margin accumulated by preceding blocks. We then study three local remedies -- per-block, hardness-gated, and depth-scaled -- that recover current-layer separation measures without relying on backpropagated gradients. On CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, these remedies dramatically improve layer-separation statistics, with $4\times$--$45\times$ gains in deeper layers, while changing accuracy by less than one percentage point for non-degenerate training procedures. Tiny ImageNet provides a tougher cross-dataset check for our selected block-wise configuration and reveals the same qualitative gap between layer-health diagnostics and final accuracy. Calibration experiments further show that architecture and augmentation choices have a larger effect on final accuracy than the training-rule modifications studied here. Cumulative free-riding is therefore a real and repairable optimization pathology. Nonetheless, for the FF training rules, architectures, and datasets we study, it is not the dominant factor limiting achievable accuracy.

</details>


### [218] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图池化在图分类中效果不稳定的原因，发现池化算子需要节点特征与图拓扑结构良好对齐，提出了节点特征质量量化指标，并验证了满足条件时池化能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化在图分类中相较于标准WL-1表达力的GNN往往只能带来边际或不一致的提升，这一现象尚缺乏深入理解，本文旨在通过分析节点特征与图拓扑的交互来揭示其根源。

Method: 分析了池化算子对节点特征与图拓扑对齐程度的要求，形式化了有效池化所需的节点特征基本条件，并引入了特征质量的量化度量指标。

Result: 当节点特征满足与图拓扑对齐的条件时，图池化能够带来性能提升，并在合适的数据集上得到验证。

Conclusion: 节点特征与拓扑结构的对齐是池化生效的关键前提，仅有高表达力的GNN架构不足以保证池化的有效性。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图联邦学习中的异质性挑战，本文提出FedGMC方法，从流形统一视角处理语义和结构异质性，通过双流形校准机制在保持全局共性的同时最大化客户端个性化表示空间，实验证明其显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐模型参数或原型来处理异质性，这隐含地依赖全局线性假设，用一个全局一致的表示空间压缩了客户端的个性化表示空间，无法保留多样的局部图分布。

Method: 提出FedGMC方法，包含双流形校准机制：(1) 语义异质性方面，服务器通过等距语义锚构建几何最优的语义流形，指导局部语义流形校准；(2) 结构异质性方面，服务器通过全局结构模板构建全局结构流形，指导局部结构流形校准；(3) 服务器通过聚合局部流形动态优化全局语义流形和结构流形。

Result: 在11个同配性和异配性图上的大量实验表明，FedGMC有效平衡了全局共性与局部个性化，显著优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC从流形统一视角处理图联邦学习中的语义和结构异质性，通过双流形校准机制替代传统的刚性对齐，在保护隐私的同时更好地保留了客户端的个性化表示空间。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [220] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向端到端自动驾驶规划的分层归因框架，通过从六视角输入中由粗到细地定位关键区域，并提取三种归因统计量（熵、空间方差、基尼系数）作为规划风险的预测信号，实验表明这些信号与轨迹误差和碰撞检测显著相关且具有良好的泛化性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶模型虽然提升了系统集成度，但存在决策不透明和风险难以定位的问题。已有的辅助监控模型或文本解释方法与规划过程解耦，无法揭示轨迹生成背后的视觉证据。归因方法虽可提供直接解释，但规划任务与图像分类不同，需要同时捕捉关键视角和区域及其对连续多步轨迹输出的影响，且归因图能否支持风险识别尚未被探索。

Method: 提出分层归因框架：以原始轨迹的L2一致性为目标，采用由粗到细的区域归因策略，先在六视角全图上搜索候选区域，再在区域内精化归因；进一步提取三种归因统计量——归因熵（衡量注意力集中程度）、视角内空间方差（衡量归因在单视角内的分散程度）、视角间基尼系数（衡量六视角间归因分布的不均匀性）——作为规划风险的预测信号。

Result: 在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD上的实验表明，这些统计量与轨迹误差的Spearman相关系数为0.30±0.07，碰撞检测的AUROC为0.77±0.04；信号能够泛化到未见场景且性能下降可忽略，在替代归因基线下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 归因统计量能够有效作为规划风险的预测信号，为端到端自动驾驶模型提供可解释的风险识别能力，且具有良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning. Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [221] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FP-FM是一种通过函数投影实现流匹配的生成算法，它学习基函数来覆盖训练分布的 velocity fields，并通过最小二乘投影适应新分布，无需额外训练即可高效生成多样目标分布的样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在自然语言条件生成方面取得了成功，但如何从示例数据点实现模型适应仍是一个相对未被充分探索且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM)，学习基函数以张成对应训练分布的 velocity fields，通过最小二乘投影适应新分布；并引入多个变体（如使系数依赖时间）以平衡表达能力和计算成本。

Result: 在合成数据集和基于图像的数据集上，FP-FM在精确率和召回率方面均显著优于基线方法，尤其是在未见过的分布上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: FP-FM能够在不进行额外推理时训练的情况下，高效生成来自多样目标分布的样本，为基于示例的生成模型适应提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [222] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层感知交叉熵（HACE）损失函数，通过预测聚合和祖先标签平滑两个组件将类别层次结构直接融入损失计算中，在CIFAR-100、FGVC Aircraft和NABirds数据集上，端到端训练中18个架构-数据集组合中有15个提升准确率（平均+4.66%），在冻结DINOv2-Large特征的线性探测中全面超越所有对比方法（平均+2.18%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失平等对待所有错误分类，忽略了类别层次结构中蕴含的语义距离信息，无法利用类别间的层级关系来指导模型学习。

Method: HACE包含两个组件：1) 预测聚合：将模型的预测概率沿类别层次结构向上传播，使父节点累积子节点的置信度；2) 祖先标签平滑：将真实标签信号沿从真实类别到根节点的路径进行分配，实现层次化的标签平滑。

Result: 端到端训练中，HACE在18个架构-数据集组合中的15个上优于标准交叉熵，平均增益4.66%；线性探测中，HACE在所有三个数据集上优于所有竞争方法，比次优基线平均提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE作为一个可直接替换标准交叉熵的损失函数，能够有效利用类别层次结构提升分类性能，且具有架构无关性和良好的通用性。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [223] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一种两阶段框架，先通过理论指导的主动生成增强集成多样性，再对丰富后的集成进行剪枝，在保持对原始集成保真度控制的同时优于现有剪枝和压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型性能优异但需要大量弱学习器，导致部署困难、可解释性差及鲁棒性验证复杂；现有剪枝和压缩方法各有局限，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出PACE两阶段框架：阶段一通过理论驱动的过程主动生成新学习器以增强初始集成的多样性；阶段二对丰富后的集成进行剪枝。两阶段均可对原始集成的保真度进行精细控制。

Result: 实验结果表明PACE在性能上优于现有的剪枝和压缩方法，同时提供了有原则的保真度保证控制。

Conclusion: PACE通过交错集成剪枝与压缩范式，在保证保真度的前提下实现了更优的集成模型轻量化效果。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [224] [Attributions All the Way Down? The Metagame of Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06295)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Fabian Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"元游戏"（metagame）概念框架，通过将解释方法本身视为合作博弈并计算其Shapley值，量化模型解释的二阶交互效应，即特征j对特征i归因的定向影响（元归因）。理论上证明归因可分层分解为元归因，并建立其与现有交互指数的定向扩展关系。实验在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器及多模态扩散变压器三个场景验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型解释方法仅考虑一阶归因（单个特征对预测的贡献），缺乏对特征之间如何相互影响归因结果的二阶交互效应的量化框架。

Method: 将归因方法本身视为合作博弈，通过计算Shapley值得到特征j对特征i归因的定向影响（元归因），形成元游戏框架。

Result: 理论上证明了归因可分层分解为元归因，并建立了与现有交互指数的定向扩展关系。实验在指令微调语言模型、视觉-语言编码器和多模态扩散变压器上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 元游戏框架能够有效量化模型解释中的二阶交互效应，为理解特征间定向影响关系提供了新工具，在多种可解释性应用场景中展现了实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce the metagame, a conceptual framework for quantifying second-order interaction effects of model explanations. For any first-order attribution $φ(f)$ explaining a model $f$, we measure the directional influence of feature $j$ on the attribution of feature $i$, denoted as meta-attribution $\varphi_{j \to i}(f)$, by treating the attribution method itself as a cooperative game and computing its Shapley value. Theoretically, we prove that attributions hierarchically decompose into meta-attributions, and establish these as directional extensions of existing interaction indices. Empirically, we demonstrate that the metagame delivers insights across diverse interpretability applications: (i) quantifying token interactions in instruction-tuned language models, (ii) explaining cross-modal similarity in vision-language encoders, and (iii) interpreting text-to-image concepts in multimodal diffusion transformers.

</details>


### [225] [Perceive, Route and Modulate: Dynamic Pattern Recalibration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06310)
*Siru Zhong,Zhao Meng,Haohuan Fu,Haoyang Li,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态模式重校准（DPR）机制，通过轻量级"感知-路由-调制"流水线实现时间token级别的自适应重校准，解决了深度预测模型中使用固定权重矩阵无法适应局部时间动态变化的问题。作为骨干无关的适配器可增强多种架构，作为简约独立模型DPRNet在12个基准上取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列中的局部时间模式持续变化，全局共享的变换次优；现有深度预测模型无论规模与复杂度，均使用固定权重矩阵统一应用于所有时间token，导致模型陷入折中平均状态，无法适应变化的局部动态。

Method: 提出Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR)机制，采用"Perceive-Route-Modulate"（感知-路由-调制）三阶段轻量级流水线：首先感知输入特征，然后基于学习的自适应响应模式基计算软路由分布，最后生成时间感知调制向量，通过残差Hadamard积重校准隐藏状态。

Result: 作为骨干无关的适配器，DPR能够以最小开销增强多种不同架构的预测性能。作为简约的独立模型，DPRNet在12个基准数据集上取得竞争性表现，验证了动态重校准相对于宏观参数扩展的有效性。

Conclusion: DPR机制有效解决了深度预测模型中的局部时间动态适应瓶颈，且具有架构无关性，其轻量级特性使得在不显著增加计算负担的前提下即可实现显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Local temporal patterns in real-world time series continuously shift, rendering globally shared transformations suboptimal. Current deep forecasting models, despite their scale and complexity, rely on fixed weight matrices applied uniformly to all temporal tokens. This creates a static pattern response: models settle into a compromised average, unable to adapt to changing local dynamics. We introduce Dynamic Pattern Recalibration (DPR), a backbone-agnostic mechanism that resolves this via token-level recalibration. Through a lightweight "Perceive-Route-Modulate" pipeline, DPR computes a soft-routing distribution over a learned basis of adaptive response patterns, generating a time-aware modulation vector that recalibrates hidden states via a residual Hadamard product. As a backbone-agnostic adapter, DPR enhances forecasting across diverse architectures with minimal overhead, confirming it addresses a general bottleneck. As a minimalist standalone model, DPRNet achieves competitive performance across 12 benchmarks, validating dynamic recalibration against macroscopic parameter scaling.

</details>


### [226] [LINC: Decoupling Local Consequence Scoring from Hidden Matching in Constructive Neural Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06332)
*Shaofeng Qin,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LINC解码器架构，通过显式计算一步决策后果（旅行、等待、松弛、容量变化）并依据决策角色分别处理（局部比较器对比相对后果，可行集摘要调制解码器上下文），从而减少隐藏状态的学习负担，在CVRPTW、CVRP和TSP上显著提升了求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构造型神经路由求解器通常将下一步动作的评分仅通过解码器上下文与候选嵌入的全局匹配来完成，隐藏了诸如旅行、等待、松弛、容量变化等确定性的单步后果，导致隐藏状态需要重新发现过渡算术，增加了学习负担。

Method: 提出LINC（Local Inference via Normed Comparison）解码端候选决策架构：显式计算每一步决策的确定性后果，并按决策角色分别使用——中心的相对后果由共享的线性局部比较器进行对比，可行集摘要则调制解码器上下文，保留标准全局匹配机制。

Result: 在CVRPTW上，LINC将PolyNet在Solomon和Homberger基准上的间隙分别从13.83%/38.15%降低到7.26%/14.71%；在TSP和CVRP上，也改善了外部基准间隙。

Conclusion: LINC通过显式建模一步决策后果并按其角色分离处理，有效降低了隐藏状态的学习负担，显著提升了多种受约束路径规划问题的求解质量，且接口具有可扩展性。

Abstract: Constructive neural routing solvers usually score the next action by matching a decoder context to candidate embeddings, hiding deterministic one-step consequences such as travel, waiting, slack, and capacity changes. We propose LINC (Local Inference via Normed Comparison), a decoder-side candidate decision architecture that computes these consequences explicitly. LINC uses them according to their decision role: centered relative consequences are compared by a shared linear local scorer, while feasible-set summaries modulate the decoder context. This preserves standard global matching and relieves the hidden state from rediscovering transition arithmetic. The Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) serves as the main constrained-routing stress test; the same interface extends to the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) and Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). In particular, for CVRPTW, LINC reduces PolyNet's Solomon/Homberger gaps from 13.83\%/38.15\% to 7.26\%/14.71\%; for TSP and CVRP, it also improves external-benchmark gaps.

</details>


### [227] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化比较问题（患者比较三元组问题）从大语言模型中间接提取相关性和因果关联信息的方法，结合统计模型和不变因果预测，在COPD和MS两个临床领域验证了该方法能够获得稳定、可解释且支持因果推断的相关性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的训练数据包含大量生物医学文献，反映了不同患者群体的数据。研究者希望从LLM中恢复患者特征之间的相关性和因果关联信息，以支持医疗决策。直接询问LLM存在偏差和不可靠问题，因此需要一种间接的、结构化的方法。

Method: 提出基于患者比较三元组问题的结构化比较方法，结合LLM表征的统计模型（无需访问激活值或模型内部）。通过观察LLM基于第一个变量做相似性决策时，提供第二个变量信息对决策的影响来估计相关性。通过引入提示层面的环境偏移获得不同子群体的相关性估计，进而应用不变因果预测获得保守的候选父节点因果关系。

Result: 在COPD和MS两个临床领域，该方法获得的跨环境相关性估计平滑、稳定且具有临床可解释性，同时在统计上存在显著差异，支持下游不变性检验。ICP方法能够获得一小部分候选不变父节点关联。

Conclusion: 通过三元组比较的间接诱导方法能够从LLM中恢复有意义的关联结构，并提供了一条从隐式相关到与LLM回答模式一致的因果陈述的谨慎路径。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [228] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于约束优化和对偶理论的决策框架来建模模型级联（cascade）的成本-质量权衡，证明了两模型级联的成本-质量前沿是分段凹函数，k模型级联的前沿可由两两级联的点状包络刻画，并发现了轻量级预生成路由器优于最佳级联策略，主要是因为避免了廉价模型的生成成本而非更强的路由信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型级联方法将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对成本-质量前沿几何形状的理论指导，需要建立严格的决策理论框架来理解级联策略的优化边界。

Method: 采用约束优化和对偶理论构建决策框架，推导了两模型级联的分段凹性及对偶影子价格关系；对k模型级联，刻画了确定性两两阈值级联的点状包络和最优切换点；推导了k模型级联的一阶最优条件（影子价格均衡边际质量-成本比）。在5个基准、8个模型上进行了验证。

Result: 完整固定链（full fixed chains）性能低于两两级联包络；优化子序列级联无明显增益；轻量级预生成路由器在4/5数据集上超越最佳级联策略，原因在于它避免了将查询发给大模型时的小模型生成成本，而非更强的路由能力。

Conclusion: 级联性能的主要限制来自结构性成本（级联在决策前必须先支付小模型成本），而非中间阶段不足；轻量级路由器的优势主要源于规避了小模型的生成成本。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [229] [Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Modelling with Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06355)
*Ignacio Peis,Pablo M. Olmos,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了顺序无关自回归模型（OARMs）在缺失数据场景下的应用，提出了第一个可直接在不完整数据集上训练的原理框架，并利用其进行主动信息获取。实验表明提出的MO-ARM模型在多个真实基准上优于传统插补方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序无关自回归模型在深度生成建模中表现优异，但在不完整数据场景下的应用尚未被充分探索。现有方法缺乏在不完整数据集上直接训练OARMs的框架，也无法利用其特性进行主动信息获取。

Method: 1) 证明OARMs在完全观测数据上的标准训练隐式执行了完全随机缺失机制下的插补；2) 提出了第一个在一般缺失机制下直接从不完整数据训练OARMs的理论框架；3) 利用其摊销条件密度估计进行主动信息获取，顺序选择最具信息量的缺失变量用于下游任务。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上，MO-ARM模型一致优于已有的插补基线方法。

Conclusion: 顺序无关自回归模型可被重新解释为缺失数据视角下的生成模型，其标准训练隐式具备鲁棒的插补能力；提出的原理框架使OARMs能直接处理不完整数据，并结合主动学习策略进一步提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Order-Agnostic autoregressive models have demonstrated strong performance in deep generative modeling, yet their use in settings with incomplete data remains largely unexplored. In this work, we reinterpret them through the lens of missing data. First, we show that their standard training procedure on fully observed data implicitly performs imputation under a missing completely at random mechanism, resulting in robust out-of-sample imputation performance in settings with high missingness. Second, we introduce the first principled framework for training them directly on incomplete datasets under general missingness mechanisms. Third, we leverage their amortized conditional density estimation to perform active information acquisition, i.e., sequentially selecting the most informative missing variables for downstream prediction or inference. Across a suite of real-world benchmarks, our Missingness-Aware Order-Agnostic Autoregressive Model (MO-ARM) consistently outperforms established imputation baselines.

</details>


### [230] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对迭代随机净化防御的鲁棒评估问题，提出了一种基于梯度检查点（gradient checkpointing）的全梯度攻击框架。该框架通过减少内存消耗实现精确的端到端梯度计算，避免了因近似反向传播导致的鲁棒性高估问题，并设计了控制随机变异的评估协议。在扩散模型和Langevin采样（EBM）上的实验表明，全梯度攻击能发现近似梯度方法遗漏的漏洞，实现了更强的l_inf和l_2白盒攻击，并支持OOD鲁棒性探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代随机净化防御评估中，由于内存限制，攻击者常采用近似反向传播来计算梯度，这会削弱攻击信号并高估模型鲁棒性。同时，迭代净化中的随机性（不同净化轨迹）未得到有效控制，可能导致鲁棒性指标的显著波动。因此需要一种兼顾内存效率和梯度精确性的评估框架。

Method: 1）利用梯度检查点（gradient checkpointing）通过额外重计算换取更低内存消耗，实现长净化轨迹的精确端到端梯度计算；2）结合控制随机变异的评估协议，减少不同净化轨迹对鲁棒性度量的干扰；3）将该框架应用于扩散模型净化和Langevin采样（EBM）两种防御方法。

Result: 全梯度攻击发现了近似梯度攻击遗漏的漏洞，在l_inf和l_2白盒攻击设定下实现了更强的攻击效果（达到当前最优），并能用于探测OOD（out-of-distribution）鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确梯度评估对于迭代随机净化防御的可靠基准测试至关重要，近似梯度方法会显著高估防御鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [231] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文初步分析了Chronos基础模型处理与内部表示频域信息的能力，通过在线最小描述长度探针在可控条件下测试离散正弦信号，揭示了模型对频率信息的编码特性及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时间序列基础模型已被广泛采用，但其对基本信号属性（如频率信息）的内部编码程度尚不明确，缺乏系统性的表征分析。

Method: 以离散正弦信号为最简单的信号类别，在固定频率下生成数据；使用轻量级在线最小描述长度探针分析Chronos解码器架构的内部表示，检测频率信息的存在性与可分性。

Result: 揭示了Chronos内部表示中频率内容在不同频谱区域的捕获方式，识别了表示质量可能退化或需要特殊处理的频率区间。

Conclusion: 为Chronos用户在信号处理与信息融合场景中提供了实用指导，并推动了时间序列基础模型可解释性与评估方法的发展。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [232] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuxPath-FM框架，通过引入任意分布的辅助变量η到概率路径中，推广了条件流匹配方法，支持多种先验分布和标签引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件流匹配方法将辅助组件限制为高斯噪声，缺乏灵活性和通用性。AuxPath-FM旨在解除这一限制，允许辅助变量服从任意分布，从而设计更多样化的概率路径。

Method: 将辅助变量η引入轨迹形式X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η，其中η可服从任意分布（高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯、离散Rademacher等），并理论上证明该构造保持连续性方程和边际训练目标的一致性。

Result: 理论上证明了任意分布辅助变量下连续性方程和边际训练目标仍然成立；不同先验分布展现出独特的几何特性；支持标签引导生成等特殊任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM为概率路径设计提供了通用且理论完备的框架，兼具理论普适性和实践灵活性，适用于多样化生成建模任务。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [233] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于条件独立性的局部协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中同样有效（与无环模型一样完备），并通过σ-无环化不变性理论建立了统一的因果效应估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际观测数据中，因果效应估计常面临潜在混杂和反馈循环的挑战，现有方法通常假设无环性依赖全局因果结构学习，计算效率低且适用范围有限。本研究旨在探究局部数据驱动的协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中的有效性。

Method: 基于条件独立性的局部协变量选择方法，利用σ-无环化（σ-acyclification）下条件独立断言的不变性，将循环模型中的协变量选择问题归约到无环情形进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了该协变量选择方法在循环因果模型中同样保持可靠性和完备性，建立了统一的跨循环/无环设置的因果效应估计视角，并在大规模合成数据上验证了在循环因果模型中的稳定表现。

Conclusion: 基于条件独立性的局部协变量选择方法可无缝适用于循环因果模型，无需修改算法，实现了对无环和循环因果模型的统一处理，为实际中更复杂的因果推断场景提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [234] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），通过将非正优势区域的无效负强化替换为局部散度最小化，同时保留正强化学习，从而解决同策略蒸馏（OPD）中优势加权策略梯度存在的方差高、零优势区域梯度消失和探索瓶颈三大结构性问题。在数学推理基准上，AOPD在强/弱初始化下分别平均提升4.09/8.34，且保持更高的策略熵和更好的能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）在利用教师反馈训练学生模型方面表现优异，但其标准优势加权策略梯度存在三个结构性问题：高方差更新、零优势区域梯度消失以及校正信号不足时的探索瓶颈，限制了其性能和稳定性。

Method: 提出非对称同策略蒸馏（AOPD），在非正优势区域使用局部散度最小化替代无效的负强化，同时保留正区域的强化学习信号，形成非对称的优化策略。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，AOPD在强/弱初始化下分别平均提升4.09/8.34，训练过程中保持更高的策略熵，并在顺序工具使用适应中展现出更好的能力保持。

Conclusion: AOPD通过非对称处理正负优势区域，有效克服了标准OPD的结构性缺陷，在数学推理任务上取得显著且一致的性能提升，并改善了策略多样性和泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [235] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge是一种后训练半结构化稀疏框架，通过直接优化稀疏掩码（结合Hessian感知重要性估计和渐进退火软掩码）替代大规模稀疏重训练，在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏度下仅用5B重训练令牌实现57.27%零样本准确率，超越稠密模型（56.43%）并接近SOTA方法用40B令牌的结果（57.52%），显著提升了精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化稀疏虽能利用硬件加速LLM，但后训练剪枝因结构耦合导致严重质量下降，现有方法依赖大规模稀疏重训练来恢复精度，计算成本高昂。

Method: SparseForge结合Hessian感知重要性估计与渐进退火软掩码，直接优化稀疏掩码而非增加重训练令牌量，将软掩码渐进退火为硬件可执行的半结构化稀疏模式。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B的2:4稀疏度下，SparseForge仅用5B重训练令牌达到57.27%零样本准确率，超越稠密模型（56.43%），接近SOTA方法使用40B令牌的结果（57.52%），且跨模型家族一致有效。

Conclusion: SparseForge证明了直接优化稀疏掩码可以大幅降低后训练半结构化稀疏的恢复成本，在精度-效率权衡上取得显著改进，且具有良好的跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling. Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [236] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fisher-Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe)方法，通过预测分布空间中的Fisher-Rao测地线替代原始IG的输入空间直线路径，结合最大熵参考点和KL/欧几里得信任区域，提升归因可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Integrated Gradients (IG)方法因依赖启发式基线、直线路径、离散化和饱和问题而表现脆弱，亟需更鲁棒的归因方法。

Method: 在预测分布空间中定义最大熵参考点，沿Fisher-Rao测地线（概率单纯形上）构造轨迹；通过拉回Fisher度量实现输入空间轨迹，并用KL散度和欧几里得信任区域稳定；沿该轨迹积分输入梯度得到归因。

Result: 在六种ImageNet架构上，FRInGe在面向校准的归因指标（尤其是MAS分数）上提升最显著，同时在扰动AUC和不忠度指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: FRInGe通过将归因路径从输入空间转移到预测分布空间，有效缓解了IG的脆弱性问题，在校准类指标上表现突出。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [237] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，用于预测混合专家模型（MoE）是否会形成健康的专家生态或出现死亡专家问题。通过12组控制实验（8个视觉任务、4个语言任务），总计超过11,000个训练epoch，证明当E >= 0.5时即可保证零死亡专家，无需手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失。此外还发现6个额外现象，包括死亡专家可复活、正交毒性依赖于数据集等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在训练过程中常出现"死亡专家"问题（部分专家未被激活），传统解决方案依赖手工设计的负载均衡辅助损失函数，缺乏统一的诊断指标。作者希望通过构建一个综合超参数的单一控制参数来预测和调控MoE的专家生态健康度。

Method: 提出了无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)，其中T为路由温度，H为路由熵权重，O为oracle权重，B为平衡权重。在8个视觉任务和4个语言任务上进行了12组控制实验，总计超过11,000个训练epoch，验证E >= 0.5时零死亡专家的有效性。

Result: E >= 0.5足以保证零死亡专家；死亡专家可被重新激活；正交毒性具有数据集依赖性；任务复杂度改变关键E阈值；模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；专家生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持不变。

Conclusion: E可作为MoE训练的统-诊断指标，类似于流体动力学中的雷诺数。该参数消除了对手工负载均衡损失函数的依赖，为MoE训练提供了一种物理启发式的控制框架。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [238] [Federated Cross-Client Subgraph Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06433)
*Selin Ceydeli,Rui Wang,Kubilay Atasu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对分布式图场景下GNN子图模式检测存在表示等价性差距的问题，本文提出了逐层嵌入交换框架，通过每步前向传播同步中间节点表示，在扩展子图假设和共享参数条件下恢复与集中式GNN相同的表示，实验表明嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN子图模式检测方法假设图数据集中存储，当图分布在多个参与方时，客户端本地GNN计算会偏离集中式模型，导致表示等价性差距。

Method: 提出逐层嵌入交换框架：在前向传播的每一层，客户端同步交换中间节点表示，不暴露原始特征或标签。基于扩展子图假设和跨客户端共享模型参数，恢复与集中式GNN相同的节点表示。

Result: 在包含环、双团和散聚模式的合成有向多重图上的实验表明：嵌入交换与联邦参数聚合互补而非可互换，两者结合可恢复大部分表示差距，且要求交换的嵌入是每步新鲜而非每轮陈旧的。

Conclusion: 逐层嵌入交换框架能有效弥合分布式图场景下的表示等价性差距，联邦聚合与嵌入交换需协同使用。

Abstract: Subgraph pattern detection aims to uncover complex interaction structures in graphs. However, state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN)-based solutions assume centralized access to the entire graph. When graphs are instead distributed across multiple parties, client-local GNN computations diverge from those of a centralized model, resulting in a representation-equivalence gap. We formalize this as a structural observability problem, where subgraph patterns crossing partition boundaries become locally unidentifiable. To bridge this gap, we propose a per-step, layer-wise embedding exchange framework in which clients synchronize intermediate node representations at each layer of the forward pass, without exposing raw features or labels. Under an extended-subgraph assumption and shared model parameters across clients, this framework recovers the same node representations as a centralized GNN over the full graph. Experiments on synthetic directed multigraphs with cycles, bicliques, and scatter-gather patterns show that embedding exchange and federated parameter aggregation are complementary rather than interchangeable: their combination recovers most of the representation gap, provided exchanged embeddings are fresh per-step rather than stale per-epoch.

</details>


### [239] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedFrozen，一种两阶段联邦优化框架：先全模型热身训练，再冻结注意力机制中的query/key模块仅优化value模块。理论分析表明热身阶段可视为正则化核轮廓目标的不精确下降，冻结阶段简化为固定注意力核下的受限value块优化，并揭示了热身长度的显式权衡。实验验证了该方法在异构联邦学习中提升Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端异构性导致客户端漂移问题，现有方法多通过目标正则化或更新校正机制解决。近期研究表明Transformer架构在异构联邦训练中可能比传统模型更鲁棒，受此启发，作者探究注意力机制内不同参数组件如何影响联邦优化。

Method: 将注意力模块分解为query/key块（决定注意力核）和value块（在诱导核下进行语义变换），提出FedFrozen两阶段框架：第一阶段全模型热身训练，第二阶段冻结query/key块仅优化value块。在线性注意力公式下进行了理论分析。

Result: 仿真实验验证了预测的偏差-漂移行为，真实数据实验表明FedFrozen在异构联邦学习中提升了Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过对注意力机制参数组件的分解和针对性优化策略，FedFrozen有效缓解了异构联邦学习中的客户端漂移问题，且理论分析揭示了热身长度的显式权衡，为联邦学习中Transformer模型的训练提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [240] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMoE-C，一种基于场景自适应混合专家模型的跨域CSI人体活动识别方法，通过注意力路由机制仅激活相关专家模块，配合轻量训练协议，在保持高精度的同时显著降低推理成本，实现可扩展的跨域部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CSI的人体活动识别方法在跨物理环境（域偏移）下性能严重下降，而当前持续学习解决方案存在随累积域数量增加而扩展性差、依赖大容量回放缓冲区或推理成本线性增长等问题。

Method: 提出Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C)，将跨域CSI人体活动识别建模为混合专家系统，通过基于注意力的语义路由器为每个输入仅激活选定的专家模块，并设计仅需极小回放缓冲区的训练协议来稳定路由器的域判别能力。

Result: 在四场景CSI数据集上，SAMoE-C达到了接近最先进的准确率，同时显著降低了推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过结合模块化专家、选择性激活路由器和轻量级训练协议，SAMoE-C实现了低训练开销和高计算效率的可扩展跨域CSI人体活动识别部署。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [241] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部几何框架，将语义等价输入映射为潜空间中的结构化区域（不变子空间保留语义，扰動方向容纳释义变异），并开发对比性子空间发现方法分离语义保持与语义改变的变化，实验验证了不变结构在特定深度区域涌现、语义位移主要位于扰動子空间外、不变成分对模型输出的因果作用，以及不变表征可用于零样本模型溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对释义具有强鲁棒性，表明语义信息可能通过稳定内部表征编码，但这类不变性的结构与起源尚不清楚。

Method: (1) 对不变潜特征提出几何刻画；(2) 开发对比性子空间发现方法，分离语义保持与语义改变的变化；(3) 将不变表征应用于零样本模型溯源。

Result: 不变结构在特定深度区域涌现，语义位移主要位于扰動子空间外，表征层面干预表明不变成分对模型输出具有因果作用，不变表征可捕捉模型特定几何模式并实现准确溯源。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可视为潜表征的局部几何性质，为理解语言模型如何组织意义提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [242] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图粗化过程中结构多样性追踪的图级表示方法——多样性曲线，该表示具有可解释性、高效性和跨层次可比性，并通过实验证明了其在图聚类、几何区分、分子图比较和形状表征等任务中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级表示方法在处理不同基数的图（即使来自同一分布）时存在困难，且无监督任务需要可解释、可扩展且可靠的尺寸感知图表示。

Method: 通过追踪图在粗化层次上的结构多样性来生成图嵌入（多样性曲线），利用边收缩粗化并证明其能提升表达性，同时引入了一种新的等距不变量——图的分散度（spread）来编码度量多样性和几何结构。

Result: 在多个任务中优于基线方法：（i）聚类和可视化不同尺寸的模拟图，（ii）区分单细胞图的几何结构，（iii）比较分子图数据集的结构，（iv）表征几何形状。

Conclusion: 多样性曲线是一种可解释、高效且强大的图级表示方法，通过粗化过程中的结构多样性追踪，在无监督图分析任务中展现出优于传统结构描述符的性能。

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [243] [No Triangulation Without Representation: Generalization in Topological Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06467)
*Johannes S. Schmidt,Martin Carrasco,Ernst Röell,Guy Wolf,Nello Blaser,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MANTRA流形三角剖分基准数据集，发现GNN和HOMP方法在该基准上均可达到饱和，但依赖于正确的表示和特征分配。作者提出了基于表示多样性和三角剖分细化的新评估协议，结果表明现有模型无法泛化到数据的组合结构之外，指出了拓扑感知归纳偏置的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习模型日益增多，但缺乏统一的评估标准。拓扑对象允许图形数据不具备的操作（如结构细化），使得评估更加复杂。现有基准可能被GNN和HOMP方法饱和，但其泛化能力未知。

Method: 扩展MANTRA基准到更广的流形类别和更多样化的同胚类型；通过不同表示和特征分配测试GNN与HOMP方法的表现；提出基于表示多样性和三角剖分细化的新评估协议。

Result: GNN和HOMP方法均可饱和该基准，但依赖于正确的表示和特征分配；现有模型无法泛化到数据的组合结构之外；无证据表明模型能理解独立于尺度的拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 需开发能够理解拓扑结构、具备拓扑感知归纳偏置的模型。本文提供了评估未来模型的框架，并指出了拓扑深度学习的关键研究空白。

Abstract: Despite an ever-increasing interest in topological deep learning models that target higher-order datasets, there is no consensus on how to evaluate such models. This is exacerbated by the fact that topological objects permit operations, such as structural refinements, that are not appropriate for graph data. In this work, we extend MANTRA, a benchmark dataset containing manifold triangulations, to a larger class of manifolds with more diverse homeomorphism types. We show that, unlike prior claims, both graph neural networks (GNNs) and higher-order message passing (HOMP) methods can saturate the benchmark. However, we find that this is contingent on the right representation and feature assignment, emphasizing their importance in baseline models. We thus provide a novel evaluation protocol based on representational diversity and triangulation refinement. Surprisingly, we find no indication that existing models are capable of generalizing beyond the combinatorial structure of the data. This points towards a research gap in developing models that understand topological structure independent of scale. Our work thus provides the necessary scaffolding to evaluate future models and enable the development of topology-aware inductive biases.

</details>


### [244] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子理论的离线强化学习表示学习框架，通过击中时间观测恢复受控马尔可夫过程的定向时序几何结构。该框架学习希尔伯特空间中的位移几何，使期望击中时间实现为潜在位移的线性泛函。基于此理论，作者提出了同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，通过显式击中时间回归确保学习到的几何反映真实的决策时间进度，在离线迷宫导航任务中取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常产生对称距离或无法满足三角不等式，无法准确捕捉受控马尔可夫过程的定向时序几何结构，因此需要一种能恢复非对称且具有组合结构的表示学习框架，以支持长时域导航中的多阶段规划。

Method: 1) 提出算子理论的表示学习框架，在潜在线性封闭假设下证明表示的存在性和唯一可辨识性；2) 证明有限维实现中全局击中时间误差由一步转移误差与环境暂态谱半径的乘积界定；3) 给出包含近似、统计复杂度和轨迹标签不匹配的有限样本保证；4) 提出IEL算法，结合HILP风格一致性目标与显式击中时间回归。

Result: 在离线迷宫导航数据上，IEL在基础策略学习方面达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的非对称且具有组合结构的表示学习框架能够有效捕捉受控马尔可夫过程的定向时序几何，IEL算法作为目标无关的基础策略学习方法，在长时域规划任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [245] [Efficient Serving for Dynamic Agent Workflows with Prediction-based KV-Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06472)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Jia Wu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBKV是一种基于预测的KV-Cache管理系统，通过预测动态工作流中的智能体调用序列来优化缓存复用，在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-Cache管理方法要么在智能体级别管理，无法利用工作流内的复用机会；要么在工作流级别管理但假设工作流调用静态智能体序列。然而实际工作流是动态的，智能体调用顺序取决于任务上下文，导致缓存复用机会难以被有效利用。

Method: PBKV通过融合历史工作流的引导信息和目标工作流的上下文，预测未来若干步的智能体调用；基于预测结果评估缓存条目的复用潜力，将高潜力条目保留在GPU显存中；同时在缓存驱逐和预取阶段采用保守策略以应对预测误差。

Result: 在三个工作流基准测试上，PBKV在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。

Conclusion: PBKV通过预测驱动的KV-Cache管理有效应对了动态工作流中的缓存复用挑战，兼顾了预测的收益与误差鲁棒性，在动态和静态场景下均取得了性能提升。

Abstract: LLM-based workflows compose specialized agents to execute complex tasks, and these agents usually share substantial context, allowing KV-Cache reuse to save computation. Existing approaches either manage KV-Cache at agent level and fail to exploit the reuse opportunities within workflows, or manage cache at the workflow level but assume that each workflow calls a static sequence of agents. However, practical workflows are typically dynamic, where the sequence of invoked agents and thus induced cache reuse opportunities depend on the context of each task. To serve such dynamic workflows efficiently, we build a system dubbed PBKV (\textbf{P}rediction-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{KV}-Cache Management). For each workflow, PBKV predicts the agent invocations in several future steps by fusing the guidance from historical workflows and context of the target workflow. Based on the predictions, PBKV estimates the reuse potential of cache entries and keeps the high-potential entries in GPU memory. To be robust to prediction errors, PBKV utilizes the predictions conservatively during both cache eviction and prefetching. Experiments on three workflow benchmarks show that PBKV achieves up to $1.85\times$ speedup over LRU on dynamic workflows, and up to $1.26\times$ speedup over the SOTA baseline KVFlow on the static workflow.

</details>


### [246] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-MMR框架，通过数据点权重学习实现离线策略评估，在仅需Q^π可实现性条件下获得与函数类统计复杂度无关的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略评估（OPE）是离线强化学习的核心问题，现有方法（如重要性采样、FQE）在函数逼近下常面临高方差或对覆盖条件要求严苛的问题。本文旨在设计一种新的OPE框架，能在一般函数逼近下提供维度无关的有限样本保证。

Method: Q-MMR通过矩匹配目标函数学习每个数据点的标量权重，以重加权奖励来近似目标策略下的期望回报。权重以自上而下的归纳方式学习，使用值函数判别器类。理论分析建立了在Q^π可实现性条件下的有限样本保证，并揭示了与重要性采样、线性FQE等方法的内在联系。

Result: 建立了数据依赖的有限样本保证，误差边界与函数类的统计复杂度（如Rademacher复杂度、覆盖数等）无关；在仅需Q^π可实现性这一温和条件下即可成立；揭示了覆盖条件的新理论见解。

Conclusion: Q-MMR为离线策略评估提供了一种新的理论框架，放宽了现有方法对函数类和覆盖条件的要求，为离线RL中覆盖概念的理解提供了新视角。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [247] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VPSD-RL框架，通过李群作用与拉回算子发现连续时间/状态/动作强化学习中的精确与近似价值保持结构，并通过确定方程残差最小化学习无穷小生成元，实现数据效率与鲁棒性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间与状态/动作空间的强化学习通常数据密集且对扰动与分布偏移脆弱，现有方法多局限于预设对称性与精确等变性，缺乏发现更一般价值保持结构的通用手段。

Method: 将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，通过李群作用与拉回算子定义价值保持映射；证明价值保持结构等价于拉回价值函数与前推动作与控制生成元和奖励泛函的可交换性；通过HJB残差小量保证近似结构；通过拟合可微漂移、扩散、奖励模型，最小化确定方程残差学习无穷小生成元，ODE流指数映射获得有限变换，并通过转移增强与变换一致性正则化集成到连续RL中。

Result: 有界生成元/奖励残差意味着最优价值函数沿近似轨道的定量稳定性，其敏感度由有效视界控制；在连续控制基准上观察到数据效率与鲁棒性的改进。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL能自动发现精确与近似的价值保持结构，有效缓解连续RL中的数据效率低与鲁棒性差问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [248] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cubit架构，将Transformer中的注意力机制重新解释为Nadaraya-Watson回归，并进一步引入核岭回归（KRR）的闭式解作为替代，同时提出Limited-Range Rescale（LRR）提升训练稳定性。实验表明Cubit在长序列建模上优于传统Transformer，且优势随序列长度增加而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer被广泛采用，但其注意力机制在数学基础上仍存在局限性。作者从Nadaraya-Watson回归的角度重新审视注意力机制，并希望用数学上更严谨的核岭回归（KRR）替代，从而构建更强的序列建模架构。

Method: 1) 将注意力机制解释为Nadaraya-Watson回归；2) 提出Cubit架构，将注意力计算替换为KRR的闭式解，融合核相似度与核矩阵逆的归一化；3) 提出Limited-Range Rescale（LRR）对值层进行范围受限的缩放，以改善训练稳定性。

Result: 实验表明Cubit在长序列建模任务上表现优于Transformer，且性能增益随训练序列长度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: Cubit作为一种基于KRR的架构，相比基于Nadaraya-Watson回归的Transformer具有更强的数学基础，尤其在长序列建模方面展现出潜力。

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning.
