Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [249] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次大规模研究6种表格上下文学习模型的逐层机制，发现层间冗余和推理阶段动态，并用单层循环模型（仅20%参数）实现可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的表格基础模型（TFMs）在中小规模表格预测基准中表现优异，但其推理机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对6种最新表格上下文学习模型进行大规模逐层机制分析，研究预测如何随深度涌现，识别推理的不同阶段，揭示与语言模型不同的潜在空间动态；基于发现设计单层循环模型（仅使用原模型20%参数）。

Result: 发现多个模型存在显著的层间冗余，表明推理阶段存在重叠计算的迭代精炼过程；单层循环模型在使用20%参数的情况下实现了与原始模型可比的性能。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型存在大量层间冗余，推理过程本质上是迭代精炼而非逐层独立计算，因此可以大幅压缩层数而不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [250] [Agentic AIs Are the Missing Paradigm for Out-of-Distribution Generalization in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06522)
*Xin Wang,Haibo Chen,Wenxuan Liu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了基础模型面临的分布外(OOD)问题在结构上不同于传统OOD问题，并提出模型中心范式存在参数覆盖上界，而智能体系统（具备感知、策略选择、外部动作和闭环验证四大属性）是解决该问题的必要补充范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在开放世界部署中面临的知识边界、能力上限、组合性偏移和开放式任务变化等OOD现象，与以往OOD研究设定的场景有本质区别，且预训练和后训练分布通常只能部分观测，现有模型中心范式不足以应对这些结构性挑战。

Method: 通过四步论证：(1)提出阶段感知的OOD形式化框架，兼容部分观测的多阶段训练分布；(2)证明参数覆盖上界定理，指出存在模型中心方法（包括训练时和测试时）在ε容差内无法处理的输入；(3)刻画智能体OOD系统的四个结构属性——感知、策略选择、外部动作、闭环验证，并证明其严格扩展了可达集；(4)回应七个反论点，承认其中两个，并提出研究议程。

Result: 证明了参数覆盖上界的存在性，表明模型中心方法在处理某些实际相关输入时存在固有局限；智能体系统通过外部动作和闭环验证等机制能够突破这一上界；模型中心与智能体两种范式是互补关系。

Conclusion: 基础模型OOD是一个结构上独特的问题，单纯依赖模型中心范式无法解决，必须将智能体系统视为与模型中心范式互补的第一类研究方向，两者协同才能推动FM-OOD的实质性进展。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly deployed in open-world settings where distribution shift is the rule rather than the exception. The out-of-distribution (OOD) phenomena they face -- knowledge boundaries, capability ceilings, compositional shifts, and open-ended task variation -- differ in kind from the settings that have shaped prior OOD research, and are further complicated because the pretraining and post-training distributions of modern FMs are often only partially observed. Our position is that OOD for foundation models is a structurally distinct problem that cannot be solved within the prevailing model-centric paradigm, and that agentic systems constitute the missing paradigm required to address it. We defend this claim through four steps. First, we give a stage-aware formalization of OOD that accommodates partially observed multi-stage training distributions. Second, we prove a parameter coverage ceiling: there exist practically relevant inputs that no model-centric method (training-time or test-time) can handle within tolerance $\varepsilon$, for reasons intrinsic to parameter-based representation. Third, we characterize agentic OOD systems by four structural properties -- perception, strategy selection, external action, and closed-loop verification -- and show that they strictly extend the reachable set beyond the ceiling. Fourth, we respond to seven counterarguments, conceding two, and outline a research agenda. We do not claim that agentic methods subsume model-centric ones; we argue that the two are complementary, and that progress on FM-OOD requires explicit recognition of the agentic paradigm as a first-class research direction.

</details>


### [251] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR的推理能力主要集中于rank-1分量，并提出周期秩1替换法揭示RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象，同时刻画了RLVR的三个特性：rank-1分量仅保留数学推理能力、优化特定奇异谱（呈重尾分布）、左奇异向量在训练中表现出更强的对齐趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RLVR增强的推理能力主要集中于rank-1分量的观察，旨在深入揭示RLVR如何塑造模型参数，并探索其存在的隐式奖励过拟合现象。

Method: 采用Periodic Rank-1 Substitution方法，通过分析rank-1分量的替换效果来研究RLVR的行为特性，并结合奇异值分解分析线性层的奇异谱分布及左奇异向量的对齐趋势。

Result: 发现RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合（训练奖励低但测试性能好）；rank-1分量仅保留数学推理能力；RLVR模型线性层奇异值呈重尾分布；rank-1左奇异向量在训练中呈现更强对齐趋势。

Conclusion: 揭示了RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱和提升采样效率来塑造模型参数的本质，为改进现有RL范式或实现持续学习提供了潜在见解。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [252] [Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling: A Feynman-Kac Analysis of Bias and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06538)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Sebastien Motsch,Advait Parulekar,William Porteous,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可处理的替代路径和费曼-卡克公式，系统地表征了基于扩散的后验采样器（如DPS和STSL）的偏差，并解释了低温和离散化不稳定性问题，为设计稳定变体提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散后验采样器在实际中广泛用于逆问题，但其理论行为（偏差来源、低温不稳定性和离散化误差）尚不明确，亟需严格的理论分析框架。

Method: 引入一条连接真实后验到标准高斯的可处理替代路径，与采样器路径对比；建立密度比的抛物型偏微分方程，其反应项衡量累积偏差；利用费曼-卡克公式将Radon-Nikodym修正表示为显式路径期望。

Result: 1. DPS的修正项为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck路径期望，耦合数据条件协方差与奖励曲率；2. STSL通过辅助漂移将轨迹引向低不确定性区域，削减DPS反应项的空间变化部分；3. 早停策略可缓解前向欧拉积分在低温下的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架阐明了采样器偏差的解析来源，解释了已有修正策略的有效性，并为设计稳定的变体提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based posterior samplers use pretrained diffusion priors to sample from measurement- or reward-conditioned posteriors, and are widely used for inverse problems. Yet their theoretical behavior remains poorly understood: even with exact prior scores, their outputs are biased, and in low-temperature regimes their discretizations can become unstable. We characterize this bias by introducing a tractable surrogate path connecting the true posterior to a standard Gaussian and comparing it to the sampler's path. Their density ratio satisfies a parabolic PDE whose reaction term measures the accumulated bias. A Feynman-Kac representation then expresses the Radon-Nikodym correction as an explicit path expectation, identifying which posterior regions are over- or under-sampled.
  We apply this framework to DPS and STSL, a related sampler. For DPS, the correction is an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck path expectation coupling the data conditional covariance with the reward curvature, revealing where DPS over- or under-samples. Next, we reinterpret STSL as an auxiliary drift that steers trajectories toward low-uncertainty regions, flattening the spatially varying part of the DPS reaction term. Finally, we characterize early guidance-stopping, a common mitigation for low-temperature instabilities caused by forward-Euler integration of the vector field. Together, these results clarify sampler bias, explain existing correctives, and guide stable variant designs.

</details>


### [253] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MELO方法，通过记忆对冲的指数加权最小二乘在线聚合，在分布偏移下实现无需先验知识的自适应预测，在法国电力负荷预测中降低RMSE 34.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中面临分布偏移时，预测器需在平稳期保持稳定、在漂移期迅速适应，且最优适应记忆长度未知，现有方法难以平衡这两者。

Method: MELO方法：将任意非前瞻基预测器池与多个遗忘因子的EWLS自适应专家结合，再通过无参数在线聚合规则MLpol聚合原始预测和EWLS自适应预测。

Result: 在法国电力负荷预测中，相比仅用基预测器的MLpol，MELO将整体RMSE降低34.7%，且优于使用外部COVID政策协变量的TabICL基准方法。

Conclusion: MELO是一种轻量级、无需重训练的模型无关方法，能在分布偏移下自适应地竞争最优原始预测器及最优时变仿射组合，且具有理论保证。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [254] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种序贯框架，利用强化学习策略优化遗传电路，同时应对分子反应的固有随机性和跨实验室条件变异。通过预训练的摊销方法，避免了传统贝叶斯方法中昂贵的推理步骤，实现了基于观测的即时自适应。在异源基因表达和repressilator电路模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计受限于两种不确定性：生物分子反应的固有随机性以及跨实验条件的变异性。现有贝叶斯方法虽能处理不确定性，但每次实验后需进行计算密集的推理和优化步骤，导致延迟。因此需要一种能同时应对两种不确定性且计算高效的优化框架。

Method: 提出序贯优化框架，采用基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程的模拟器模型，结合强化学习策略方法。关键创新是采用摊销（amortized）方法：在可能的不确定参数分布上进行预先训练，从而在设计周期中无需显式参数推理，实现基于观测的即时自适应。

Result: 在异源基因表达模型和repressilator电路模型上验证，该方法能有效处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过预训练的摊销策略解决了传统贝叶斯方法的计算瓶颈，为遗传电路设计提供了一种高效处理双重不确定性的序贯优化方案。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [255] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY是一种无需额外训练的引导机制，通过散度自由动力学扰动漂移项，在保持边际分布不变的同时提升扩散模型和流匹配模型的生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型在生成高质量样本时往往缺乏多样性，容易出现样本坍缩或模式覆盖不足的问题。现有提升多样性的方法通常需要额外训练或牺牲质量。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程的对称性，通过无散度（散度为零）的漂移扰动改变粒子轨迹，同时保持边际分布不变。具体通过基于核函数的反对称成对矩阵场（从排斥方向构建）实现散度自由动力学，促进联合粒子层面的多样性。针对文本到图像等计算开销大的场景，提出实用近似方法。

Result: 在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务上，EDDY显著提升多样性，同时保持与常见基线相当或更优的分布保真度。

Conclusion: EDDY提供了一种无需训练、理论保证边际分布不变性的多样性提升方案，在保持生成质量的前提下有效促进样本多样性，并且可通过近似策略扩展到高维实际应用场景。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [256] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了树集成模型下满足可行性和可操作约束的最优反事实解释计算问题，提出了约束规划（CP）方法CPCF，并与MaxSAT和MILP范式进行了系统比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释的可信度依赖于推荐变更的极小性，次优解释可能给不同用户带来不公平的过高代价。现有方法缺乏对多种优化范式的系统比较，且针对树集成模型的组合优化特性尚未被充分利用。

Method: 提出CPCF，一种基于约束规划的方法，将数值特征编码为分裂阈值诱导的区间域，离散特征保留有限域表示，形成紧凑的有限域公式。同时扩展了MaxSAT方法以支持软投票集成，并与现有最优MILP方法进行对比。

Result: 在10个数据集和3种树集成类型上，CP在整体性能上最优。具体而言：CP最具通用性，MaxSAT在处理硬投票集成时表现突出，MILP在分裂层级适中的摊销推理场景中仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 不同数学规划范式在不同场景下各有优势，CP因其通用性和整体性能而被推荐为首选方法，但特定场景下MaxSAT和MILP仍具有不可替代的价值。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [257] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了模型复杂度（逻辑回归、随机森林、SVM）和特征选择（可变基因数量从50到20,518）对乳腺癌亚型分类的影响。结果表明，尽管所有模型均取得高准确率，但宏F1分数揭示了亚型层面的显著差异：逻辑回归表现最稳定均衡，随机森林对 minority 亚型性能较差，SVM对特征维度敏感。研究强调了模型简洁性、评估指标和特征选择在高维生物分类任务中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据具有高维小样本的特点，给机器学习模型带来挑战。准确分类乳腺癌亚型对诊断和治疗选择至关重要，但现有研究对模型复杂度、特征选择以及不同评估指标（尤其是宏F1）对亚型级分类性能的影响缺乏系统评估。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机三种模型，采用不同数量的高变异基因（50到20,518个）进行特征选择，使用分层5折交叉验证，以准确率和宏F1分数作为评估指标。

Result: 所有模型均达到高准确率，但宏F1分析显示亚型级性能存在显著差异。逻辑回归在亚型间表现最稳定均衡，能更好检测稀有类别；随机森林虽整体准确率高，但在少数亚型上表现不佳；SVM性能受特征维度影响明显。

Conclusion: 在高维生物分类任务中，模型简洁性、合适的评估指标（尤其是宏F1）以及特征选择至关重要。逻辑回归因其稳定均衡的亚型级性能，更适合乳腺癌亚型分类任务。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [258] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNAPO是一种将神经策略嵌入可微模拟器，通过平滑近似处理硬约束，并利用伴随方法在一次反向传播中同时计算策略梯度和所有输入敏感性的框架，在天然气储存、养老金管理和制药制造三个领域展示了高效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划在状态维度上呈指数级扩展，难以应对高维问题；黑盒强化学习训练缓慢且无法产生敏感性信息。需要一种既能处理高维状态又能高效计算灵敏度的框架。

Method: 将神经策略嵌入已知的可微模拟器中，用平滑近似替代硬约束，通过单次伴随反向传播计算目标函数相对于所有策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度。

Result: 天然气储存：不到1分钟训练，365条远期曲线敏感性零边际成本；养老金管理：敏感性计算速度提升6.5-200倍；制药制造：4单元工艺链的跨单元敏感性，20个ICH Q8法规敏感性在74.5毫秒内通过5次伴随传播完成。

Conclusion: 所有敏感性均由训练策略的同一反向传播产生，计算成本仅相当于一次反向传播，与敏感性数量无关，显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities.
  We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass.
  We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds).
  All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [259] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度学习优化中梯度方向对齐与损失收敛可以解耦，现有优化器缺乏利用梯度方向时序一致性的机制；为此提出GONO优化器，根据连续梯度余弦相似度自适应调整Adam的动量系数，在保持收敛率的同时实现振荡检测，并在多个基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏显式利用梯度方向时序一致性的机制，仅依赖基于幅度的信号，无法区分平台、鞍点和真正收敛。作者发现方向对齐与损失收敛可以解耦，即优化器可能表现出近乎完美的方向一致性而损失仍居高不下。

Method: 提出GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer)，基于连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t自适应调整Adam的动量系数beta_1：方向一致时放大动量，振荡时抑制动量。理论证明GONO保持Adam的O(1/sqrt(T))收敛率，且在无信息信号时退化为Adam。

Result: cc_t在振荡检测上达到F1=1.00（梯度范数仅0.45）；GONO在MNIST (98.15%)、CIFAR-10 (43.14%)和ResNet-18 (75.44%)上与AdamW保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一个理论上可证、实用上可行的优化信号，GONO通过自适应动量调整有效利用了该信号。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [260] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRL-Safety，一个多轴安全评估基准，用于评估图表示学习方法在五种安全轴（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）上的表现，涵盖12种代表性方法和25个数据集。研究发现：安全性受表征设计与压力因素交互影响，而非方法家族决定；基础模型时代的方法在特定轴上有优势而非全面领先；当前最优方法在多种部署场景下仍存在能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRL评估主要关注干净迁移、自适应和任务覆盖，但缺乏对部署压力（如图信号异常、图上下文变化、标签支持不足、结构群体偏差、预测证据异常）下方法可靠性的系统性评估。

Method: 构建多轴安全评估基准GRL-Safety，在标准化评估条件下对12种代表性方法（包括拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型和图基础模型）在25个数据集上进行评估，覆盖5个安全轴（腐败鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性），按轴和子条件分别报告而非单一聚合分数。

Result: 1) 安全行为由表征设计与压力因素的交互决定，而非方法家族；2) 基础模型时代的方法展现轴特定优势而非全面安全主导；3) 即使在最优评估方法下，多种部署场景仍存在能力差距，需要新的鲁棒性、自适应或训练目标。

Conclusion: GRL-Safety揭示了当前GRL方法在安全性方面的系统性不足，指出了超越模型选择的新研究方向，包括开发新的鲁棒性机制、自适应策略和训练目标。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [261] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布鲁棒的方法来学习凸优化中一阶方法的超参数（如步长），通过在性能估计问题的Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本上最小化，统一了经典学习优化（L2O）和最坏情况最优算法设计（PEP）两个极端。方法通过随机梯度下降求解，其中每一步需对内部半定规划进行微分。理论给出了算法真实风险的高概率界，实验在无约束二次极小化、LASSO和线性规划基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习优化（L2O）方法基于固定数据集学习算法参数，其性能在分布外可能显著下降；而最坏情况最优设计（PEP）虽然鲁棒但过于保守。作者希望弥合两者之间的鸿沟，在经验性能和最坏情况保证之间取得平衡。

Method: 考虑Wasserstein分布鲁棒的PEP框架，以分布鲁棒半径参数统一L2O（半径趋于0）和PEP（半径趋于无穷）。对给定数据集，最小化分布鲁棒版本的PEP目标。采用随机梯度下降求解，每一步需对内部半定规划问题进行微分（通过隐式微分或KKT条件）。理论分析给出了真实风险关于样本量的高概率上界。

Result: 在无约束二次极小化、LASSO和线性规划三个基准上，学习到的算法在分布外测试中表现出色，具有可证明的鲁棒性，同时优于最坏情况最优（PEP）和普通L2O基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布鲁棒L2O方法既能享受数据驱动学习的经验性能优势，又能提供类似最坏情况设计的鲁棒性保证，是连接数据驱动优化和经典鲁棒优化的一座桥梁。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [262] [Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06588)
*Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出理论框架，证明编码器几何特性（双Lipschitz性质）对神经图编辑距离（GED）估计质量至关重要，并通过FSW-GNN作为即插即用模块在多个基准上显著提升GED预测与排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经图匹配架构虽然在GED估计上取得进展，但编码器几何结构对GED估计质量的影响尚未被深入研究。

Method: 1) 建立理论框架，将编码器几何与两类GED估计器（图级回归和匹配对齐）的质量联系起来；2) 提出图级和节点级bi-Lipschitz编码条件；3) 引入FSW-GNN作为bi-Lipschitz且WL等价的即插即用编码器。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，几何感知变体显著优于原始基线，GED预测和排序指标均有明显提升；消融实验和迁移实验验证了改进源于编码器几何特性。

Conclusion: 编码器几何应作为神经图匹配中一个有用的设计原则。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [263] [BRICKS: Compositional Neural Markov Kernels for Zero-Shot Radiation-Matter Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06591)
*Richard Hildebrandt,Evangelos Kourlitis,Baran Hashemi,Manuel Bünstorf,Thierry Meyer,Nikola Boskov,Michael Kagan,Dan Rosenbaum,Sanmay Ganguly,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合离散-连续Transformer模型和黎曼流匹配的组合式神经替代方法，用于辐射-物质相互作用的下一粒子预测，实现了可微分、可组合的零样本模拟，并在GPU上获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机械模拟器（如蒙特卡洛方法）在辐射-物质相互作用模拟中计算成本高、不可微分，且难以集成到下游可微管线中。现有神经方法缺乏组合性和可扩展性，无法泛化到未见的大规模材料分布。

Method: 利用粒子相互作用的局域性和马尔可夫性质，构建"下一粒子预测"核；采用混合离散-连续Transformer模型，基于积流形上的黎曼流匹配（Riemannian Flow Matching）生成变长大小的类型化粒子集合及辐射副作用；通过组合多个核实现零样本模拟大规模材料分布。

Result: 单核执行在GPU上相比CPU上的机械模拟实现显著加速；在核级别评估中展现出多轮自回归 rollout 下的预测稳定性；发布了包含2000万事件的辐射-物质相互作用数据集。

Conclusion: 该组合式神经替代方法在保持可微性和可追踪似然的同时，实现了零样本组合模拟，为辐射-物质相互作用的高效、可扩展神经模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a new strategy for compositional neural surrogates for radiation-matter interactions, a key task spanning domains from particle physics through nuclear and space engineering to medical physics. Exploiting the locality and the Markov nature of particle interactions, we create a \emph{next-particle prediction} kernel using hybrid discrete-continuous transformer models based on Riemannian Flow Matching on product manifolds. The model generates variable-sized typed sets of particles and radiation side effects that are the result of the interaction of an incident particle with a material volume. The resulting kernel can be composed to simulate unseen large-scale material distributions in a zero-shot manner. Unlike mechanistic simulators, our model is designed to be differentiable, provides tractable likelihoods for future downstream applications. A significant computational speed-up on GPU compared to CPU-bound mechanistic simulation is observed for single-kernel execution. We evaluate the model at the kernel level and demonstrate predictive stability over multi-round autoregressive rollouts. We additionally release a novel 20M-event radiation-matter interaction dataset for further research.

</details>


### [264] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次严格证明了带权重衰减（L2正则化）的Transformer损失函数满足Villani强制性能量函数准则，推导了对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，揭示了正则化强度λ和模型维度d对收敛速度的影响，并通过GPT-Neo-125M实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减虽被广泛用作大语言模型的正则化器，但它在塑造Transformer损失景观方面的理论作用仍未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供严格的理论表征。

Method: 利用泛函分析证明正则化交叉熵损失满足Villani准则（无穷可微、二次增长、高斯可积尾部、微分增长条件），推导出显式的对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并引入可扩展的Villani诊断量Ψ_s，通过Hutchinson迹探针高效估计。

Result: 在GPT-Neo-125M上的实验验证了Ψ_s的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀以及指数收敛行为，与对数Sobolev分析一致。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅经验上改善泛化，还建立了快速Langevin混合和曲率感知优化的数学条件，为深层学习的理论收敛保证提供了基础。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [265] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAPRO，首个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于在多轮LLM交互中为事件发生时间构建可靠的预测下界。DAPRO满足预算约束，提供分布无关的有限样本覆盖保证，且覆盖界随平均删失权重的平方根而非最差权重缩放，理论上优于先前方法。实验表明其在任务成功、越狱攻击、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等场景下一致优于静态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话场景下LLM性能评估（如越狱、任务完成）计算成本高昂，关键事件可能罕见且难以观测；现有共形生存分析框架采用静态预算分配，效率低下。

Method: 提出DAPRO（Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization），一种动态预算分配框架，通过投影优化动态调整计算资源分配；证明其满足预算约束并提供分布无关的有限样本覆盖保证；提出新的覆盖界，随平均删失权重的平方根缩放。

Result: 在Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等模型上的agent任务成功、越狱攻击、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉实验中，DAPRO一致达到更接近名义水平的覆盖率，方差更低，且满足预算约束。

Conclusion: DAPRO是首个理论有效的动态预算分配框架，为多轮LLM交互中的事件时间预测提供了更高效、更紧致的保障，并可扩展到群体层面评估指标的无偏低方差估计。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [266] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了softmax注意力机制的Transformer如何在线性分类数据上实现上下文学习（ICL）。作者构建了一类多层Transformer，每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降，从而完成上下文逻辑回归。通过训练单层自注意力层（由一步梯度下降监督）并循环应用该层得到循环模型，可得到该构造。提供了自注意力层的训练收敛保证和循环模型的分布外泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer为何能通过上下文隐式执行算法以提升预测和生成能力，特别是softmax注意力机制下ICL的理论机制尚不清晰。

Method: 构建多层Transformer，每层执行一步归一化梯度下降实现上下文逻辑回归；通过一步梯度下降监督训练单层自注意力层，再循环应用得到循环模型。

Result: 证明了所构造的Transformer可通过训练单层自注意力（受一步梯度下降监督）并循环应用得到；给出了自注意力层的训练收敛保证和循环模型的分布外泛化保证。

Conclusion: 推进了ICL机制的理论理解，展示了softmax Transformer如何有效充当上下文学习器。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [267] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE提出了一种动态Top-K选择机制的可微分稀疏自编码器，能够根据输入复杂度自适应调整激活特征数量，相比固定稀疏度的TopK SAE更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TopK SAE使用固定的稀疏度K处理所有输入，但真实数据的局部固有维度随样本变化，固定K在简单输入上引入噪声，在复杂输入上丢失结构信息。

Method: 提出SoftSAE，使用可微分的Soft Top-K算子学习与输入相关的稀疏度k，使模型能根据每个输入的复杂度动态调整激活特征数量。

Result: SoftSAE不仅能发现有意义的特征，还能为每个概念选择正确的特征数量。

Conclusion: 动态稀疏度能更好地匹配数据结构，解释长度反映输入信息量，优于固定稀疏度的传统方法。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [268] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中注意力沉溺现象的机制根源：自注意力中的值聚合过程导致系统性的方差差异，而FFN层中超神经元的激活进一步放大了这种差异，最终迫使模型形成注意力沉溺作为结构锚点。通过受控实验验证了这一因果链，并提出head-wise RMSNorm作为解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力沉溺现象在LLM中普遍存在（初始token不成比例地占据注意力分数），但其结构起源尚不明确，本文旨在提供对这一现象的机制性解释。

Method: 1) 追溯注意力沉溺根源至自注意力中的值聚合过程导致的系统性方差差异；2) 证明FFN层中超神经元的激活会极大放大这种差异；3) 通过两种受控干预（注意力掩码修改和目标token表示方差放大）验证因果链；4) 提出head-wise RMSNorm架构修改以稳定值聚合输出。

Result: 两种受控干预均能在任意位置复制注意力沉溺现象；head-wise RMSNorm在预训练中恢复位置间的统计奇偶性，显著加速收敛。

Conclusion: 本研究为注意力沉溺现象提供了机制性理解，为系统控制沉溺形成奠定基础，并通过head-wise RMSNorm展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [269] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声模型，证明了SignSGD和Muon等符号优化算法在稀疏噪声场景下相比SGD可将复杂度降低d倍，并在GPT-2 124M参数预训练中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管符号优化算法（如SignSGD、Muon）在实践中表现出色，但在标准光滑性和有限方差条件下，SGD已被证明是极小极优的，因此无法从理论上解释符号方法为何能超越SGD。本文旨在突破这一理论障碍。

Method: 提出基于ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞光滑性和可分离噪声模型的分析框架，推导SignSGD的匹配上下界，并将其推广到矩阵域分析Muon优化器，最后通过GPT-2 124M参数预训练实验验证理论。

Result: SignSGD在稀疏噪声下相比SGD可将复杂度降低d倍；Muon优化器在矩阵域中同样保持与维度相关的最优缩放；理论预测与实际训练收敛速度一致。

Conclusion: 符号优化算法在特定问题几何（ℓ₁/ℓ∞框架、稀疏噪声）下具有理论优势，这解释了其在大模型训练中的实践成功，并为设计更高效的优化器提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [270] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二元分类和有界回归推广到无界回归场景，通过引入共形预测元素，并实验证明基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在较大训练集上能略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器虽然在有效性上具有优良性质，但仅适用于二元分类以及有界回归，这限制了其在更广泛回归问题中的应用。本文旨在克服这一局限，将其推广至无界回归场景。

Method: 通过引入共形预测（conformal prediction）元素，将Venn-Abers预测器从二元分类和有界回归扩展到无界回归场景。

Result: 模拟和实证研究表明，从Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在较大训练集上能够略微提升标准回归器的预测效率。

Conclusion: Venn-Abers预测器可成功推广至无界回归，且在此场景下能带来一定程度的预测效率改进。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [271] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了基准缺失下的语言模型安全对比评分问题，提出基于场景审计的实证链方法（SimpleAudit），并通过挪威语安全包和公共部门采购案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏标注基准的情况下，许多部署场景仍需比较候选语言模型的安全性（如特定语言、行业或监管体系）。现有方法无法提供有效的部署证据，亟需一种无需标签即可评估模型安全对比可靠性的形式化框架。

Method: 形式化定义了基准缺失下的安全对比评分契约（scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, rerun budget）；引入工具性效度链：安全与消融对照的响应性、目标驱动方差主导性、跨重跑稳定性；实现为SimpleAudit工具，通过挪威语安全包和Petri平台验证。

Result: 安全目标与消融目标AUROC在0.89-1.00之间，目标身份主导方差（η²≈0.52），严重性分布在10次重跑后稳定。框架可迁移至Petri。挪威公共部门采购案例显示，更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 安全评分不能简化为单一排名，必须同时报告分数、匹配差值、临界率、不确定性及其使用的审计员和评审员；关键差异产生于评分链上游的声明-契约执行和部署适配度。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [272] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在大型语言模型的监督微调（SFT）阶段，使用与预训练相同的优化器（优化器-模型一致性）能够实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡，比使用其他优化器甚至LoRA都能更少遗忘预训练知识。研究通过实验和理论分析解释了这一现象背后的正则化效应和权重更新结构，并比较了Muon和AdamW在不同场景下的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在LLM的监督微调阶段，不同优化器选择对模型在保持预训练知识（少遗忘）和学习新任务能力之间的权衡影响，特别关注使用与预训练相同优化器所带来的优势。

Method: 通过控制实验和理论分析，研究优化器对激活值的正则化效应如何塑造模型损失景观，以及权重更新应遵循的结构；同时通过合成语言建模实验比较Muon和AdamW在预训练和微调阶段的表现差异。

Result: 1) 优化器-模型一致性（使用与预训练相同的优化器进行全参数微调）在SFT阶段能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡；2) 优化器通过正则化效应影响激活值，不同优化器导致预训练检查点周围不同的损失景观；3) Muon在推理任务微调中表现较差，因其倾向于死记硬背（rote memorization），不利于小样本下的模式习得。

Conclusion: 在SFT阶段使用与预训练相同的优化器能有效降低知识遗忘，该发现对于LLM的微调实践具有指导意义；同时Muon的强死记硬背倾向在微调场景下可能成为劣势。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [273] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VHG框架，通过引入独立验证器构建三方自对弈机制，在问题生成中同时保障有效性和难度，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在生成有效、有挑战性且新颖的问题方面表现不佳，现有方法要么依赖昂贵的人工专家，要么采用朴素的自对弈范式导致奖励破解和无效问题。

Method: 提出VHG（验证器增强的难题生成框架），在传统设定者-求解者二元结构中加入独立验证器，使设定者的奖励由验证器评估的问题有效性和求解器评估的问题难度共同决定。具体实现了两种验证器变体：Hard符号验证器和Soft基于LLM的验证器，并在不定积分任务和通用数学推理任务上评估。

Result: VHG在所有基线方法上取得了显著的性能提升，实验结果表明该框架能有效生成有效且具有挑战性的问题。

Conclusion: 三方自对弈的验证器增强框架能有效约束问题生成过程，解决奖励破解问题，为LLM训练和自主科学研究提供高质量问题来源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [274] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPool提出将MoE中每层独立的专家集合替换为全局共享的专家池，通过池级辅助损失和NormRouter实现稳定训练。在多个模型规模上，UniPool在更少专家参数（41.6%-66.7%）下超越传统MoE，证明专家参数无需随深度线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构中每层拥有独立专家集，导致参数量随深度线性增长，且深层路由存在冗余（随机路由仅下降1.0-1.6个点）。本文旨在打破这种刚性分配，实现专家容量的全局高效利用。

Method: 将各层独立专家集替换为一个全局共享专家池，各层独立路由器从中选择专家；提出池级辅助损失平衡全局专家利用率；采用NormRouter实现稀疏且尺度稳定的路由。

Result: 在五个LLaMA架构规模（182M-978M参数）上，UniPool持续改善验证损失和困惑度，最高降低0.0386验证损失；使用41.6%-66.7%专家参数即可匹敌或超越传统MoE。

Conclusion: 专家参数无需随深度线性增长，共享池设计支持次线性增长；池大小成为显式的深度缩放超参数；UniPool可与细粒度专家分解进一步组合优化。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [Direct From Darwin: Deriving Advanced Optimizers From Evolutionary First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05284)
*Daniel Grimmer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文从进化第一性原理推导出梯度优化算法，通过引入达尔文谱系模拟（DLS）统一了Fisher和Wright的进化理论，证明SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法及Adam等算法只需添加DLS噪声即可成为符合达尔文进化论的模拟工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化算法多采用物理启发式或浅层生物学隐喻，放弃了进化保真度；同时进化计算领域长期存在Fisher确定性进化与Wright随机漂移的理论对立，需要统一框架。

Method: 提出达尔文谱系模拟（DLS），通过将Fisher确定性进化的总群体划分为Wright随机漂移的子群体，并引入DLS噪声关系（结构化噪声）实现形式等价。利用该表示自由度证明多种梯度优化算法与进化动力学兼容，通过添加DLS噪声使其成为进化模拟。

Result: 证明了Fisher和Wright的进化观在无性繁殖背景下形式等价；证明SGD、自然梯度下降、阻尼牛顿法等算法天然兼容进化动力学；Adam可通过微小的数学调整实现进化合规。

Conclusion: 梯度优化算法与达尔文进化论存在深层数学联系，通过合适的噪声注入（DLS噪声/遗传漂变）可将优化算法转化为科学有效的进化模拟工具，弥合了优化与进化模拟之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Evolutionary computation has long promised to deliver both high-performance optimization tools as well as rigorous scientific simulations of Darwinian evolution. However, modern algorithms frequently abandon evolutionary fidelity for physics-inspired heuristics or superficial biological metaphors. This paper derives a suite of advanced gradient-based optimization algorithms directly from evolutionary first principles. We introduce Darwinian Lineage Simulations (DLS) to prove that, in an asexual context, Fisher's and Wright's historically opposed views of evolution are actually formally equivalent. This unification requires carefully partitioning Fisher's deterministically-evolving total population into Wright's randomly-drifting sub-populations. We prove that proper bookkeeping requires introducing a specific kind of structured noise (the DLS noise relation). Crucially, however, any bookkeeping choices which satisfy this relation will result in a faithful simulation of evolution. Using this vast representational freedom, we prove that a broad family of battle-tested optimization algorithms are already perfectly compatible with evolutionary dynamics. These include: Stochastic Gradient Descent, Natural Gradient Descent, and the Damped Newton's method among many others. By simply adding DLS noise (i.e., evolutionarily faithful genetic drift), these algorithms become scientifically valid in silico simulations of Darwinian evolution. Finally, we demonstrate that even the state-of-the-art Adam optimizer can be brought into evolutionary compliance through a minor mathematical surgery.

</details>


### [276] [CoupleEvo: Evolving Heuristics for Coupled Optimization Problems Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06341)
*Thomas Bömer,Bastian Amberg,Max Disselnmeyer,Anne Meyer*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: CoupleEvo提出三种进化协调策略（顺序、迭代、集成），利用大语言模型自动设计启发式算法来解决耦合优化问题。实验表明，基于分解的策略（顺序和迭代）比集成策略具有更稳定的收敛性和更优的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的许多优化问题由多个紧密耦合的子问题组成，需要协调求解才能实现整体高性能，而现有的大语言模型驱动的自动化启发式设计方法仅局限于单问题场景。

Method: 提出CoupleEvo框架，包含三种进化协调策略：顺序策略（逐一进化子问题启发式）、迭代策略（在不同子问题的启发式进化间交替迭代）、集成策略（同时进化所有子问题的启发式）。

Result: 在两个代表性耦合优化问题上的实验表明，基于分解的策略（顺序和迭代）提供更稳定的收敛性和更高的解质量，而集成进化策略面临搜索复杂度增加和变异性大的问题。

Conclusion: 强调了跨相互依赖子问题协调进化搜索的重要性，并展示了LLM驱动的启发式设计在复杂耦合优化问题上的潜力。

Abstract: Many real-world optimization problems consist of multiple tightly coupled subproblems whose solutions must be coordinated to achieve high overall performance. However, existing large language model driven automated heuristic design approaches are limited to single-problem settings. In this paper, we propose CoupleEvo. CoupleEvo proposes three evolutionary coordination strategies to evolve heuristics for coupled optimization problems: the sequential strategy evolves heuristics for one subproblem after the other; the iterative strategy alternates the evolution of heuristics for different subproblems over successive generations; and the integrated strategy evolves heuristics for all problems simultaneously. The approach is evaluated on two representative coupled optimization problems. Experimental results show that decomposition-based strategies (sequential and iterative) provide more stable convergence and higher solution quality, while the integrated evolution strategy suffers from increased search complexity and variability. These findings highlight the importance of coordinating evolutionary search across interdependent subproblems and demonstrate the potential of LLM-driven heuristic design for complex coupled optimization problems. The code is available: https://github.com/tb-git-kit-research/CoupleEvo.

</details>
